 ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
I encourage everyone to follow the walkthrough as much as possible, the lists 
at the end are great, but you will probably find it a lot easier to collect 
everything if you're following along with the guide.  I cannot stress the 
importance enough of using the Heart Piece checklist at the bottom of the 
guide every time you get one, by the end of the game you can be sure you 
collected them all but find yourself missing one and having no idea which.  
If you've been checking them off as you go, you'll know exactly where to look.

+----------------------------- GAMECUBE CONTROLS ------------------- CN.GC --+

+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| D-Pad        | Show / Hide Map                                             |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| Analog       | Move                                                        |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Up         | Speak to Fairy                                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Down       | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Left       | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Right      | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| B Button     | Swing sword & cancel                                        |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| A Button     | Action button & confirm decisions & play Ocarina            |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| X Button     | Same as C-stick                                             |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| Y Button     | Same as C-stick                                             |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| R Button     | Hold shield                                                 |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| L Button     | L-target & look forward                                     |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| Start        | View subscreens                                             |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+

+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+

+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Down       | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Left       | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| C-Right      | Use items & play Ocarina notes                              |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| B Button     | Swing sword & cancel                                        |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+
| A Button     | Action button & confirm decisions & play Ocarina            |
+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+

+--------------+-------------------------------------------------------------+

| Start        | View subscreens                                             |

       __   __)
      (, ) /
        /(
     ) /  \_
+-- (_/      OKIRI FOREST ------------------------------------------ 01.01 --+

Please note that for every area of this walkthrough, there is an identical 
area below in the item checklist section of the guide where you can keep track 
of all the items you find in an area to help see any that you may have missed.

When the game begins, you are a young Link, lying in bed and greeted by a 
Fairy.  The instant Link wakes up you are able to move around and control him.  
Acquaint yourself with the controls and leave Link's house.  As you leave, the 
young elf girl Saria comes running and greets you.  Approach her and press the 
A button to speak, listen to what she has to say.  Note that all directions I 
will use, such as east, west, north, south etc will pertain to those 
directions on the map in the corner of your screen.  Head west of where you 
start and you will come across a ramp leading up to a higher level.  Go up the 
ramp to reach a kind of fenced in area.  What you want to do is navigate 
between the fences, on the other side there is a little crawlspace in the wall 
for Link to crawl into.  Simply approach the crawlspace and press the A button 
to enter it.  Hold up on the analog stick and Link will crawl along to the 
other side.  You can read the sign directly ahead of you, it will give you all 
sorts of helpful tips on how to control young Link.  What you need to be 
careful of here is a large boulder, it is rolling around this area and there's 
not much you can do to stop it, hiding in the little alcoves is about your 
only chance to avoid it.  Start running east and then make a right, then a 
left and then a right.  This will lead you to a large chest which contains the 
KOKIRI SWORD.  Open up the menu and select the sword from the equipment 
section, if it appears in Link's hand that that means you have equipped it 
correctly.  Now you can press the B button whenever you wish to swing the 
sword and attack your foes.  Press the A button after standing stationary for 
a moment and you can put the sword away.  Also if you hold the B button for a 
couple of seconds and release you can perform a spinning sword slash.

Return to the Kokiri Forest back through the crawlspace that you came in from 
now.  With the sword in your possession you can begin to gather Rupees, enough 
to purchase the shield.  You won't be let out of the village unless you have a 
sword and shield so you might as well start collecting.  Here are the best 
ways to accumulate the 40 Rupees that you will need.  First of all, in the 
area where you got the sword you should have found a couple of blue Rupees, 
these are worth five Rupees apiece.  Slashing plants will often new you maybe 
one Rupee or so, but there are better ways.  Head back past the fenced area 
and run down the sloping path again, into the house at the bottom, the one 
beside that circle of rocks.  There are four treasure chests in this room, 
open them all to acquire a BLUE RUPEE, a RECOVERY HEART, a BLUE RUPEE, and a 
pathetic GREEN RUPEE.  Now you'll be at approximately 25 Rupees, we're getting 
there.  Directly east of this house you will notice a few square shaped 
platforms sticking up out of the water.  Run toward these platforms, as you 
come to the edge Link will automatically jump in the direction you are moving 
assuming you are running.  Jump across all three of these platforms on the 
water to get a Blue Rupee.  Head back to Link's house, east of it is a 
building with a ramp that you can go up to reach the top of the house.  It 
leads to a bridge, cross this bridge and the other bridge to reach a raised 
platform with a girl and a Blue Rupee on it.  At this point you had might as 
well just run around the village slashing all the shrubs and gradually 
collecting Rupees until you have acquired 40 of them.  Once you do have 40 
Rupees, then your destination will be the shop.  It has kind of an orange roof 
and is located on the northeast side where all of the water is.

Inside the shop, speak with the shopkeeper.  Use the analog stick to move left 
and right and make your selections.  You'll find the Deku Shield located on 
the lower shelf on the right side, select it and purchase it if you have 40 
Rupees.  You just bought yourself a DEKU SHIELD!  Be sure to equip it in the 
menu, after that you can use it with the R button.  If you press and hold the 
L/Z button while defending with the shield, then you can move around while 
using it.  Now make sure that both your sword and shield are equipped and make 
your way east.  Speak with the little elf guy blocking the path and he will 
allow you to pass if you are properly equipped.  Pass by Mido and begin your 
trek down the path.  As you do suddenly something happens, some evil plants 
pop out of the ground.  If you hold the L/Z button you can approach the enemy 
carefully while you target it and swing your sword, it should go down with a 
single strike.  You will most likely acquire a Deku Stick after defeating this 
enemy.  A Deku Stick has many uses, it can be used as a weapon instead of your 
sword, but that's really not what it's for, it's likely to beak that way.  
Just keep it in your inventory and you'll use it later.  Proceed to defeat the 
other plant enemies before you go any further.  Approach the great Deku Tree 
and listen to what it has to say.  The great Deku Tree opens its mouth wide to 
reveal and entrance.  It's now time to go inside the tree and break the curse.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EKU TREE -------------------------------------------- 01.02 --+

The plants inside this dungeon are a little nastier than the ones you have 
already fought.  Instead of sitting there and taking the punishment, these 
ones are going to lunge at you.  Fortunately they cannot go any further than 
their stems will let them.  Keep your distance and attack with your sword.  
These enemies will drop Deku Nuts, another new item which you can use to stun 
your enemies.  Note that at any point when you hear Navi call "Hey!" to you, 
you can listen to her by pressing C-up.  Sometimes if she has something really 
important to say, she'll just say it anyway without having you press a button.  
Note there is a ladder on the wall here to the left of you when you come in 
from the main entrance.  Across at the far end there is also a vine growing on 
the wall.  Both can be climbed to bring you up to the ledge above, take either 
one it doesn't matter.  If you went up the ladder then you will have to run 
around and jump across to the top of the vines.  From there proceed along the 
path as it gradually slopes up.  At the top of the path you will come across a 
large chest.  Open this chest to acquire the DUNGEON MAP.  Most if not all 
dungeons have a Dungeon Map which fills up the map in your menu to show the 
entire dungeon and hopefully give you a better idea of where to go.  

Since you cannot get up the vine wall beside the chest yet, that means you had 
might as well continue along the path you are currently on.  Soon enough you 
will come to a door, Navi gives you a little tutorial on how to open doors, 
it's really about as easy as it sounds.  Opening a door isn't particularly 
difficult.  Walk up to the door and press A to go through it.  When you do you 
will see something in the middle of the room.  It keeps popping out of the 
ground over and over again, firing Deku Nuts at you.  When you try to go close 
to it and attack with your sword it just pops back down into the ground again.  
The solution?  Stand a little ways back, far enough so that it isn't afraid to 
pop up, and hold the R button to bring up your shield.  Deflect the nut back 
at the enemy, that is how you defeat him.  The easiest way to aim it of course 
is to hold the L/Z button to target him, and then defend with your shield.  
After doing this then chase the little guy down, when you corner him he will 
speak to you.  Grab the Recovery Heart that the Deku Scrub drops and proceed 
through the door that just opened, not the one you came through.

There is a floating platform in this room, you can try to jump to it and jump 
across.  Once you land on it the platform will break, it doesn't matter if you 
fall though, simply climb up the vines on the other side.  They lead up to a 
large treasure chest, open it to find out what's inside.  The item inside 
turns out to be the FAIRY SLINGSHOT.  The Fairy Slingshot fires Deku Seeds, 
you are given 30 of them automatically to start with and you can find many 
more just by slashing grass and stuff.  Equip the Fairy Slingshot to one of 
the C buttons.  To pull back, hold that C button and then let go of it when 
you're ready to fire.  You can aim while holding the button.  Now you may be 
wondering how to get back across to the door since there aren't any vines to 
climb,  The solution is simple, now that you have the Fairy Slingshot, use it 
and take aim at the ladder above the door you came in from.  When you hit the 
ladder it falls down.  Before going back to the door note the vine beside this 
chest that you can climb up, it leads to a small hidden room with a treasure 
chest containing RECOVERY HEART.  After getting it then you can leave.

Return to the main room, the large inner trunk area and go back to the chest 
where you got the Dungeon Map, the chest beside those vines on the wall.  
Before you couldn't climb up the vines because those damn Skulltula things 
would knock you off.  Now you can safely stand below them on the ground and 
fire upward using your Fairy Slingshot.  You can aim manually if you want, but 
it's even easier to aim by targeting the enemy using the L/Z button and then 
simply tapping the C button to fire directly at it.  It should only take a 
single hit to drop these enemies, though you may have to aim manually to hit 
the third one higher up.  Once they have been dispatched then you can safely 
begin to climb this wall.  When you reach the top of the vines make a right 
and drop down onto the walkway.  Directly in front of you is a larger 
Skulltula.  These ones are a bit more difficult to defeat.  What you have to 
do is wait for them to drop down.  After a moment they will spin around and 
reveal their back, this is your chance to strike.  After striking the 
Skulltula will go up in the air again, wait for it to descend and do the same 
thing.  The second hit will kill the enemy.  Continue along this path past 
where you fought the enemy and go through the first door you come to.

When you enter the room take a look to your right, there is a large button on 
the floor.  When you step on this button three platforms rise up out of the 
ground in the middle of the room.  They won't stay up forever it is only 
temporary so you have to move quickly.  Jump to the first platform, then the 
one on your left, then the one on your right and finally from that one to the 
ledge.  There is a large treasure chest here and inside it you will find the 
COMPASS for the dungeon.  The Compass takes your map and places an indicator 
on it which shows your exact position and the direction you are facing, very 
helpful and will make it a lot easier for me to refer to directions like east 
and west.  Return to the entrance of the room and press the button again.  
This time jump from the first platform, to the second platform and then jump 
south to the other ledge.  Open the small chest here to get a RECOVERY HEART 
from it.  Notice the Skulltula on the wall, it is different from any other you 
have faced so far, it's more... golden than the rest.  This is a special 
Skulltula called a Gold Skulltula.  To defeat it all you have to do is swing 
your sword, it doesn't have a protective faceplate like the other.  When the 
enemy has been defeated it will drop an item.  This is GOLD SKULLTULA [001] 
token that proves you killed it.  The reason I list the number beside it is so 
you can use the checklist at the bottom of this guide and note that you have 
number 001 already, it will really help out later to know which ones you have 
and which ones you don't.  There are exactly 100 Gold Skulltula throughout the 
game and something good will happen if you kill them all, so we'll work toward 
that as we progress along with the walkthrough.  Now head back to the door.

You will notice the door is still locked and you cannot leave.  What you are 
going to need here is a Deku Stick, if you don’t have one then drop down and 
kill the plant enemy, it is sure to drop one.  Set the Deku Stick to one of 
your C buttons and whip it out so that you are holding it.  Walk up to the 
torch which is on fire and it will light the Deku Stick.  Now run quickly over 
to the other torch and light it using the stick, you don't have to swing the 
stick, just approach the torch.  After it is lit, you can pick the stick away 
before it burns out entirely by pressing the B button.  Now that the door is 
no longer locked, leave this room.  On your left there is another large 
Skulltula to kill.  Approach it and eliminate it.  Now what you have to do is 
jump down all the way to the very bottom, remember that spider web in the 
middle of the room when you came in?  Well it's time to break that web, and 
you break it by falling from such a huge distance down on top of it right in 
the middle.  If you land on the side of the web you'll be fine but it won't 
break.  I find that if you run to the edge, jump and let go of the analog 
stick immediately after jumping, then usually that's just about perfect.

There should be a low ledge there beside you when you land, grab hold of the 
edge and climb up.  Defeat the plant monster here that is in your way.  The 
first thing you are going to want to do is take out your Fairy Slingshot and 
aim up at the Gold Skulltula on the vine beside you.  Fire two shots at it to 
kill the monster and then climb up the vine to grab the GOLD SKULLTULA [002].  
Now get in the water and find an area, right next to the large button on the 
higher ledge, that you can stand on while in the water, the platform is only 
slightly submerged underwater.  While standing on it look over toward the 
water gate and note the Gold Skulltula crawling around on it.  Take out your 
handy dandy Fairy Slingshot and fire two shots at the enemy to kill it.  Now 
jump from the ledge with the big button toward the water gate and grab the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [003] in mid air.  Climb back up onto the platform with the big 
button and step on the big button.  This will ignite the torch behind you.  
Open the chest beside the torch to get a RECOVERY HEART and then whip out one 
of your many Deku Sticks.  Set the stick on fire and jump into the water, but 
not the deep water, jump onto the raised area which is only slightly 
submerged.  From there you can jump to the lower ledge without ever having to 
actually swim.  When you start swimming the Deku Stick obviously gets 
extinguished.  Once you reach this lower platform with the burning Deku Stick, 
run up to the web and the fire will burn it away, now go through the door.

Once again there is a damn little Deku Scrub in this room.  What you have to 
do of course is target the enemy with the L/Z button and then hold the R 
button to use your shield and deflect the Deku Nut back at him.  The scrub 
tells you that you cannot defeat the brothers unless you punish them in the 
right order.  The correct order is 2, 3, 1.  Keep that in mind or just look 
back at this sentence I guess.  The scrub will drop a Recovery Heart for you 
to pick up but you still can't get out of this room.  Here's the trick, equip 
your Fairy Slingshot and aim up at the eyeball above the door.  Fire away at 
the eyeball and the bars will disappear, the door will be unlocked.  

In this next room there is a rolling spike log thing that you are going to 
have to avoid somehow.  Navi gives you a tip right at the beginning, you can 
dive underwater while swimming if you press the A button.  Jump into the water 
and dive under, take a look along the south wall to find an underwater button 
and when you swim up to it, the button is automatically pressed.  Now with the 
button pressed the water level in this room drops and the moving platform is 
lower than the rolling spike log.  Return to the entrance and jump down onto 
the platform, you will be able to move safely underneath it and jump to the 
ledge on the other side.  There is a Skulltula here, not a gold one, I'm sure 
you remember how to kill it, simply strike when the enemy's back is turned.  
Notice the kind of depression in the ground and a block on the side.  If you 
run up to this block and press the A button you can grab hold of it from any 
side if you are standing stationary.  If you are moving toward the block when 
you press A beside it, then you will climb up on top, like Navi says just keep 
an eye on the green action button at the top of the screen, it tells you what 
it will do.  Push the block along this depression until it can't move any 
further and then climb on top.  Climb from there up to the door and proceed 
through it.  The doors in this room are locked and there are some dangerous 
plants growing about.  Don't bother killing them all, they just respawn.  Your 
goal is to equip a Deku Stick and light it on fire, then use it to light all 
of the torches in this room.  This will unlock both doors, head north now.

Immediately as you enter this room a Skulltula drops down in front of you, be 
ready to strike and defend yourself.  Upon reaching the centre of this room, 
three new enemies drop down.  These will be the first enemies you have fought 
which can actually move freely.  Their weak point is the eyes on the front, I 
find it is easiest to simply fight these enemies without using the targeting 
system, it will allow you to move about easier.  Once the three of them have 
been defeated then equip another Deku Stick and light it on fire.  Use the 
fiery Deku Stick to burn the spider web on the east side.  It will reveal a 
path to a crawlspace, approach the crawlspace and enter it using the A button.  
When you pop out you'll notice that you have actually been here before, it's 
the large room with all the water but you're up on top of a platform that you 
could not reach before.  The first thing you are going to want to do is locate 
the box that you can push along the depression in the ground.  Push it over 
the edge down into the water, this way you can always get back up here quickly 
instead of going the long way around like you just did.  Jump down from here 
to the area with the large button.  Light another Deku Stick and then jump 
from this platform directly onto the box you just pushed and then from that 
box up onto the platform.  With your Deku Stick on fire, stand on top of the 
spider web and press the C button that corresponds to the Deku Stick to swing 
it and set the web on fire.  Get ready for a big free fall down a level.

There are a number of hearts here in the water so you should be okay in terms 
of health.  This is exactly what the Deku Scrub was talking about, there are 
three of them here and you have to catch them in the correct order.  That 
order was 2, 3, 1, from left to right.  So basically start by deflecting the 
Deku Nut back at the scrub in the middle, then the one on the far right and 
then the one on the far left.  Catch him and he'll speak with you.  The scrub 
now reveals the secret of Queen Gohma, which as you probably guessed, is the 
boss.  You have to strike while she is stunned, doesn't sound too complicated.  
Grab the Recovery Heart that the scrub leaves behind and enter the next room.

Prepare yourself for the first boss battle, the battle against Queen Gohma, 
the enemy that the Deku Scrub was talking about.  When the door slams shut, 
equip your Fairy Slingshot and aim it up at the ceiling, once you make eye 
contact the battle against Parasitic Armored Arachnid, Gohma will begin.  At 
the very beginning of the battle, Gohma will pretty much be wandering around 
doing her own this, this is your chance to attack.  Hold the L/Z button to 
target her eye and tap the Fairy Slingshot button to fire a Deku Seed right 
into it, stunning the boss.  While the boss is stunned, run up to her and 
begin to slash her with your sword as many times as you possibly can.  After 
this the boss will begin climbing up the wall and shimmy onto the roof.  After 
a few seconds she will drop three eggs down directly below where she is on the 
ceiling.  You now have a small amount of time to go and strike those eggs, one 
single hit will kill them however if you are not fast enough they will hatch 
into those little one eyed enemies you fought before.  Wait another few 
seconds and Gohma will drop down again, and the pattern begins anew.  Target 
Gohma's eye and let her get close, while she is up on her back legs and the 
eye is red that is the best time to fire.  It should take no more than three 
or four sessions of stunning the boss and attacking with your sword.

When the battle is over there is a full HEART CONTAINER [001].  Your hearts 
have now been increased to four, that's pretty nice.  Step onto the blue 
portal and it will automatically warp you outside of the Deku Tree.  Listen to 
everything the Deku Tree has to say because it's all very important.  After 
the tree speaks it gives you the KOKIRI'S EMERALD.  Return to Kokiri Forest 
but there really isn't much to do there at all, it's time to depart for Hyrule 
Field.  Simply run all the way west to the exit of the forest, a scene will 
take place between Link and Saria.  She will give Link the FAIRY OCARINA.  
_____________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE FIELD ------------------------------------------ 01.03 --+

When you go to leave you will be interrupted by a nosy owl.  He seems to know 
a lot about what you're going to do.  Listen to what the owl says to get a 
basic handle on what's to come.  After listening to the owl do the following: 
turn around 180 degrees and head back toward the entrance to the Kokiri 
Forest.  Stand outside the entrance and do absolutely nothing.  Notice the 
light level is changing, wait until it gets completely dark and turns to 
night.  You will know when this happens when you hear a howling sounds and 
suddenly zombies appear out of the ground.  Quickly run into the Kokiri Forest 
at this point.  Your destination is the southwest corner, go up the slope that 
lead you before to the fenced are and the crawlspace, but this time ignore the 
fences and go around behind the house up here to find a Gold Skulltula.  It 
shouldn't be too difficult to defeat at all, the difficulty you may find comes 
in actually acquiring the token.  It's easier than you think though, just use 
the jump attack.  To use the jump attack hold down the L/Z button and press 
the A button, Link will jump into the air a little and it should give him just 
enough height to grab hold of that GOLD SKULLTULA [004].  This enemy only 
appears in the village at night which is why you could not come here and get 
it before.  Now that you have the token it's time to leave here once again.

Hyrule Field is absolutely enormous so you might just want to run around and 
explore for awhile, note the different enemies and be careful at night of 
course when the zombies attack.  At some point during the day, make your way 
northeast to reach the Hyrule Market, your primary destination.
______________________________________________________________________________


      __     __)
     (, /|  /|
       / | / |
+-- (_/  |/  |_ ARKET ---------------------------------------------- 01.04 --+

Right as you enter you will see a door beside you, head through that door to 
reach a room with all sorts of pots in it, this is  a great place to gather 
many Rupees and you can leave the room and re-enter as many times as you like.  
When you're satisfied with everything you have acquired then it's time to 
leave this room and proceed into the actual market.  There is all sorts of 
activity going on through here it's hard to decide what to do first.  Many of 
the places here are closed up anyway so that eliminates a lot of the 
possibilities.  Let's take a look at the shops first, find the building with 
the scary man face above it, this is the Bazaar.  The only noteworthy thing 
they sell here is the new shield however don't bother purchasing it at all, 
you're going to get the same shield soon enough, and for free.  Leave the shop 
and head next door, this is the Potion Shop, another shop which sells 
absolutely nothing you should be spending your money on at this point.

Locate the Shooting Gallery, it is the house in the Hyrule Market with the 
large target symbol and arrow through it, this place should not be too 
difficult to find.  What you have to do in the Shooting Gallery is pay 20 
Rupees in order to begin the game.  You are given fifteen shots and you have 
to shoot down ten Rupees.  If you shoot down all ten you will get a special 
prize.  Each Rupee appears in the exact same position every single time:

1 - The first one appears directly in the middle of the screen, a green one.  
    It is not moving so simply fire at it head on when you see it.
2 - The second one is a blue one that moves in from the left side of the 
    screen toward the middle and stays just a bit to the left.
3 - The third one comes in from the right side of the screen toward the 
    middle and stays just a bit right of the middle, a blue one.
4 - The fourth is a green one which pops out of the middle, up into the air
    and then drops down, hit it at the highest point of the jump.
5 - The next one is a blue Rupees similar to the second one however it comes
    from the left near the middle at the same time as number six
6 - Comes in at the same time as number five but from the right side, you
    have to hit both of them before they both exit the screen.
7 - This is a red Rupee which moves at a moderate speed from the right side of 
    the screen to the left side of the screen, it will not stop.
8 - This is a red Rupee which moves at a moderate speed from the right side of 
    the screen to the left side of the screen, it will not stop.
9 - This is another red one which this time moves from the left side of the
    screen to the right side of the screen, quite quickly so aim fast.
10 - This is another red one which this time moves from the left side of the
     screen to the right side of the screen, quite quickly so aim fast.

If you manage to hit eight out of the ten targets then you will be rewarded 
with a free game, if you manage to hit ten out of the ten targets then you 
will be rewarded with a BULLET BAG for your Deku Seeds, this allows you to 
carry 40 Deku Seeds for use with your Fairy Slingshot instead of 30, and is 
quite a helpful item.  Be sure to win this item as it will likely help out.

Leave this place and speak with the disgustingly ugly lady in front of the 
Potion Shop wearing the blue dress, if she won't shut up about her dog then 
you have the right person.  Head down the alley on the opposite side as the 
entrance you came in from to reach the Hyrule Castle area.  Time proceeds 
normally here so your only goal is to wait until it is night time.  Enter the 
Market again to find that most people are gone and there are a bunch of dogs 
running around.  Your goal is to find the fat lady's dog.  It may be 
randomized but for me, her dog was the white dog just left of the main 
entrance to town, left when you are looking directly at the entrance.  It was 
running around just below that person in the window.  Once you find the dog 
you have to head down into the Back Alley.  The Back Alley is located just to 
the right when you're facing the main entrance.  Enter the first door with the 
dog following you (to get the dog to follow you simply walk up to it) and 
inside this house is the lady.  Assuming you brought her the correct dog she 
will reward you with your first PIECE OF HEART [001].  Just like in the other 
Zelda games, four Pieces of Heart will make up one Heart Container and 
increase your maximum number of hearts by one.  Be sure to note that you have 
acquired this one on the checklist and all other ones you find in the future.

What you have to do now is head back to the Hyrule Castle area once again.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE CASTLE ----------------------------------------- 01.05 --+

Now we are going to actually head for the castle itself.  Start by moving 
forward and listening to what the owl has to say, sometimes it's important but 
most of the time, it really isn't.  Note the large tree directly in front of 
you, what you want to do is run up to this tree and hit the A button to roll 
right into it.  You'll here the secret noise and a Gold Skulltula will drop 
out of the tree.  Defeat the enemy and pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA [005].  
Speak to the young girl beside you, next to the vine.  Twice if need be.  She 
should give you the WEIRD EGG.  This item's purpose will become clear soon 
enough, for now just hang onto it.  Begin climbing the vine beside Malon, our 
goal is going to be to get into the castle without alerting the guards.  Stand 
at the top of the vines and just let the game sit through the night (game 
night I mean, not all night.)  In the morning when you hear the rooster, the 
egg you have will hatch into a CHICKEN.  Indeed, the miracle of life.

When you reach the bridge leading over toward the castle, note the ladder 
coming up that you can climb down.  Since there is a patrol at the end of the 
bridge you are going to want to climb down this ladder.  Head through the door 
at the very bottom.  Begin trekking up the path until you see two guards, at a 
reasonable distance start running up the slope in front of them to avoid 
detection, but be careful of the lone guard at the top, be sure to stay behind 
him.  From here you can pretty safely head toward the stairs leading up to the 
castle, but don't actually approach them since they are heavily guarded.  
Instead note the odd rock wall to the left of the entrance, you can in fact 
climb up this wall.  At the top make your way past the Gossip Stone and jump 
down toward the moat.  If you jump into the water near the top you will just 
keep getting Rupees as you swim along down the moat.  Get out at the corner 
where the ground kind of slopes upward.  At the end of the path you will find 
Malon's father there, sleeping on the job.  Since you can't wake him up by 
speaking to him, stand beside him and equip the Chicken.  Press the C button 
which corresponds to the Chicken and he'll be up and about in no time.

After Talon runs off like a madman, notice like usual that these boxes are in 
a little area of depressed ground.  You goal is to grab hold of these boxes 
and push them off the ledge, don't worry about where because there's only on 
possible place you can push them, where the edge is open beside the small 
waterfall.  Grab both blocks and push them off this ledge and that will allow 
you to get up close to the waterfall.  After you've pushed one block over the 
edge and the other block on top of the other one you can climb up on top of it 
and jump toward the waterfall.  Press the A button to crawl in through the 
crawlspace, note that you must go during the day, night will not work here.

Our goal is going to be to sneak past all the guards in this courtyard area 
and reach the end without being thrown out of here, and you're going to do it 
correctly because I'm going to help you.  In the first area there is a square 
hedge and a guard who circles around it.  The solution to this is simple, when 
he is moving away from you with his back turned follow him, and then when he 
goes up the side you proceed to the next area.  The second area has two guards  
patrolling the fountains, the second one likes to move very quickly.  Follow 
the first guard across and then since the second guard moves so fast, you 
might have to follow the first guard up in between the fountains as well, and 
then go left and around to reach the third area.  Here you will note that 
there are many Rupees in the centre, well not that many, and they are not 
worth getting.  You can pick up that many Rupees in a single session of pot 
smashing.  There is a Red Rupee among them yes but still not worth it.  Go up 
the little steps and then grab onto the ledge above.  Walk carefully toward 
the middle and then cross over on the beam that goes to the other side.  The 
camera may be a bit awkward so move very slowly across and if you drop, you'll 
most likely grab onto the ledge.  The fourth area is a statue with two guards 
patrolling around it.  Wait for the seconds guard to pass and move along right 
behind them as you normally would for a single guard.  They don't move at the 
exact same speed which could be a problem but as long as they are reasonably 
close together then you should be able to get to the other side just fine.

The final area is of course the most difficult, you start at the bottom, 
hidden behind a hedge and have to make your way up past two fast moving guards 
that are circling the centre hedge.  Wait until they have both gone past on 
your left and then run up the left side.  Head for the top when the coast is 
clear and then go down the path toward the castle to reach the inner garden.  
Equip the Fairy Slingshot and aim right at the window, when you fire it a Red 
Rupee will pop out, that's worth twenty!  You should also note the pictures 
behind the window.  Take aim and fire at the window on the left, your bonus 
isn't going to be quite as beneficial here.  When you're ready then approach 
Princess Zelda and speak with her.  After a long speech she gives you a 
letter, ZELDA'S LETTER.  Leave Zelda and speak with the person at the exit, 
Impa.  She will teach you our first song for the Fairy Ocarina.  

It's time to go after the next two Spiritual Stones.  Impa tells you that the 
Spiritual Stone of Fire is located on Death Mountain, home of the Gorons so 
that will soon be your destination.  Before that there are a few other places 
which we are going to visit so prepare yourself accordingly.  Our next 
destination will be the Lon Lon Ranch, home of Talon and Malon.  Note the 
large circle in the middle of the Hyrule Field map, this is Lon Lon Ranch.  
Head there now and find the sloping path that leads you up into the ranch.
______________________________________________________________________________


        _    
    ___/__)  
   (, /      
     /       
+-- (_____ ON LON RANCH -------------------------------------------- 01.06 --+

Upon arriving in Lon Lon Ranch what you want to do is go through the door with 
the sign over top of it.  There are all sorts of chickens running around in 
this room and it is going to be the focus of a minigame.  Speak with Talon and 
for ten Rupees you can challenge yourself to try to find all three Super 
Cuccos in thirty seconds.  Basically it's just a matter of lifting chickens 
and throwing them out of the way until you pick up the correct ones.  Try to 
keep your eye on one of the Super Cuccos at the beginning and then just 
lifting chickens randomly to find the other two.  After you have located all 
three of the Super Cuccos, Talon will reward your with some LON LON MILK.  
It's not actually the milk that is worth it but rather the EMPTY BOTTLE that 
you have after the milk is gone.  Empty bottles are rare and special items 
that you can keep virtually any kind of liquid or other items in so the more 
you have the better.  Now leave this house and head up toward the fence.

You should be in an area where the horses are running around, turn to your 
left and look for a tree, it's the only tree you'll see so it can't be very 
hard to locate.  Just like you did at the Hyrule Castle, run up to the tree 
and roll into it to hear the secret sound and reveal a Gold Skulltula.  Defeat 
this enemy to acquire yet another GOLD SKULLTULA [006].  Make your way to the 
far southwest side to find a large stone tower of sorts.  Enter into this 
tower and you will come across some cows and not much else.  Notice all the 
boxes that you can move however.  What you want to do is reveal the corner 
which is blocked by a box.  Let's take it from the perspective of just entering 
the room facing away from the door.  The upper right most box, the one beside 
the cow should be pulled down toward the door.  After one pull go to the other 
side and push it down.  Now grab hold of the box in the upper left corner, the 
corner you want to access and pull it to the right.  You should now be able to 
get to the crawlspace.  Press the A button to get into the crawlspace and at 
the end you will find a PIECE OF HEART [002].  Now leave the tower.

Enter into the middle of the Ranch, there is an entrance on the north side.  
Speak with Malon and equip your Fairy Ocarina.  Speak with her again and 
she'll say something about the song.  Use your Ocarina and she should teach 
you the notes to the song, play along with it and you will learn EPONA'S SONG.  
Now that you have Epona's Song there is something special you can do, not just 
now, but whenever the hell you want.  Head back to the entrance of Lon Lon 
Ranch but instead of going into the house with Talon, go into the one on the 
other side.  Notice there are many cows in this room.  Take an Empty Bottle 
and head over to a cow.  Stand beside it and whip out the Fairy Ocarina.  Play 
Epona's Song, up, left, right, up left, right and the cow will give you free 
Lon Lon Milk, as will other cows.  That's a pretty nice gesture.

There's one more thing to do here, leave Lon Lon Ranch and stand around until 
you hear the howling of night time.  Re-enter Lon Lon Ranch at night.  Make 
your way all the way around to the south side of the ranch, note on your map 
that there is kind of a little feeding area just along the edge of the horse 
fence, not the outer wall of the ranch, the inner fence.  What you want to do 
is go around behind this little area and there you will find another GOLD 
SKULLTULA [007], be sure to defeat it and add another token to the collection.

Next what you have to do is return to the Kokiri Forest, when you first enter 
the forest make a left and climb up onto the ledge.  Note the wall here which 
is covered in vines, you need to climb up this wall and follow the path which 
leads you to The Lost Woods.  There something in here you need to get.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______)
      (, /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ HE LOST WOODS ----------------------------------------------- 1.07 --+

When you get into The Lost Woods there are three paths to take not counting 
the one you just came from.  Move toward the right one and notice the music 
gets louder, head through this one.  Note the object here hanging out of the 
tree, some kind of circle.  Whip out your trust Fairy Slingshot and fire a 
seed at the exact centre of the circle, you'll know you hit the centre if the 
number 100 pops up.  Hit it exactly three times in a row and something good 
will happen, a Deku Scrub will come out and give you another BULLET BAG, this 
time upgrading the amount of seeds you can carry from 40 to 50.  Position 
yourself as if you had just entered this area, facing the tree the next path 
you want to take is the one leading to the left.  At this point you will be 
interrupted by that damn owl once again.  Listen to what he has to say.

The path you want to take is the path on the right, the one to the right of 
the centre path that looks all fancy.  The music will get louder as you 
approach it so you'll know you are going in the right direction.  Make a left 
in this next area and it will take you to another fork, a 4-way split here.  
Continue straight in the same direction you have been going and then make a 
left when you get to the next fork after this one.  Finally make a right here 
at this last one and soon you will reach the Sacred Forest Meadow.  
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ ACRED FOREST MEADOW ---------------------------------------- 01.08 --+

As you approach the gate here there is kind of a miniboss, the wolf pops up 
out of the ground and this wolf means business.  Defeating him shouldn't be 
too difficult though, I recommend busting out that old shield of yours which 
if you're like me, you don't use very often.  Target the wolf with the L/Z 
button and then while targeting hold your shield with the R button to deflect 
attacks.  Now when you think you have a clear shot, for example right after he 
attacks, press the A button to do the jumping slash which is quite powerful.  
Land a few of these babies and the wolf will go crying back to its wolf 
mother.  Enter the next section and keep an eye on your map because it can be 
a little confusing.  Make a right at the very beginning and follow the path to 
a couple of Deku Scrubs.  These aren't the chatty type, you can go ahead and 
kill them after you've used your sword to deflect the Deku Nuts back in their 
direction.  Make your way up to the top until you reach some stairs.

Turn around and notice the ladder on the wall here, climb up this ladder.  In 
the middle there is another ladder and an area where you can jump down.  There 
is a hole down at the bottom of this area, when you drop down the hole you'll 
find a Fairy Fountain.  Faeries have the ability to heal you, they can also be 
captured and placed in bottles.  Whenever you have a Fairy in a bottle, it 
will automatically revive you when you die.  Leave the Fair Fountain and climb 
up the ladder outside.  Return to that large staircase and ascend it to reach 
the top where you will find Saria.  She will teach you SARIA'S SONG which will 
be quite important to you in your quest.  Return to the Lost Woods.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______)
      (, /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ HE LOST WOODS ---------------------------------------------- 01.09 --+

Just as you get here once again the owl swoops down to have a chat with you.  
Listen to the owl and then bid him goodbye.  Get back to Kokiri Forest, then 
turn around and enter the Lost Woods once again.  Make a right and you'll find 
yourself in the area with the target hanging down from the tree.  Descend down 
the ladder then stand on the tree stump.  Equip your Fairy Ocarina to one of 
the C buttons and then use it, a message should be displayed telling you to 
follow along.  They will gradually move up to a song with five notes to it, 
simply listen and look at what they play and then play it yourself.  The prize 
for winning this is a GREEN RUPEE.  Agree to play again and this time you will 
have to follow along up to six notes instead of five.  Your reward for 
completing this challenge will be a BLUE RUPEE.  Finally there is one more 
that involves you playing and memorizing eight notes.  It shouldn't be too 
difficult and your reward for this far exceeds the previous two, you get a 
PIECE OF HEART [003] for completing it successfully.  On one more piece to go.

Ascend up the ladder once again and take the path directly in front of you, at 
the next fork take the path dead ahead once again.  Jump onto the tree stump 
and press the C button associated with your Fairy Ocarina.  Play Saria's Song 
while standing on this stump and the kid will give you yet another PIECE OF 
HEART [004].  This should complete a full Heart Container if you have been 
following the guide up to this point.  There is a path to take in this area, 
not the one you came from, there is another one.  Take that one and it will 
lead you to an area with all sorts of pillars scattered about.  There is 
really only one thing to do here, jump down to the very bottom and run to the 
far end.  There you will find a Deku Scrub hidden behind one of the pillars.  
Use your shield and deflect a Deku Nut back at him.  After doing so approach 
the Deku Scrub and speak to him.  You can purchase the DEKU STICK UPGRADE from 
this little scrub, for the low low price of 40 Rupees he will enable you to be 
able to carry around 20 Deku Sticks, it's well worth the price.  There is a 
long ladder back on the opposite side, take it and leave The Lost Woods.

Your destination finally is now Kakariko Village as Impa was talking about.  
Leave Kokiri Forest and head for the Hyrule Field.  You will find Kakariko 
Village located near the drawbridge to the Hyrule Market.  East of the bridge 
there is a river with a bridge going over it, cross this bridge and it will 
lead you in the general direction of a set of stairs leading up into the 
mountains.  Ascend these stairs and follow the path into Kakariko Village.
______________________________________________________________________________


       __   __)
      (, ) /
        /(
     ) /  \_
+-- (_/      AKARIKO VILLAGE --------------------------------------- 01.10 --+

For a change from the usual what you are going to want to do is enter Kakariko 
Village at night, preferably just after the howling so that it hasn't been 
night for too long.  There are quite a few Gold Skulltulas in this town to 
acquire so let's go hunting for a few of those first.  Enter the village and 
go past the tree in the centre.  When you have the first opportunity to make a 
right, do so and climb up the stairs.  Make a right at the top of the stairs 
and you will come across a Skulltula crawling on the side of the house, 
eliminate this enemy and pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA [008].  Return to that 
tree in the centre of town, this time keep moving in the same direction as 
when you come in from the entrance of town, climb the stairs straight ahead of 
you and turn around to find another set of stairs going even higher, up to 
that kind of brick area.  Here you will find another Skulltula crawling around 
on the brick wall, eliminate it and pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA [009].  Come in 
from the entrance to the village once again and make a left this time, there 
is a large set of stairs leading up to the area above.  When you get up there 
move to the north side of that tall tower, then pull out your Fairy Slingshot 
and aim up at it.  Crawling around on the ladder is yet another Skulltula, 
fire two Deku Seeds at this enemy and then climb up the ladder to acquire the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [010].  Next it's time to visit the local cemetery.

At the southeast corner of town you will find the path to the Kakariko Village 
Graveyard.  Follow that path to get to the graveyard.  The old man Dampe 
should be wandering around the graveyard here, when you speak to him he offers 
to dig up a portion of the ground for the low low price of 10 Rupees.  The 
only purpose of doing this is to hope you acquire the PIECE OF HEART [005].  
It's not a common occurrence and it can get pretty annoying.  What you're 
trying to do is speak to him and have him dig while he's on the patches of 
dirt on the stone path, not the stone path itself, if you look you'll see 
them.  Now what you want to do is head over to the east side of the graveyard 
beside the large tombstone and stand on the Triforce emblem on the ground.  
Use your Fairy Ocarina and play Zelda's Lullaby, which is left, up, right, 
left, up, right.  After doing this there is a large explosion and the 
tombstone is destroyed, revealing a path down into the ground.

Jump down into the whole which leads down and you'll find yourself in the 
Royal Tomb.  What you will want to do is equip the Fairy Slingshot and use it 
to eliminate the four bats you see perched on the walls here.  After doing 
that the door will be unlocked and you can climb up the ledge and continue 
through it.  Zombies!  These things are so hideous that if they even look at 
you, you will be completely frozen and unable to move.  If he jumps you then 
simply jam buttons on the controller until you can move again and start 
running.  Ignore the zombies, just stick to one side of the room and stay 
close to the wall as you make your way toward the door.  When you reach the 
end of the tomb take a look at the inscription on the wall.  You will notice 
that below it there is some kind of melody already written on there, perhaps 
you should play it.  After doing so you learn the SUN'S SONG.  Return to the 
entrance of the tomb and step on the glowing platform to get outside.

Return to the graveyard and play the Sun's Song which you just learned to 
change the time to night once again, as most of the stuff there is to do in 
the graveyard must be done at night.  Head to the entrance of the graveyard 
and follow the path up past the first row of tombstones to reach the second 
row on the east side.  The fourth tombstone from the right (south) is going to 
have a hidden path below it, drop down and defeat the zombie, then stand on 
those little steps and equip your Fairy Ocarina.  Play the Sun's Song, right, 
down, up, right, down, up and a treasure chest should appear right in front of 
you.  Open this treasure chest to get the PIECE OF HEART [006].  Return to 
where you enter the graveyard and head up toward the first tombstones, note 
that one of the ones right in front of you has three small plants growing in 
front of it.  What you want to do is grab hold of this tombstone with the A 
button and then pull it backward to reveal a secret hole.  Open the small 
chest here to acquire a HYLIAN SHIELD, the one I told you not to purchase 
before because you get it here for free.   Leave the grave and leave the 
village entirely.  Play the Sun's Song to change to daytime, then go back.

Head back to the graveyard entrance but don't actually go into the graveyard, 
instead speak with the woman standing just opposite the entrance with her 
hands on her face beside the chicken coop.  Speak to her and she will ask you 
to bring back her Cuccos to the pen.  The first one is easy, it should be 
walking around right beside you near the entrance to the graveyard, grab it 
with the A button and throw it into the fenced area beside the girl.  Go back 
to the entrance of the village and you will find a second Cucco wandering 
around minding its own business.  Pick it up over your head with the A button 
and then run all the way east up the stairs, and turn around to find another 
set of stairs going up to that incomplete brick house.  There is a special 
neat trick about Cuccos, when you jump off a cliff the violent flapping of 
their wings will allow you to glide.  Jump off the edge of this incomplete 
house with the Cucco still in hand and glide directly west to reach the little 
raised ledge with the Cucco, behind that fence that you could not access 
otherwise.  Throw your Cucco back down into the middle of town and take this 
one over to the chicken coop.  Go back to the main middle area of town with 
the tree and notice the crate against the house when you face northeast.  
Break this crate by rolling into it and grab the Cucco, bring it back to the 
chicken coop.  From the middle of town take the large north set of stairs up 
toward the path that leads to Death Mountain, you should come across a Cucco 
there which is just minding its own business.  Pick up this Cucco and bring it 
back to the girl.  Grab hold of the fourth one, which is the one you threw off 
the edge when you landed and got the Cucco behind the fence.  Don't throw it 
back, but head toward the graveyard entrance, climb the stairs leading north.

You should still have the Cucco in hand like I said, you'll need it here in 
order to glide.  Notice that if you run straight north and jump off the edge, 
you can't quite make it over the large fence, don't worry.  Notice that beside 
the large fence is a smaller fence that actually extends out further toward 
you, this fence you CAN barely glide over so do that now, you'll land beside a 
door.  Face the main area where you just came from and throw the Cucco back, 
you can get it easily afterward.  Jump down behind you and you'll land beside 
a hole, drop down into the hole and open the small treasure chest to acquire a 
RED RUPEE.  Climb out of this hole and then go north to find a Cucco beside 
the ladder.  Lift it up and throw it over the fence like you did the other 
one, it will be there waiting for you after.  Now climb that fence up to where 
the windmill is and grab the final Cucco.  Use this one to glide back across 
and throw it into the chicken coop.  Now all you have to do is find those two 
which you threw over the fence for later and that takes care of all seven.  
Speak to the girl and she will reward you nicely with another EMPTY BOTTLE.

Your next destination is going to be the House of Skulltula.  When you enter 
the village from Hyrule Field, stick to the right until you come across some 
stairs on your right.  Head up them, and at the top turn right and immediately 
right again as a 180 degree turn.  In front of you should be the door into the 
House of Skulltula.  The people in here have been imprisoned by Skulltula and 
only you can free them by collecting Gold Skulltula tokens.  Assuming you have 
collected at least ten, the guy on the right side of the house will be free 
and he will reward you with the ADULT'S WALLET.  Now I have a quick trip for 
you to make, so please indulge since it's close by.  Leave the village and 
head across the bridge and into Hyrule Castle Town.  You don't actually have 
to enter the Market, go through the door right beside the drawbridge entrance 
and begin filling this new wallet of yours with plenty of Rupees.  While 
you're here there is a Skulltula you can collect which was not mentioned 
before, note the two crates at the back of the room?  Run into them and roll 
with the A button to break them open, one will reveal a Skulltula inside.  
Defeat this enemy in order to acquire GOLD SKULLTULA [011].  

Now it's finally time to proceed with the game.  Head back to Kakariko Village 
once again, during the day or night, it doesn't really matter.  I guess that 
day is preferable most of the time.  Approach the guard beside the gate at the 
north side of town.  Equip Zelda's Letter on one of your C buttons and use it 
while standing directly in front of the guard.  After seeing the letter the 
guard has no choice but to open the gate for you and allow you to pass, it's 
time to begin your arduous trek up the Death Mountain toward Goron City.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EATH MOUNTAIN TRAIL --------------------------------- 01.11 --+

The first creatures you will encounter on this trail are the jumping spider 
enemies you may recognize from previous games.  They should not be too 
difficult to defeat, just hold the R button to defend with your Hylian Shield 
when they jump into the air.  Make your way up the path past the Goron and 
past the door blocked by the enormous boulder.  Continue up the linear path 
toward the end where you will find the large entrance to the Goron City.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ORON CITY ------------------------------------------- 01.12 --+

Goron City is a relatively open and active place, perhaps not so much at the 
moment, but soon.  There are a few things to do while you are here, the first 
of which is to launch yourself off the edge at the top here and land way down 
at the bottom of the city.  Note that there is kind of a rug thing in front of 
the door to the north, stand on this rug and use your Fairy Ocarina.  Play 
Zelda's Lullaby to open this large door.  Head inside the room and speak with 
the leader of the Gorons, Darunia.  After speaking with him, stand in front of 
the large Goron and play Saria's Song.  The Goron will be so happy that you 
played the song, and offers the Spiritual Stone of Fire if you head for the 
Dodongo's Cave and rid it of all the monsters.  He also gives you the GORON'S 
BRACELET which will allow you to pick up bombs from Bomb Flowers and use them.

Equip a Deku Stick, something you likely haven't used in awhile and use one of 
the torches in Darunia's Room to set it on fire.  Exit this room and just 
outside, run around lighting the various torches as well as the Bomb Flowers 
on the wall.  When all four of the torches are lit, the large pot in the 
middle begins to spin around and around.  When at least one of the Bomb 
Flowers is lit, it blasts open that door beside it and reveals the entrance to 
the shop.  There's nothing really important to buy here, so just note that 
this shop does in fact exist, and then leave.  You'll need another Deku Stick 
at this point, if you do not have any then you should note they can be found 
by breaking the various pots in Darunia's Room.  Light the Deku Stick using 
one of the four torches you just lit, preferably one of the ones at the bottom 
of the stairs then climb the stairs and light the two torches on the next 
level up.  After doing that go down the hall past the torches and light the 
Bomb Flowers here.  What you have just done is blast open a secret tunnel 
which takes you to the Lost Woods, a great shortcut from Goron City.

The time has come to leave Goron City, but not through the shortcut, rather 
back through the way you came in that takes you up to the Death Mountain 
Trail.  When you get outside make a right turn and follow the path to a Goron 
laying down beside a Bomb Flower.  Speak to the Goron to learn about Bomb 
Flowers as if you haven't already been using them.  Grab a bomb from the Bomb 
Flower, hold up on the analog stick and press A to throw it over the edge.  
The camera should change to give you a view of Dodongo's Cavern, if you threw 
the bomb correctly then it will completely blast the boulder blocking the 
entrance to bits.  When you manage to do this successfully, take a look on 
your left for a small platform and jump from there down to the cave entrance.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   ODONGO'S CAVERN ------------------------------------- 01.13 --+

Right away you are greeted by a strong sturdy door which will not allow you to 
pass.  The solution to this is simple, just grab one of the bombs out of the 
Bomb Flowers and throw it at the door, the explosion should make short work of 
it.  As you enter the cave Navi takes a moment to speak with you, listen to 
what she says because some of these lava pits can be quite dangerous.  Jump 
from this bridge straight across to the pillar which is moving up and down.  
When it's at he top, jump from the pillar to the middle platform.  There is 
an enemy in the middle of this platform, a laser-beam shooting eyeball statue.  
Note the eyeball which spins around slowly, just make sure you never get into 
the line of sight of this eyeball and you'll be fine.  While still facing the 
same direction, turn right and jump across to the pillar, and from there to 
the ledge on the other side.  Notice that there are two doors to be blown up 
on this platform, one on each side and a Bomb Flower on the middle.  From 
facing the same direction, blast the one on the right.  The one on the left 
only reveals a simple Gossip Stone.  If you happen to get seen by the laser 
statue here, simply run away from it and once you get a certain distance away, 
the laser will stop firing.  Proceed through the door you blasted open.

Do not underestimate the enemies in this next room.  They are small slugs 
which squirm up to you and only take a single strike to defeat, however 
seconds after you defeat them the slugs will curl up and explode, so keep your 
distance.  Defeat the first three and then when the second three pop up, as 
you head down the hall, note the wall on the left which can be blown up.  Kill 
at least one of these enemies near the door and allow it to explode, revealing 
a secret path.  Take this secret path into a room with a single torch.  Notice 
that one the wall behind the torch there are three bats, eliminate them using 
your Fairy Slingshot from a distance and then get close.  There is a Skulltula 
here on the wall, defeat it and use the jumping attack to acquire another GOLD 
SKULLTULA [012].  Return to the previous room, make a right turn and climb up 
onto the higher platform where you will find a large blue button.  When you 
step on this button the gate blocking the door disappears.  The problem is 
that whenever you step off the button, the gate drops down again.  What you 
have to do is grab hold of one of those statues on the side using the A 
button, and drag it on top of the button so it holds the button down, then you 
are free to climb up to the doorway and go through it without any problem.

Eliminate the two bats in this next hallway and grab the treasures hidden 
within the pots.  Enter the room through the door at the end of the hall and 
it locks behind you.  This room is the battlefield for a duel between you and 
two sword wielding lizard monsters.  There's no way to escape so whip out your 
sword.  These things make the absolute most annoying noises ever, but that 
doesn't mean they are difficult.  Take on one at a time if possible, but 
that's usually the way it works out anyway.  A safe way to win is to simply 
block an attack then go in with one of your own, defending and them using the 
jump attack by locking on and pressing the A button is an effective tactic, 
you can also go offensive and just keep attacking without giving them a chance 
to attack themselves.  After they have taken a lot of damage the lizards may 
retreat, obviously all you have to do is run after them.  Once both lizards 
have been defeated, proceed through the door and continue on.  If you've lost 
track which door is which, no problem.  If you go through and you're back in 
that hallway again, then it's the wrong door, continue on to the correct path.

This room is populated by a couple of Dodongo enemies.  These are similar to 
the ones you fought before, however much bigger and fully developed.  They 
weakness is their tail on the back end, when you strike the tail the enemies 
do a 180 degree turn and blast fire in your face.  While they are turning you 
should have enough time to move and get behind for another strike if the first 
one did not finish them off.  Grab hold of a bomb and use it to blast open the 
wall beside the entrance to this room.  It leads to a small room with a single 
Deku Scrub.  Since the Hylian Shield totally disintegrates Deku Nuts that hit 
it, just use your Fairy Slingshot and fire  Deku Seed at the scrub when he 
appears, that works just as well.  This scrub will sell you Deku Sticks at 15 
Rupees apiece, you'll need a few but I'm sure you likely already have some.

Back outside this room again, kill all of the Dodongos.  Be sure to stay away 
from them after they have been defeated, just like the mini Dodongos they will 
explode just before death.  Whip out a Deku Stick and then light it on fire 
using the torch in this room, use that Deku Stick to light all of the other 
three torches and that will open the door at the end of the hall.  Head 
through this door and step on the button.  Jump out of this little area back 
into the main room once again, and then from here onto the pillar which is 
moving up and down.  You need to get across to the middle platform and keep 
going straight to the opposite side of the room from where you came in.  Grab 
a bomb from out of the Bomb Flower and use it on the cracked wall beside you, 
this wall will crumble and reveal a large treasure chest inside.  Open the 
chest to acquire the DUNGEON MAP.  Now take the door just beside this one.

There are nearly two dozen Bomb Flowers growing in this room, your goal is 
clear as the light of day.  Grab hold of a bomb and use it to blast open the 
wall, revealing the path to the next room.  The door and a gate close behind 
you, you won't be getting back easily.  There is something fishy about those 
statues, as it turns out, the one in the middle is no ordinary statue.  It is 
alive, and has very bad intentions.  You can only kill it with a bomb and it 
only comes alive once you touch it, so the solution is to throw a bomb at it, 
then when the bomb glows red, slash the enemy with your sword and run like 
hell.  That should take care of him no problem.  Open the enormous chest and 
you will receive the COMPASS for this dungeon, that will come in handy, now we 
can start using more accurate directions, keep an eye on your minimap.

Head back to the previous room, the one with the dozens of Bomb Flowers 
growing along the wall.  Note the two following things, there is a single Bomb 
Flower growing near the door you just came through, and there is no 
Bomb Flower growing in the exact middle of all the other ones.  The solution 
of course is to take the bomb from out of the flower beside the door and set 
it down in that empty space.  When the bomb explodes it will also ignite the 
two bombs on its left and right, and then they will ignite the next two etc.  
Eventually when all is said, done, and blown up, those explosions weakened the 
concrete structure in the middle and now you can climb up the stairs.  Make 
either a left or a right when you reach the top, they both lead to the door.  
When you arrive you'll notice that there is a Skulltula crawling around on the 
wall which you can kill with a Jump Attack, and above it is a Gold Skulltula 
which you can kill using your Fairy Slingshot.  This wall is climb-able, so 
climb up and grab your GOLD SKULLTULA [013] then head through the door.

Once again the door closes behind you as does the gate.  There are a couple of 
bats flying around in this room, not normal bats, flaming bats!  Kill them 
using your Fairy Slingshot before they can swoop down and set you ablaze.  
Three of the four statues in this room are actually enemies, use the same 
tactic as before where you place the bomb down before you actually bring them 
to life.  After the three of them have been eliminated, move the one normal 
statue out of the way and climb up the ladder.  Take the east door to get out 
of this room.  Be careful here once again of the flaming bats flying around 
this room.  There is a gap in the bridge which obviously means you should take 
caution, but you should not have any problem jumping the gap and continuing 
east on the other side.  Keep going and enter the next room.

It's dangerous down at the bottom of this room here but you don't have a 
choice about jumping down.  Watch out for the spike traps which react to your 
movement and try to hit you.  Listen to what Navi says about L/Z targeting.  
On the east side of this room you will find a large block under a ladder.  
Grab hold of this block and pull it away from the wall.  Behind it you will 
find a single recovery heart.  Climb up on top of the block and then ascend 
the ladder.  At the top of the ladder make a right, then do a 180 degree turn 
and drop down onto the pillar with the Bomb Flower and the small chest 
containing a RED RUPEE.  Your timing for this next thing has to be perfect, 
lift a bomb above your head and wait.  Let it start flashing red and keep 
holding it.  When it starts flashing extremely fast, wait approximately one 
second or so and then throw the bomb at the wall at the top of the ladder.  It 
should blast it down.  Drop down and take the ladder to the next area.

Equip your Fairy Slingshot and aim it up at the eyeball above the door at the 
end here.  Now that the flames have been extinguished, jump across to the door 
on the other side and proceed through the hall.  Ignore the smaller Dodongos 
here and you make your way through the door at the end.  it's time for yet 
another battle against these lizard enemies, the same exact tactic as last 
time still applies.  Defend against their attacks and then use a few attacks 
of your own.  If you're low on health note that there are two recovery hearts 
down at the end of the long path, but at that point you aren't fighting over 
top of hot magma, you're fighting over top of the room below, so don't fall.  
When the two lizards have been defeated, proceed through the west door.

Extinguish the flame by using your Fairy Slingshot to fire up at the eyeball 
above the door.  After the flame has been extinguished, jump to the platform 
and aim left.  There is another eyeball in here which will extinguish the 
second flame.  Go through the door and jump across the gap, here inside the 
large treasure chest you will find the BOMB BAG.  The Bomb Bag allows you to 
place bombs yourself rather than relying on Bomb Flowers, this particular bag 
comes equipped with a maximum carrying capacity of 20 bombs.  Take the west 
exit beside this chest and step on the button to raise the pillar up into the 
air.  Head right and cross over the large bridge to the other side.  Use your 
newly acquired Bomb Bag to blast open this wall and reveal a small chest 
containing a BLUE RUPEE.  Now on your way back, note that there are two gaps 
in the bridge.  Stand right at the edge of the first gap and use a bomb.  Drop 
it down the gap and wait for it to explode.  It should land in the eye cavity 
and make the little secret noise.  Now do the same thing, drop another bomb 
down the other bridge gap and when both eyes are illuminated, the mouth opens.

If you want you can return to the previous room and bomb the north wall, there 
are two Deku Scrubs in there will to sell you Deku Nuts and Deku Seeds, it's 
really not worth it at all.  Jump down to the main room and enter through the 
mouth door you just revealed.  Use your Fairy Slingshot to eliminate the bats 
and then jump up into the tunnel leading east.  Once again be sure to kill the 
flaming bats before they cause you any real trouble.  What you're trying to do 
primarily is just climb around on the blocks and gradually make your way to 
the north side of this room, where there is a wall you can blast open using a 
bomb.  Drop a bomb beside the statue in this room, kill it if you wish but all 
that matters is that it gets out of the way because behind it is another GOLD 
SKULLTULA [014].  Grab the token and leave the room.  Climb around in this 
room once again and make your way across to the west door.  This hall leads to 
a block you can push down into the room below.  Grab this block after you have 
pushed it over the edge and drop it down into that hole in the middle.  When 
you're ready, head through the west door and open the small treasure chest 
here to acquire BOMBS.  Place a bomb in the centre of this room to blast a 
hole and then drop down the hole in the middle to trigger the boss fight.

I cannot begin to explain the simplicity of this boss, perhaps I will explain 
it by basically just telling you how to defeat him.  First of all you should 
acquaint yourself with the little 45 degree slope between the path you are on 
and the lava in the middle, while standing on this slope you are immune to all 
his attacks.  None of them can hit you there, you could go eat dinner and come 
back the boss would sill be running around like an idiot.  As for actually 
fighting the boss here's how it works.  He stops and begins to suck in air for 
his fire breath attack which launches a blast of fire down the path and around 
the corner.  What you have to do is lift a bomb above your head while the boss 
is breathing in, and then throw it into his mouth.  When the bomb explodes the 
boss drops down and that is your chance to hit him exactly once.  Use the Jump 
Attack when he falls.  After the boss gets up he will roll down and around the 
corner, dodge this attack easily by standing on that little sloping area like 
I told you.  At the end the boss uncurls itself and the process begins all 
over again.  It should take only four hits to kill the boss with normal 
attacks, and as little as three with the Jump Attack.  He will never be able 
to use his fire breath as long as you always throw a bomb into his mouth.  In 
fact the only real danger of damage in this fight is from you, holding a bomb 
for too long.  When the boss is defeated he will roll himself into the lava in 
the middle of the map and then just die, solidifying it in the process.  Grab 
the HEART CONTAINER from the middle of the room and take the warp back 
outside.  Darunia appears and makes good of his word, you receive the GORON'S 
RUBY.  From here all you have to do is run up the path to Goron City again.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ORON CITY ------------------------------------------- 01.14 --+

Enter the Goron City and drop down to the second lowest level, you should be 
at just about the same height as the top of that spinning urn.  What you have 
to do is stand at the side here and throw bombs from your Bomb Bag into the 
urn.  If you manage to get one in successfully then the urn will spin around 
in circles even faster and more wildly.  Note the three different expressions 
on the side of it, one happy, one neutral and one angry.  Your goal is to spin 
it so it lands on the happy face and when it does, a PIECE OF HEART [007] will 
pop out.  You can also get a number of Rupees from doing this as well.

Make your way up now to the next level, the one in the middle.  This one is 
easy to discern from the rest of them because it has a crazy Goron rolling 
around, damaging you if you get anywhere close to him.  Notice that on the 
north side there is kind of an underpass, a closed in tunnel that the Goron 
passes through.  Drop a bomb from your Bomb Bag in the middle of this tunnel 
and time it so that the bomb explodes just as the Goron is rolling over top of 
it.  For doing this successfully the Goron rewards you with the BIG BOMB BAG 
which allows you to carry 30 bombs instead of a mere 20.  If you're having 
trouble with it, note that most people tend to overestimate the Goron's speed, 
he really isn't going very fast at all.  Now head back up to the top.

On the west side of the path there are three medium sized rocks sitting in 
your way.  Take a bomb out of your new, larger Bomb Bag and place it in front 
of these three rocks to blast them out of the way.  This path now leads you 
through a little tunnel.  At the end of this tunnel you will find a kind of 
rock maze.  There is no way I can dictate to you through words how to get 
through this, but fortunately it's not a matter of actually being difficult, 
it's just a matter of having enough bombs.  As long as you have a full supply 
you'll be able to blast your way through and inevitably make your way to the 
end no matter how poorly you navigate the maze.  At the end you will find two 
chests containing a PURPLE RUPEE, and another PURPLE RUPEE.  Behind these two 
treasure chests is a crate, smash the crate by rolling into it with the A 
button to reveal a Skulltula.  When you defeat the enemy you can pick up the
