GOLD SKULLTULA [015].  That's pretty much all there is to do in Goron City, 
your destination is the Death Mountain, take the trail up to get there.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EATH MOUNTAIN TRAIL --------------------------------- 01.15 --+

Leave Goron City but follow the normal path, don't jump down the shortcut 
where the Bomb Flower is.  As you leave the city stick to the wall on your 
left which will eventually turn into a fence.  When the fence on your left 
ends, take a look at the wall on your right to find a weak area of wall.  Blow 
it up using one of your bombs and inside you will find a chest containing a 
PURPLE RUPEE.  Next return to the entrance of the Death Mountain Trail, where 
you entered it from Kakariko Village.  Make your way up the trail while 
keeping an eye on the right wall, when you reach a section that looks a little 
bit awkward, equip your bombs and place a bomb in front of that section of 
wall.  The wall breaks open and reveals a very small path that leads only to a 
Skulltula.  Defeat the enemy with your Fairy Slingshot and then you can climb 
the wall it was clinging to get the GOLD SKULLTULA [016].  Climb back up 
toward Goron City but stop at that steep hill, the one that leads up to a 
whole bunch of rocks and a sign.  The sign should say Mountain Summit, Danger 
Ahead - Keep Out.  If you find this sign then you're in the right place.

There are indeed rocks in your way here, rocks that will not allow you to 
climb up any further.  Fortunately you have that Bomb Bag that you got from 
the cavern so take out a bomb and use it to blast these rocks which are in 
your way.  At the top of the first sloping path you will have to lift a bomb 
above your head and time the throw in order to explode the rock across from 
you.  Jump over the gap and climb up further.  You will come across a large 
rock in your path and when you destroy this rock it reveals a hole underneath.  
Drop down this hole to find three things, Rupees, health and a cow.  If you 
like since you probably aren't using them for anything else, you can fill 
those two Empty Bottles with Lon Lon Milk by standing in front of the cow and 
playing Epona's Song on your Fairy Ocarina.  Epona’s Song is up, left, right, 
up, left, right if you don't remember.  Now leave this little hole.

Continue along the path until suddenly, out of nowhere everything begins to 
shake and rocks fall down from the skies above onto your head.  Death Mountain 
is erupting and taking all its rage out on young Link.  You have two choices, 
you can hold the R button to shield yourself until it stops and then run for a 
couple of seconds before it starts again, or you can realize that the rocks 
really don't do much damage at all and just keep running until you reach the 
end of the path where the eruption stops.  There is a wall here to climb, be 
sure to equip your Fairy Slingshot and fire up at the Skulltulas before 
actually beginning to climb.  There are a total of three of them on this wall 
and you should not attempt to climb to the top until you know that all three 
have been taken care of.  At the top equip your bombs and then use one of them 
on the wall to your left.  Take the path into the Great Fairy's Fountain.

This is a critical moment for Link, something good is going to happen.  
Similar to what you did in the graveyard, stand on the Triforce emblem and 
play Zelda's Lullaby.  The combination is of course left, up, right, left, up, 
right.  The Fairy of Power comes out of the water and grants you a sword 
technique.  You finally have a magic meter now and can use the magic Spin 
Attack.  You can still perform the normal Spin Attack that doesn't consume 
magic energy by rotating the analog stick and pressing the B button.  Leave 
and take the pass this time on the right, beside the Great Fairy Fountain.  
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EATH MOUNTAIN CRATER -------------------------------- 01.16 --+

A timer appears on the screen, basically this timer keeps track of your 
ability to withstand the searing hear from the volcano.  You only have as much 
time in the crater as the timer shows.  The first thing you are going to want 
to do here is roll into the crate directly on your left when you enter.  A 
Skulltula pops out of the crate when it smashes and you can kill it and then 
pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA [017].  After doing this you have wasted precious 
valuable seconds so leave the crater and then enter it a second time.

This time you are going to be going in a little bit further.  Head straight 
ahead and soon enough you will come to an edge where there is a thin board 
going across.  Ignore the board and instead, hold up on the analog stick very 
slightly so that you are only walking.  As you walk off the edge you should 
grab onto it, this wall is climb-able which means you can climb down as well.  
Descend down this wall and as you go you should notice an opening in the 
middle, get below it and climb up to reach the inner section.  Here you will 
find another PIECE OF HEART [008].  If you have been following the guide then 
that is your eighth Piece of Heart and you have now just netted yourself an 
additional Heart Container.  If you are having trouble with this, like for 
example finding that your timer gets lower and lower even when you leave, I'm 
fairly sure it's based on how many hearts you have.  Head back into the Great 
Fairy Fountain and have her heal you, it should start at the maximum time.

Leave the Death Mountain Crater and speak to the owl perched upon the sign.  
He offers to lend you a ride back down the mountain, accept his offer and he 
will drop you onto a roof in Kakariko Village, not just any roof, a roof which 
you would have difficulty accessing any other way.  We need to take advantage 
of this opportunity.  Here's what to do, after you are dropped off on the 
room, walk slowly to the southeast most corner of the roof.  Look at your map 
to judge this, you should be beside a wall and just above the chicken coop 
where you threw all the chickens.  Directly below you should be kind of a 
little roof cover thing for the chicken coop, what you want to do is walk very 
slowly and hang off the edge, then drop onto this roof cover.  Beside you is a 
secret entrance into the house, take it.  Grab the PIECE OF HEART [009] here 
and then leave the house, I guess you could use that cow to get some Lon Lon 
Milk if you really wanted.  Anyway now that you have access to the bombs there 
are a number of things you can do that you couldn't do before without them.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ADVENTURE! ------------------------------------- 01.05 --+

Your first destination in this optional adventure is going to be the Hyrule 
Town Market.  Next door to the Shooting Gallery, the Bombchu Bowling Alley is 
now open for business.  Head there now, each game costs 30 Rupees and you'll 
likely be playing a lot so be sure to bring a lot of money with you.  Here's 
how it works, there are a number of prizes which are randomized.  The prizes 
are Bombs, Purple Rupee, Bombchus, PIECE OF HEART [010] and BIGGEST BOMB BAG, 
the final two are obviously the ones you'll really be working toward.  Each 
game gives you a total of ten Bombchus and three targets, the first and final 
targets are always identical while the middle one is randomized.  You can lift 
one Bombchu into the air with the B button, once lifted the bomb is activated 
and it's on a timer so do not lift until you are fully ready to throw.  A 
Bombchu will go in a straight line ahead of where you throw it, but it will 
not explode when it hits a wall, rather it will climb that wall.  You have to 
use your geometric knowledge and angles and such to win this game:

1 - For this one, the easiest one all you have to do is stand in the middle in 
    front of the blue arrow and throw a Bombchu straight ahead.  It should go 
    into the hole in the middle and explode, taking down the wall.

2 - As I mentioned there are three possible targets for this one and they are
    randomized.  The easiest is when the target appears in the middle.  
    Before you only had to deal with the spike trap obstacle but now you
    have to get it past that and the Cucco as well.  Wait for the Cucco
    to move or when it's about to move and throw your Bombchu straight ahead.
    The next possibility is for it to appear on the far left.  For this one 
    stand a couple black notches left of the middle and look straight ahead.  
    Tap the analog stick extremely lightly to the left and throw to get it in.  
    The third and final possibility is that it will appear on the far right.  
    This is similar to the one on the far left however I find you have to 
    stand closer to the right wall for this one than stood close to the left 
    wall for the other one.  Rather than standing a few notches off the 
    centre, stand a few notches left or the right wall and tap the analog 
    stick weakly to the right.  It should go down and then into the hole.

3 - This final one is the most difficult, a large Cucco appears in the middle 
    and while it is very possible to get it down the middle past both of them, 
    it's extremely difficult and not worth doing.  Instead stand exactly where 
    you would stand if it was on the far left, about two or three black 
    notches left of the middle arrow.  Now tap the analog stick on a 45 
    degree angle to the upper right, or northeast then let loose.  If you're 
    lucky it will curve around the wall and into the hole eventually.

With those two important items now in your possession, it's time to head for 
the Hyrule Castle again.  If you want to save ten Rupees you can climb up the 
vine and drop down behind the gate, or you can just approach the guard and 
give him ten Rupees, he'll let you through.  Just past the gate there is a 
fork in the road and the path leading right leads to a large rock.  Now that 
you have bombs, pull one out of your bag and use it to destroy this rock 
standing in your way, then enter the crawlspace behind it.  This turns out to 
be one of the Great Fairy's Fountains.  Head into it and approach the water, 
just like before you have to take out your Fairy Ocarina and play it while 
standing on the Triforce emblem.  Play Zelda's Lullaby, left, up, right, left, 
up, right and a Fairy will appear in front of you.  She teaches you the DIN'S 
FIRE technique.  After that she heals you up and sends you on your way.  The 
easiest way to get out of here is to just get caught.  Return to Hyrule Field.

Directly west of the drawbridge leading into Hyrule Castle Town there is a  
rock just sitting out there in the field, take out one of your bombs and use 
it to blast that rock to bits.  Drop down the hole that you find below the 
rock and open the small chest to acquire a BLUE RUPEE.  How very not worth it.  
Anyway there is only really one other thing of note on this field.  At the 
very northwest corner of Hyrule Field, west of where you just were there is 
another rock across the stream.  Below it you will find a normal Fairy 
Fountain with a number of faeries flying around just waiting to heal you up or 
be caught in Empty Bottles for use at a later time.  Leave the fountain and 
head all the way back to Kokiri Forest where you began the game.

Enter The Lost Woods here.  Make a right at the first fork and a left at the 
second.  Here you will come across a boulder which you can blow up using one 
of your bombs.  Down below it you will find a chest containing BLUE RUPEE.  
Leave this place and then take the door to the right of the hole in the ground 
then left at the spring.  Head straight at this next junction and then left at 
the one after that, blow up the rock and drop down the hole.  There are two 
Deku Scrubs here, fire your Fairy Slingshot at both of them.  The one on the 
right is useless however the one on the left sells you the DEKU NUT UPGRADE 
for only 40 Rupees.  Be sure to take advantage of this offer.  Head back and 
take that secret shortcut that brings you to Goron City.  Leave Goron City 
through the top exit to get back to the Death Mountain Trail.  Make your way 
down to Kakariko Village, but before you actually go into the village, make 
sure it's night time.  When you enter the village, take a look on the back of 
the large house on the north side, it should be right near you.  Here you will 
find another GOLD SKULLTULA [018] crawling around on the back wall.

That's about it for optional stuff (although Din's Fire isn't optional, you'll 
have to get it at some point eventually.)  What you want to do now is head out 
to Hyrule Field and then make your way east from the entrance to Kakariko 
Village.  Locate the river flowing along and the source of the river is your 
destination.  At the northeast side of Hyrule Field you will find a large 
enclosed area where the river flows from, follow this to reach Zora's River.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ___   
      (,   ) 
          /  
        _/_  
     )   /   
+-- (__ / ORA'S RIVER ---------------------------------------------- 01.18 --+

Just after you enter the owl speaks to your again.  He says the Zora do not 
let just anyone in, a person must have some connection to the royal family.  
Right now as you probably already know, Zelda's Lullaby is your connection to 
the royal family so keep that in mind.  Equip bombs onto one of your C buttons 
and then blow up all the rocks which are blocking your path.  Before you 
proceed, note the tree on your left.  Run up and roll into it to make a 
Skulltula fall out of the tree.  Defeat this enemy and you will receive GOLD 
SKULLTULA [019].  Make your way along the path and around the corner, you'll 
come across some guy just sitting there.  Walk up to him and speak with him.  
He offers to sell you a MAGIC BEAN for ten Rupees, and that's a pretty good 
deal, so take him up on his offer and buy one.  Plant this Magic Bean in the 
little hole beside where the guy is sitting.  For now it will remain a little 
shrub but eventually it will grow tall into a mighty beanstalk.  Keep speaking 
to him and he will continue to sell you Magic Beans.  Every Magic Bean that 
you buy is more expensive than the last by ten Rupees.  For example the first 
one costs ten however the second one costs twenty, the third costs thirty and 
the tenth costs one hundred (you can only purchase ten.)  If you wish to buy 
all of them now it's going to cost you a total of 550 Rupees so you would be 
making a few trips back and forth to fill your adult wallet.  Don't worry too 
much about it right now, I'll be sure to cover it soon enough.

Before you came across the fat guy eating the beans you probably noticed a 
Cucco walking around among all those rocks.  Go back and pick up the Cucco 
above your head.  Remember that while holding a Cucco you can jump further 
than you normally can, this will come in handy very soon.  With the Cucco in 
hand jump across the river.  Make your way east until you reach that little 
waterfall and then jump across to the north.  While you are going up this hill 
you should be able to see the Piece of Heart up on the platform from here, 
that's the one we're going for.  At the top of the hill keep going straight 
and jump across the river to reach the north side.  Now turn and keep going 
east, you should pass by a log of some sort on your right, this means you're 
going in the correct direction.  When you reach the ledge, throw your Cucco up 
to the higher part and climb up yourself, grab the Cucco again when you get up 
there.  Hop down into the water beside you, it's only knee deep, then throw 
your Cucco up to the next ledge and climb up yourself.  From here go as far 
south as you can without jumping, then turn and face southwest.  You should 
see a small platform of land way down there, and a long ladder coming up from 
it.  With Cucco above your head, glide down to that stretch of land.  Throw 
your Cucco in the river and ignore the consequences while you begin to ascend.

When you reach the top turn around and jump across to the platform to the 
south, there is a hole in this platform which you can drop down.  At the 
bottom open the chest to receive a RED RUPEE.  Leave this little cave and jump 
back across again.  Here you will find a large boulder surrounded by small 
rocks.  If you place a bomb beside this boulder and blow it up, it will reveal 
the way to a Fairy Fountain in case you need to restore your health or 
anything like that.  Leave the fountain when you're done, now it is indeed 
time to get that heart piece.  Grab hold of the Cucco which just happens to be 
wandering around here and go over to the east side.  Here there is a very thin 
bridge leading north to the other platform, face north and press the L button 
to centre your camera, walk across this extremely slowly with the Cucco above 
your head.  Now from here you can jump and glide down to get the PIECE OF 
HEART [011].  Since the Cucco you had likely just dropped off and committed 
suicide, head back to the beginning of the river and grab the original one.

Take this Cucco along the same way you did with the other one.  Everything is 
the same until that point where you face southwest and jump way down to the 
platform with the ladder on it.  Don't do that this time, instead turn around 
and throw your Cucco across the water onto the hill then go across and get it.  
Continue along the path, across the bridge and you will reach an area with all 
sorts of criss crossing land bridges.  Stay on these land bridges with Cucco 
in hand and make your way to the waterfall, or just in front of it.  You 
should find a kind of grey platform in front of it, ignore that and keep going 
up on that path to the highest part you can possibly reach.  When you turn 
around and face west you will see another heart piece, jump down with the 
Cucco so that you can glide and grab hold of the PIECE OF HEART [012].

There is no longer any reason to keep that Cucco with you.  If it's not night 
time right now then play the Sun's Song so that it becomes night time, then 
drop down in front of the waterfall, the water isn't very deep here so you can 
run around.  On the kind of northwest part of this little land bridge area, 
directly below where you collected the Piece of Heart there is a ladder and on 
that ladder is an enemy.  Defeat this enemy and pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA 
[020].  Now that you have twenty tokens there is something to do, and a couple 
other non related things to do as well.  Let's start heading back.

On your way back through Zora's River, remember that part where I mentioned a 
log on your right that you passed, around the middle of the area?  Head back 
there and climb up onto the log, there is a small space where you can stand 
and you get the message saying the frogs are looking at you.  Stand on this 
place and pull out your Fairy Ocarina.  Play Sari's Song and the frogs will 
reward you with a PURPLE RUPEE.  Play Zelda's Lullaby to get a PURPLE RUPEE, 
then the Sun's Song to get a PURPLE RUPEE and finally Epona's Song to get a 
PURPLE RUPEE.  These frogs really like their music and you're the one 
benefiting from it.  Now head back to the very beginning of Zora's River and 
speak to the fat guy, use all the money you just got from those frogs to 
purchase Magic Beans.  Now leave Zora's River and return to Hyrule Field.

Make your way west through the river and back to the stairs leading up to 
Kakariko Village.  When you get into the village make a right and go up the 
stairs to the House of Skulltula.  There should be another guy here with a 
broken curse since you have collected twenty Skulltulas.  He gives you the 
STONE OF AGONY, a special item which makes your controller rumble when a 
special secret is nearby.  Basically he just gave you a Spider-sense.  Anyway 
that's about all there is to do in this village.  The next thing is just a 
recommendation.  Return to Hyrule Castle Town, right at the entrance and go 
through the door to that room with all the pots.  Now that you have the charge 
up spin attack, you can destroy all the pots in this room easily and collect 
over a hundred Rupees in barely a minute.  Fill up your Adult Wallet to the 
full 200 Rupee capacity and return to Zora's River.  Spend the rest of it on 
any more Magic Beans you can afford, you're going to have to buy them all 
eventually anyway and you might as well get most of it out of the way, don't 
worry if you can't afford all ten, just buy as many as possible.  

Now it's time to head for Zora's Domain.  You've been there before once 
already but it's time to go back again.  Return to the large waterfall in the 
area with all those natural criss-crossing bridges.  When you stand in front 
of the waterfall you will find a special grey square, whip out your Fairy 
Ocarina on this particular spot and play the song which connects you to the 
royal family, Zelda's Lullaby (left, up, right, left, up, right.)  Jump across 
the gap and take the door which leads right into Zora's Domain.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ___   
      (,   ) 
          /  
        _/_  
     )   /   
+-- (__ / ORA'S DOMAIN --------------------------------------------- 01.19 --+

When you first enter this area, follow the path along until you reach a large 
set of stairs.  At the top there is a big, gross, thing.  Ignore it for now 
and make a left turn, follow the path through the tunnel leading west and 
speak to the Zora at the end.  You will have the option of playing the Diving 
Game for 20 Rupees.  The Diving Game is one of the easiest minigames ever, 
your goal is to jump down into the water and collect all five of the Blue 
Rupees.  Dive into the water from the waterfall above and then hold down the 
L/Z trigger button.  This should give you a nice view from above which will 
allow you to see your shadow.  Position yourself close to one of the five Blue 
Rupees and hold the A button to dive down.  At the bottom, hold the analog 
stick in whatever the direction the Rupee is in, in the event you are not 
directly over top of it.  You should be able to collect them all for sure.  

After successfully collecting all five Blue Rupees, head back up to where you 
started the game and speak to the Zora there once again.  The Zora will reward 
you with a SILVER SCALE which allows you to dive for six seconds rather than a 
mere three seconds, this means of course that you can go deeper as well.  
After this you can still play the game as much as you like and make money off 
of it.  More money in fact, now the Zora will throw Red Rupees in the water 
along with the blue ones.  Some green ones two, but you'll still make a lot.

Before we proceed with the game there is still one more thing to do.  Remember 
that big ugly thing at the top of the stairs?  Well that's the King of the 
Zora, we still don't want to speak to it, but we do want to go up to that 
room.  Notice that on your right there is a lit torch, and one that is not lit 
at the bottom of the stairs.  You know what to do, equip one of your many Deku 
Sticks and set it on fire.  Bring it down the stairs and use it to light the 
torch at the bottom.  Now put it away and bring it out again, so it doesn't 
burn out.  Light another Deku Stick on fire using the torch at the bottom of 
the stairs and then run down the path.  Make a left at the first chance you 
get and another left, there is a torch here.  This torch is not a permanent 
one, it will burn out.  Quickly de-equip and then re-equip a Deku Stick and 
light it again right here.  Run across the shallow water and light the torch 
beside that circle of rocks, then make a left and stay very close to the wall 
as you make your way toward the waterfall so you don't have to go underwater.  
Behind the waterfall are two final torches and when these are lit, a large 
treasure chest will appear.  If it doesn't then it means one of the previous 
ones probably already burnt out.  Open the chest to get PIECE OF HEART [013].

Make your way back up to the king of the Zora and speak with him.  You can 
speak to him by pressing the A button at the top of that little staircase.  It 
seems that someone has gone missing, how very unfortunate.  Here's what we 
want to do, make a left and go through the tunnel that leads you to the Diving 
Game.  Jump off the edge of the waterfall and dive down into the water.  
Remember by now you should have acquired the Silver Scale.  Swim over to where 
the underwater door can be seen and dive down.  Head through to Lake Hylia.
______________________________________________________________________________


        _    
    ___/__)  
   (, /      
     /       
+-- (_____ AKE HYLIA ----------------------------------------------- 01.20 --+

When you reach Lake Hylia you should notice something on the bottom of the 
lake directly in front of you at the start.  Swim down and pick it up to 
acquire the LETTER.  Before you bring it back to the King in Zora's Domain 
there is some stuff to do here first.  Make your way to the east side of the 
lake and you should come across a door.  This is the Fishing Pond.

Speak to the guy at the counter and tell him you wish to go fishing, it only 
costs 20 Rupees.  Here's how to play.  Walk up to the water and press the L/Z 
button to look at a fish, then you can cast your line with the B button and 
press the A button to break the line.  Wiggle the lure in with the analog 
stick and reel it in using the A and R buttons.  Press A and down to set the 
hook when you feel a hit.  That's about all there is to it.

The pond is filled to the brim with fish so if you want to catch anything big 
you're going to have to prepare yourself in advance.  Look around the pond and 
even swim around the pond trying to find the biggest fish you can.  The one 
you are looking for is almost always situated around the middle, in a tiny 
little area around one of those logs, he's bigger than everything else so he 
should stand out.  Launch your line from a distance and try to catch this 
particular fish, really he's the only one you're going to be interested in.  
Once you eventually catch this fish go back and talk to the guy at the 
counter, as a reward for the ten pound fish you get PIECE OF HEART [014].

Leave the Fishing Pond and swim across to the north shore, there you will find 
two scarecrows.  It's time to learn the Scarecrow Song, sort of.  Stand in 
front of the lower scarecrow and play your Ocarina.  Any tune consisting of 
eight notes that you play, the scarecrow will memorize and you have to 
memorize it as well.  Remember the tune you play for the scarecrow as you will 
need it later.  If you ever want to change the tune, simply stand in front of 
the scarecrow and play it again, it will overwrite the first one.

Take a look at your map and notice that big thing which goes out way over the 
water, this is a bridge and you are now going to want to take that bridge.  
Head over to the building where it starts and begin to cross the bridge.  
Ignore the owl in the middle as you continue to cross the other bridge to the 
southeast.  When you reach the tree, look east to see a small island with two 
pillars on it.  Swim over to this island and play the Sun's Song if it isn't 
nighttime already right now.  During the night a Skulltula appears on one of 
these pillars, eliminate it and then grab the GOLD SKULLTULA [021].  Now head 
to the north end of Lake Hylia and take the exit to Hyrule Field.  There is a 
ladder here on your left, take it up and then jump down on the other side in 
the field.  Make your way to the far west side and enter Gerudo Valley.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ERUDO VALLEY ---------------------------------------- 01.21 --+

This is a very interesting place, however you won't be able to find out much 
about it's charm since you are not welcome here at all.  Period.  Just follow 
what I say and you'll be able to get away with the best payload.  Right at the 
very beginning take a look on your right to see some rocks on the ground, 
under one of the rocks are some little bugs.  After lifting the rock, equip 
your bottle and use it to capture one of these bugs.  Now head across the 
bridge just past this area and notice the Cucco here.  You can't progress any 
further normally so don't even try.  Grab hold of the Cucco and run over to 
the southwest cliff.  When you look across the gap to the southwest just down 
a little bit you should see a tiny ledge with a single box, this is your 
destination.  With Cucco in hand, jump off the cliff and glide down toward 
that ledge.  When you land, throw the Cucco against the wall in an effort to 
try and keep it here, then roll into the box and grab the PIECE OF HEART 
[015].  Now pick up your Cucco again assuming it is still here.

The next thing you are going to want to do is jump off the ledge and glide 
down toward the platform below.  Way, way down at the very bottom there is a 
long ledge and on this ledge you will find a person, a cow and a hole.  Break 
the crate and get the Rupee or whatever falls out of it, now equip your bottle 
and stand in front of the hole.  Release the little bugs on top of the hole 
and they will crawl into it, after that a Skulltula will jump out.  Defeat 
this enemy and pick up the GOLD SKULLTULA [022].  After doing this you had may 
as well plant one of those Magic Bean seeds you have into the hole here.  
Finally what you want to do while still on this ledge is run all the way to 
the north end and jump into the water.  Swim behind the waterfall to the north 
and in the middle you will come across a ladder leading up.  Basically all 
this does is lead up to a PIECE OF HEART [035] in a small area.  

Now that you have all this stuff out of the way it's time to proceed with the 
main game once again, here's how to get where you want to go.  From here jump 
back into the river at the bottom of the Gerudo Valley and follow the current 
until it eventually takes you to Lake Hylia.  When you arrive in Lake Hylia 
swim east until you reach that underwater door that takes you back to Zora's 
Domain.  Make your way up to where the king is sitting.  Stand on the platform 
in front of him and use the Letter in the bottle.  After this is said and 
done, the king will move, revealing the path to the next dungeon and you will 
also receive the EMPTY BOTTLE which had the Letter inside.  On the right side 
of the room take the sloping path up and enter ate area beyond the domain.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ___   
      (,   ) 
          /  
        _/_  
     )   /   
+-- (__ / ORA'S FOUNTAIN ------------------------------------------- 01.22 --+

Here of course is the large fish monster which will be the location of our 
next adventure, but not quite yet.  As you enter the fountain make a sharp 
right and get into the water south of the enormous monster.  At the very 
southeast corner you will come across an area of land.  Notice there is a 
conspicuous tree on this bit of land, I think we all know what to do in the 
event of finding a conspicuous tree.  Run up to the tree and roll into it, 
this should knock down yet another Skulltula which you can kill and grab the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [023].  Also make note of the large blue-ish coloured rock 
here, when you place a bomb beside this rock the entire wall will explode next 
to it!  It turns out this is the entrance to another Great Fairy's Fountain.  
Head inside and stand on the Triforce emblem, you need to whip out your Fairy 
Ocarina and play Zelda's Lullaby.  What you learn here for the Great Fairy is 
a technique called FARORE'S WIND.  This technique can be sued to create and 
warp to warp points in the game's many dungeons.  Now leave the fountain.

You may notice that when you approach the mouth of the giant beast here, 
nothing really happens of any interest.  Don't fret, head back to Zora's River 
for a moment.  Jump off the great waterfall beside the king's room and when 
you land in the water.  Make your way around to the area of water that is 
swallow enough to run in and equip one of your bottles.  Locate a bunch of 
fish and run up to them, then use the Empty Bottle to catch one of the fish.  
Now it's time to head back up to the king's room and return to Zora's 
Fountain.  Remember on your way back that just beside this shallow water is 
the Zora Shop if you're interested, I believe I may have neglected to mention 
it earlier but there isn't much of interest there anyway.  So back at Zora's 
Fountain again, approach the giant fish head and use the Fish you caught.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____
        (, /
          /
      ___/__
+-- (__ /    NSIDE JABU-JABU'S BELLY ------------------------------- 01.23 --+

Let's take a moment to see what we have here.  First of all we are in the 
belly of a giant, thing.  Second of all the enemies here aren't exactly what 
most would call normal.  The bubbles that are floating around, those can be 
killed using your sword.  The Octoroks on the other hand (funnel mouthed 
things that pop out of the water) cannot be killed with your sword, similar to 
the Deku Scrubs.  Remember that you should still have your Deku Shield, equip 
that now and use it to deflect the attacks of the Octoroks back at them and 
kill them.  Now that the enemies are out of the way we can focus on the room 
itself.  You may notice that you can't actually get through the door leading 
into the next area.  What you have to do is walk back and equip your Fairy 
Slingshot.  Use it to fire up at that little thing dangling down from the roof 
of his mouth and the path will open.  You irritated his throat I guess.

The first thing to note in this particular room is that you cannot kill the 
weird jellyfish things, in fact you will be hurt yourself in the process if 
you try.  Just leave them be for the moment.  If for some reason you really 
want it dead, then bombs will get the job done.  Make your way across and head 
through the door at the end.  Enter this room and watch the scene, 
unfortunately it seems your quest into the best is not quite over yet.  After 
the scene you will automatically be placed directly beside some kind of hole 
in the ground, make sure this is the one you drop down.  Speak to the princess 
twice and she curls up into a ball to be carried.  Press the A button to lift 
her up and head through the door just behind you.  There are many bubbles in 
this room, and the great thing is that stupid princess can be used as a 
weapon.  An ineffective one, but a weapon nonetheless.  Head through the hall 
until you come to the edge of the ledge.  When you drop down, you can throw 
the princess up to the other side but you can't actually get up there 
yourself.  Don't worry too much about it, just throw her and then take a look 
up on the wall with the vine.  There is a Skulltula on this wall, higher up.  
Equip your Fairy Slingshot and fire at it until the enemy is dead.  Now jump 
onto the yellow button in the middle and this will raise the water level up.  
Use this to get high enough to reach the vine, and climb up to acquire GOLD 
SKULLTULA [024].  Jump on the button again and the water level will rise up 
high enough for you to reach the ledge that you threw the princess up to.  In 
the middle of the hallway just past here, there is a button on the ceiling.  
No need to equip your Fairy Slingshot, just hurl the princess up there to hit 
it and open the door at the end of the hall.  Proceed to the next room.

Set the princess down on the ground and use your Deku Shield to deflect the 
Octorok's attacks back at him and kill him.  Now lift the princess again and 
just stand here until the lift comes down from above.  Get on it when it stops 
in front of you.  You'll probably notice the familiar setting when you get 
off.  Head through the door leading into the room with all the holes but don't 
drop down any of them.  Instead make your way across through the door into the 
next room.  At the very entrance here there are some extremely dangerous 
enemies.  Long creatures which pop out of the ground and chase after you.  The 
normal sword is ineffective but I find that if you use the charge up Spin 
Attack, to the point where it goes orange, it will kill them in one hit, and 
maybe more than one of them at a time.  Grab the princess and make a right 
turn at the fork.  With her still over your head, jump onto the switch at the 
end and the door will open in front of you.  Take it to the next area.

This room can be pretty dangerous, you have to fight four of those aquatic 
monsters simultaneously and your sword really isn't effective against them at 
all.  There are two methods of dealing with them that work that I know of: 
first of all, if they are floating in the air then stand back and fire at them 
with your Fairy Slingshot.  Doing this requires you to hit them two times.  If 
they are swimming underground then stand back and throw bombs at them, this 
will kill them in a single hit.  Once all four of the enemies have been 
dispatched, a large chest appears at the centre of the room.  Open this chest 
to acquire the BOOMERANG.  Equip it on one of your C buttons, it's going to be 
an effective weapon in this dungeon, and then leave the room with Ruto.

Make your way across to the exact opposite side of this area, throw Ruto at 
the enemies here to defeat them easily or perhaps try out your new Boomerang, 
or both.  On the opposite side there is a blue button which opens the door 
into the next room.  If you jump off this button then the door will close 
again.  The solution is as follows: hold Ruto over your head and jump onto the 
button.  Before getting off of it, put her down and then get off, it will stay 
down.  Now proceed into the next room.  This enemy isn't too difficult, start 
off by hold the L/Z button to target it and throw the Boomerang for a hit.  
Now I think you have to get close before it will drop down again, and if you 
move back it will hide.  The solution is to get close and use L/Z targeting 
immediately right when it is coming down to hit it, before it hits you.  After 
four hits the enemy is defeated and another large chest appears in this room.  
Open the treasure chest to acquire the DUNGEON MAP for this dungeon.

Back outside once again, pick up Princess Ruto and be on your way.  Make a 
left at the fork where you had her set down on the button, Navi will stop you 
to tell you something blatantly obvious, and then you are free to enter the 
room at the end of this hall.  You have forty seconds to defeat all of the 
bubbles in this room, you can throw the princess at them, you can throw your 
Boomerang at them, you can even just run into them like an idiot but I find 
the most effective method of dealing with them is to just use the good old 
fashioned sword.  Once they have all been defeated within the time limit, a 
large chest will appear in the middle of the room containing the COMPASS for 
the dungeon.  Now return yet again to the previous area you came from.

Head back in the direction of the room where you got the Boomerang, but make a 
left at the fork this time when you reach the yellow button.  There is another 
dangling body part here that you need to deal with using your Boomerang.  No 
reward unfortunately this time.  Leave the room and head back to the very 
middle of this hall where most of the worms are situated.  You can finally go 
north now in the centre, up into the next room.  There is another one of those 
dangling things but this time you will also find a number of jellyfish 
enemies.  I would recommend making your way around the edge of the room, 
defeating the smaller enemies first and that way you won't risk accidentally 
targeting them while fighting the larger enemy.  After they are defeated you 
will hear a noise.  That's your cue to leave and head south to the hole room.

You may notice things are a little bit different here and they are.  The hole 
on the far west side of this room beside the wall is no longer blocked, so 
that's the one you want to drop down.  You should land on a higher ledge above 
the room below, right beside two Skulltulas.  If you don't land there, just 
leave the princess behind and climb up the vine.  You'll find her again at the 
top and you can just try again.  Equip your Boomerang and aim it at the enemy 
closest to you on the wall.  After it has been defeated you can throw the 
Boomerang and it will retrieve the GOLD SKULLTULA [025] for you.  You can see 
why this weapon is going to be handy.  Aim right and there is another 
Skulltula further away, shoot it with the Boomerang and use it to collect the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [026].  Pick up the princess and head through the west door.

Listen to what Ruto says and throw her up onto the platform where the 
Spiritual Stone is.  After a scene, and then when you try to move something 
happens.  No this is not the boss of the dungeon but in fact just a miniboss.

Despite the use of the word "mini" this boss really isn't so mini at all, in 
fact it might just be me but I actually found him to be quite difficult.  The 
weapon of choice for this battle is of course going to be the Boomerang so 
make sure it is equipped.  What happens here is the Giant Octorok circles 
around the middle and chases after you.  If he hits you, you're going to lose 
half a heart piece and if the spikes along the middle thing hit you, you're 
going to lose a quarter heart piece.  When you hit the miniboss with your 
Boomerang, he is stunned for a moment but his only weakspot it on the back so 
you have to run around the circle and try to get his back before he starts up 
again.  For this reason you have to stun him from a fairly decent distance in 
order to have time to get around and stun him again while he is facing the 
other direction.  I find this doesn't work too well so here are two other ways 
of hitting him that I used.  When he comes at you throw the Boomerang to stun 
and then walk backward, but not around the circle.  If you're far enough back 
he may choose to change directions and then you have just enough time to run 
up and hit him with the Boomerang before he takes off.  Also this may not 
sound too appealing, but I find it works better than anything else.  If you 
happen to get hit by the boss, that is your very best chance to strike back.  
He will knock you down and then always turn around.  Get up quickly and throw 
the Boomerang while he is facing the other direction.  The green spot will 
open up and you can slash it with your sword.  The one tip I can give you to 
help out the most is to spend almost the entire battle locked onto the boss, 
don't let him out of your site, throwing the Boomerang without a lock on isn't 
nearly accurate enough and you don't always have time to lock on when he's 
coming at you so stay locked on all the time.  He takes about three hits.

After defeating the "miniboss," ride up to the next floor and defeat the 
jellyfish, now enter the next room.  The first thing you want to do is jump 
down into the water, but stay away from those crazy seizure platforms.  Defeat 
the Octorok using either your Boomerang or by deflecting its attacks with the 
Deku Shield.  Climb back up and throw your Boomerang at the two platforms to 
calm them down and jump across.  Jump across to the second platform in the 
next room and it will descend down a floor.  You are once again in a familiar 
room.  Grab one of the two boxes on the side here and them come back over.  
You'll notice a button on the floor here, jump on it while carrying a box and 
then put the box down on the button to hold it down.  Proceed into the next 
room.  Eliminate all the jellyfish enemies here and then find the Skulltula 
crawling around on the wall here and kill it with your Boomerang.  Climb the 
wall to get GOLD SKULLTULA [027].  Keep climbing toward the top and you will 
see one of those things on the roof that opens the doors.  The problem is that 
there is a  glass panel in your way that means you can hit it straight on with 
the Fairy Slingshot, you have to use the Boomerang.  The problem is that even 
when you're using the Boomerang correctly, it still misses a lot of the time.  
Stand all the way back and target with L/Z and throw.  It should go around the 
glass but there's no guarantee it will hit the button.  If you're really 
having trouble try walking right up to the glass and to the left side without 
falling off the edge, being closer will help your accuracy.  When the button 
is hit and the door is open, head through the north door to the boss.

There's all sorts of crazy stuff going on here.  You are battling Barinade, 
the large thing in the middle of the room going crazy with electricity.  The 
first thing to note is that if you're low on health, all of the pots around 
the room each contain a Recovery Heart which should be enough to get you back 
to full from being almost dead.  Secondly make sure you keep running during 
all this because the boss is going to be firing blasts of electricity in your 
direction the whole time.  It is very important to note the following: you can 
move while throwing the Boomerang.  This is important because you're going to 
have to throw the Boomerang, but you're also going to have to be dodging as 
well.  Use the L/Z button to lock onto the three things that attach the boss 
to the ceiling and hit all three of them individually with the Boomerang.  At 
this point he creates a bunch of enemies and attaches to them via electricity.  
They spin around and around, but fortunately they don't do too much damage 
when they hit you.  Aim for the main body part and throw your Boomerang to 
stun it, if it turns blue then you know it's stunned.  Slash it as much as 
possible with your sword until you can't hurt it anymore.  Now what it does is 
take all the enemies close to it and begin spinning around rapidly.  While it 
is spinning rapidly it is very difficult to hit the boss with the Boomerang 
(though it can be done.)  It's easier if you wait.  Eventually the boss will 
disconnect its electricity link to the enemies and then you can hit the boss 
core itself with the Boomerang easier.  When you do hit the boss you still 
can't strike with your sword, instead it gives you the opportunity to target 
the enemies individually and throw you Boomerang at them, it will kill them in 
one hit.  After all those little enemies are defeated then you can stun the 
boss and keep attacking with your sword.  The enemies never respawn so you are 
free to keep stunning and attacking until the boss eventually dies.  Grab the 
HEART CONTAINER on the ground here and then step into the warp portal.  It 
will take your back to Zora's Domain, say that you wish to acquire the 
Spiritual Stone and you will be given ZORA'S SAPPHIRE.  According to the 
game's main plot arc, your next destination is going to be Zelda's Castle, 
however at this point as you may know there will soon be a very significant 
event taking place and with the Boomerang in hand there are many things to do 
before going on with the game.  Should you choose to partake in these events, 
and you most certainly should, then be sure to read the next section.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ADVENTURE! ------------------------------------- 01.05 --+

There are just so many Gold Skulltulas you can collect at this point it's 
crazy, let's get started on this shall we.  Here's what to do, save your game 
and reset.  We're going to start right from the beginning, in Kokiri Forest.  
Leave your house and head for The Lost Woods.  When you enter, make a right 
and then a left.  There should be a hole in the ground here, drop down and 
fill all three of your Empty Bottles with bugs, you can find bugs if you slash 
the grass down here beside the chest.  Leave the hole and go east, then make a 
left, head straight, go left and then go right to reach the Sacred Meadow.  
When you first enter the meadow you will feel the controller shake assuming 
you have the Stone of Agony equipped.  Place a bomb in the centre of this 
first area to reveal a hole in the ground and drop down.  There are two wolf 
enemies which you have to kill here and after they have been defeated a small 
treasure chest appears containing a PURPLE RUPEE.  Leave this little cave.

Head back to the very entrance of the Lost Woods, in fact go back to Kokiri 
Forest entirely and just enter the woods.  Make a left at the very beginning 
and then in this next large area make a left again and head south.  In front 
of you should be a hole in the ground to plant something in.  Before planting 
anything what you want to do is stand in front of it and use the Empty Bottle 
with a bug in it, the bugs will dig down into the hole and a Gold Skulltula 
will pop out, defeat this enemy to acquire GOLD SKULLTULA [028].  Equip one of 
your Magic Beans and use it on the hole to place a bean sprout into the hole 
and have it ready to grow.  There's one more thing to do in here.  From the 
entrance of the Lost Woods make a right and then make a left.  Make a right 
here and you should be at that small little pond.  Make a left and then make 
another left to reach an area with two Deku Scrubs.  Beside one of them you 
will find another hole in the ground, drop some bugs into it and GOLD 
SKULLTULA [029] will pop out of the ground.  After doing this plant one of 
your Magic Beans in the hole and be on your way.  That is the last thing there 
is to do in The Lost Woods so head back toward Kokiri Forest.

In Kokiri Forest again make your way over to the orange roofed shop, beside 
the shop you will find a hole in the ground.  Use one of your Empty Bottles 
with a bug in it to make a GOLD SKULLTULA [030] pop out of the hole.  After 
doing this plant another one of your Magic Beans and then make your way east 
and enter the Deku Tree area.  Head inside the Deku Tree and drop down the 
hold right at the very beginning.  Make your way to the upper left room on B1 
here, you will find a special wall which must be bombed here that leads 
northwest.  Defeat the normal Skulltula here and then look around on the walls 
to find the golden one.  You have to use your Boomerang to defeat it and 
acquire the GOLD SKULLTULA [031].  That's the only thing you're looking to 
find here, head back to the main room on B1 and climb up the vine to reach the 
exit.  Make your way through the area and out Kokiri Forest to Hyrule Field.

There are a number of things to find on Hyrule Field so let's begin by heading 
southwest toward Lake Hylia.  On your way there you will likely pass through a 
number of trees with many of those spinning flower enemies, and in the middle 
of these trees is a rock.  Blow it up and go down to find a RED RUPEE in the 
small chest.  Leave this hole and continue your trek southwest toward Lake 
Hylia.  You should come across an area which is fence in and a large concrete 
wall beside it.  On the west side of this wall kind of hidden from view is a 
ladder, ascend up the ladder and jump down into the middle of that fenced 
area.  Your controller should be rumbling at this point, that's a good sign.  
Place a bomb here in the middle and drop down the hole that appears.  Defeat 
the Deku Scrub using your Boomerang and then speak to it.  The scrub will sell 
you the PIECE OF HEART [016] for the astounding price of only ten Rupees, 
you'd be a fool to pass this up.  Leave this hole and hop the white fence.  
Technically I guess you could have done that before but... that's no fun.

Your next destination is going to be the area near Gerudo Valley,  You don't 
actually want to enter the Gerudo Valley itself, but rather take a look for an 
area with a circle of stones just northeast of the entrance to Gerudo Valley 
on the Hyrule Field.  When you stand in the middle of this circle your 
controller will begin to rumble, this is a good sign of course.  Take out a 
bomb and place it in the middle of this circle to blow a hole in the ground.  
Drop down the hole and note three spider webs.  Behind one of them you will 
see a Gold Skulltula and some pots and grass and stuff, this is the one you 
want.  Equip Din's Fire on one of your C Buttons and use it while standing 
beside the spider web.  Under one of these pots beside the cow, or under the 
grass or something some more bugs appear, since you might be in need of a bug 
refill for your Empty Bottles, be sure to catch them, three of them.  Aim up 
at the web in the corner and use your Boomerang to acquire the GOLD SKULLTULA 
[032].  Now leave the cave and head next for the Hyrule Castle.

You won't actually be going into the castle, just approach it.  When you do 
there should be some kind of cutscene which occurs when you get near the 
drawbridge.  Watch the scene and then jump into the water under the 
drawbridge.  Dive under the pick up the OCARINA OF TIME.  She will also teach 
you the SONG OF TIME as well.  With this little plot side done, head east.

What you have really been looking for this whole time is the tree, across the 
bridge leading toward Kakariko village, but directly beside the east wall to 
the castle on your left.  Place a bomb beside the tree to blast a hole in the 
ground, then drop down through that hole.  At the bottom there is a normal 
Skulltula for you to defeat and then you can use your Boomerang to take out 
the GOLD SKULLTULA [033] on the wall here.  Now that we're close to it, why 
don't you head into Kakariko Village for a few more things to do.

The first thing you should do is make your way to the House of Skulltula, you 
should know where it is by now.  Enter the house and speak with the person 
here who has been recently freed assuming you have more than thirty Gold 
Skulltulas.  The person will give you a GIANT'S WALLET which is a special new 
wallet that allows you to hold a maximum of up to five hundred Rupees!  That's 
quite a lot.  Now we are going to have to fill the wallet, and it's easier 
than you think.  Leave this house and head for the middle of town, where the 
tree stands tall on its own.  Now head a bit east from the three until you 
seem to be in the exactly middle of everything and the controller should be 
rumbling, place a bomb here to blast open a hole and then drop down through 
it.  There are two zombies down here in this area that you are going to have 
to defeat, then once you do a small chest will appear.  Open the small chest 
to get a HUGE RUPEE which is worth a whopping two hundred Rupees, like I said 
it's going to be easier to fill that wallet than you thought.  

Back outside in the main part of town again, now what you want to do is make 
your way to the southeast corner where the entrance to the graveyard is, but 
instead of going into the graveyard, climb the stairs beside it.  There is a 
door at the top of the stairs for you to go through.  There is some form of 
spinning platform here, don't really worry about that for now.  Look up a 
little and you should see a heart piece in this room.  What you need to do is 
climb onto one of those four grey coloured posts that surround the spinning 
thing in the middle and throw your Boomerang to acquire this PIECE OF HEART 
[017].  When this is out of the way, leave here and head for the Graveyard.

You must go to the graveyard at night for this, so play the Sun's Song if 
need be.  Inside the graveyard head for the northwest corner and there you 
will find a hole in the ground.  Empty one of your bug jars onto the hole and 
then a GOLD SKULLTULA [034] will pop out for you to defeat.  After getting the 
token be sure to plant one of your Magic Beans in this hole.  Head over to the 
southeast side of the graveyard and look up on the wall, there you should see 
a GOLD SKULLTULA [035] for you to kill with your Boomerang, same deal for 
grabbing the token.  That about does it for the village here, now it's time to 
head back to Hyrule Field and then make your way up Death Mountain Trail.

Just up the trial a little bit, in front of the Dodongo's Cavern you will come 
across a hole in the ground.  Use one of your bottles that have bugs in them 
and put them in the hole to make GOLD SKULLTULA [036] appear.  Head into the 
Dodongo's Cavern itself and jump onto that pillar on the right side that goes 
really high, all the way up to the second floor.  Head into the room to the 
east and drop down so you can eventually make your way to the southwest.  
Eventually you should come to that room with the enormous staircase, the one 
where you lowered it down by exploding all the bombs.  Well now it's still 
raised and you want to keep it that way which is why you started on the second 
floor.  Head down beside the staircase on the left side and there should be a 
vine you can climb up.  At the top is a small alcove where the GOLD SKULLTULA 
[037] is crawling around on the wall, use your Boomerang to get it.  There's 
nothing else to do here so leave the dungeon and head for Lon Lon Ranch.

You must enter the Lon Lon Ranch during the night in order to do this.  When 
you do, take a few steps forward and make sure you have the Boomerang 
equipped.  Take a look up on your left at the window on the second floor, you 
should see a Skulltula crawling around on there.  Aim your Boomerang up at the 
window and throw it to defeat the enemy and acquire GOLD SKULLTULA [038].  
Next you will want to make your way down to the southwest corner of the ranch.  
Run along the outer wall and keep your eye on it, around the southwest corner 
like I said you will come across the GOLD SKULLTULA [039].  Well you're done 
here, at least for now.  Leave the Lon Lon Ranch and head to Hyrule Field.

While you're in the area, take a minor detour and head west back to the Gerudo 
Valley, except this time actually enter it.  Be sure that you enter the valley 
during the night.  As you cross the bridge at the entrance take a look on your 
right and crawling above the waterfall here is indeed yet another Skulltula.  
You must defeat this enemy using your Boomerang and then use it again to 
retrieve the GOLD SKULLTULA [040].  Your next destination is Lake Hylia.

When you arrive there you are going to want to make sure it is night time when 
you enter Lake Hylia.  Head over to the building and begin to cross the bridge 
but when you step onto the bridge, turn around immediately and take a look on 
the back of the house, if it's night time then you should see a GOLD SKULLTULA 
[041] on there.  Eliminate it and grab the token using your Boomerang.  That's 
not all either, on the other side of the house you will find another one of 
those beanstalk holes.  Take one of your bug jars and empty it onto the hole 
to make a Skulltula pop out.  Defeat the GOLD SKULLTULA [042].  After doing 
that be sure to plant one of your Magic Beans in the hole there.  Swim down 
under the water and head through the hole that leads to Zora's Domain.  Follow 
the path to Zora's Fountain.  Make a right when you reach Zora's Fountain and 
climb up onto the huge log.  If it's not night time then use the Sun's Song to 
make it night.  During the night you will see a GOLD SKULLTULA [043] on the 
wall.  That just about covers everything there is to do, almost.

The last two things are basically just recommendations.  First of all I would 
recommend now that you have a decent about of money, you head back to Zora's 
River and buy all the rest of the Magic Beans the guy will sell you if you 
haven't done that already.  If you want to you can head over to Kakariko 
Village and then to the House of Skulltula.  For acquiring forty Skulltulas 
you are rewarded with the BOMBCHU.  That is in fact, the crappiest reward ever 
given that you probably already won some from the Bombchu Bowling Alley.  

And finally before we proceed with the game, we have, a sidequest!  Yes the 
mask sidequest is available to do at this point and if you're following along 
with the guide, we're going to do it.  As you probably know the mask shop is 
located in Hyrule Market so head there now, assuming you're coming from 
Kakariko Village it won't really be much of a trip.  Enter the Happy Mask Shop 
and speak to the owner, choose to borrow the KEATON MASK.  It is now your job 
to find a customer who will buy this mask from you, and it's not going to be 
too difficult.  Just leave Hyrule Castle Town and make your way back to 
Kakariko Village.  Set the mask to one of your C Buttons and then equip it, 
after doing that speak with the soldier guarding the north exit and he will pay 
you 15 Rupees for the mask, that's a pathetic five Rupee profit!

Head back to the Happy Mask Shop in the Market once again and this time borrow 
the SKULL MASK.  Your destination now is going to be Kokiri Forest.  Head from 
there up into The Lost Woods.  Make a right at the beginning, then a left, 
then a right, then a left and then a left.  This is the area with the dead 
tree, and two Deku Scrubs.  At the back left corner, not quite in the corner 
but in that general area, there is a hole in the ground hidden underneath the 
grass.  Run around until you drop down it.  At the bottom take a few steps 
forward so that you are overlooking all the Deku Scrubs under the ground and 
equip the Skull Mask.  They will quickly pop out of the ground and start 
running around.  Speak with the Deku Scrub on your right in order to get the 
next DEKU STICK UPGRADE.  Now head back to the entrance of The Lost Woods and 
make a left turn.  Here you will find the Skull Kid up on the pillar.  Jump 
onto the small stump here and press the A button while wearing the mask, Skull 
Kid will give you ten Rupees for it.  Oh well, no money this time.  

Return to Hyrule Market and the Happy Mask Shop again, pay back the shop owner 
the 20 Rupees for a loss of 10 Rupees.  No matter, now you can borrow the 
SPOOKY MASK from him, be sure to do that.  Your destination with this mask is 
going to be the same as the first one, Kakariko Village once again.  Make sure 
you enter the village during the way.  Head for the Graveyard and you will 
find some kind walking around here, equip the Spooky Mask and speak to him, 
he'll buy the mask for full price.  Return to the shop and pay it back.

Head to the shops and spend all of your money, on anything, who cares what, it 
doesn't matter.  The point is to just use up all of your money buying stuff.  
Now there is one final mask now to sell and it's the most difficult, but not 
by much.  Take the BUNNY HOOD from the shop and leave town.  Head southwest 
from Hyrule Town to Lon Lon Ranch, there is a man running laps around the 
ranch.  Once you find this man speak to him while wearing the Bunny Hood and 
he will pay you for it.  Basically what he does is just give you money until 
your wallet is full, that's why I said to waste all the money you had since he 
just fills it to the limit again.  Return to Hyrule Market and give the owner 
of the shop the 50 Rupees, the other 450 are yours to keep.  There is also a 
special bonus, you are now free to borrow the MASK OF TRUTH whenever you want 
to.  The Mask of Truth allows you to listen to all those Gossip Stones placed 
conveniently around the world, before they really didn't do anything.  You can 
also now borrow the GORON MASK, GERUDO MASK and ZORA MASK but they don't 
really serve any purpose besides changing conversations between random people.  
Since you can only have one mask in your inventory at a time, hang onto the 
Mask of Truth as it is the only one which really does anything.  It's finally 
time to proceed with the main game, your destination is Hyrule Town now.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______)
      (, /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ EMPLE OF TIME ---------------------------------------------- 01.25 --+

If you didn't follow the previous section then it shouldn't be that big of a 
deal, basically when you approach the front drawbridge of Hyrule Town there is 
a scene where you acquire the Ocarina of Time.  Once you have it and have 
learned the Song of Time from Princess Zelda, head into the Market.  In 
between the Happy Mask Shop and the normal shop off in the background a large 
temple can be seen, head toward that temple now.  If you have the Mask of 
Truth you can speak to all of these Gossip Stones and hear what they have to 
say.  Now enter the temple when you are prepared.  Head forward and stand on 
that little bit of red carpet, when you select to use your Ocarina of Time, 
the little note thing at the bottom should appear.  Play the Song of Time 
which you just learned (right, A, down, right, A, down).  After doing this a 
scene will trigger.  Climb the stairs and enter the next room.  Approach the 
Master Sword and pull it out of the stone to trigger another scene.  When 
everything is said and done you acquire the LIGHT MEDALLION and the MASTER 
SWORD.  You have now officially transformed into Adult Link, a world awaits.
______________________________________________________________________________


      __     __)
     (, /|  /|
       / | / |
+-- (_/  |/  |_ ARKET ---------------------------------------------- 01.26 --+

Begin by familiarizing yourself with your new character.  His moves and 
abilities are basically the same with one fundamental difference, Adult Link 
cannot use many of the items and pieces of equipment that Young Link could, 
Boomerang, Deku Sticks and the Fairy Slingshot for example.  Other than that 
it won't take too much getting used to.  Leave the Temple of Time and head for 
the Hyrule Market.  You may want to take a moment to visit the Hyrule Castle 
just to see what has happened to it.  When you're ready, head for the entrance 
of Hyrule Town.  Enter that small room that had all the pots back when you 
were Young Link.  Speak to the guy in here who runs the ghost shop, we can't 
take part in his little adventure yet but it's good to know that he is here.  
After this leave the little room and return to Hyrule Field, it too has 
changed significantly in the years passed.  Go now to Lon Lon Ranch.
______________________________________________________________________________


        _    
    ___/__)  
   (, /      
     /       
+-- (_____ ON LON RANCH -------------------------------------------- 01.27 --+

First of all note that it has to be daytime when you come here.  Make your way 
to the front of the fenced in area with the horses and you will find Ingo 
there.  Speak to Ingo and he will offer to let you ride one of his horses for 
a mere ten Rupees, you should agree to this.  A one minute timer will appear.  
The horse is directly in front of you, but this is not the horse you want.  
Bring up your Ocarina and play Epona's Song (up, left, right, up, left, right) 
and when you do this Epona the horse will automatically run toward you.  Get 
on this horse and begin to ride.  Riding is easy, simply hold up on the analog 
stick.  You have a total of six carrots which you can give to the horse at any 
time by pressing the A button while riding.  After giving all six carrots the 
horse gets exhausted which basically means you shouldn't ever give more than 
five, and wait until the carrots replenish before giving more, they will 
replenish automatically.  Note that there are two fences to jump over in here, 
simply run at the fences head on the jump over them.  For the higher ones you 
must use a carrot before you reach them otherwise the horse isn't going fast 
enough and won't jump.  When you're ready to get off the horse just slow it 
down to a stop and press the A button, you will automatically dismount.

During your next ride on the horse, approach Ingo while riding Epona and press 
the L/Z trigger to target him.  Speak to Ingo and he will ask if you want to 
bet 50 Rupees on a race, you certainly do.  When the race begins you will have 
to do one lap around the fenced area against Ingo.  The most important thing 
in the entire race is simply not to get caught on one of the corners, it will 
slow you down immensely.  Stay closer to the wall but not so close that you 
are touching it and everything will be good.  Never use more than five carrots 
at a time and other than that, just race until you eventually win.

When you do win however, Ingo challenges you to a second race and bets the 
horse Epona on this one.  It's going to be a fair bit more difficult but I 
think you can manage it.  Here's the trick I use to win this.  Right at the 
beginning of the race don't really bother with any carrots right away, use one 
just after you start to keep up as you go around the first corner, then 
another one maybe just to keep up.  After the first or second corner, move 
away from the wall a but and use all the rest of your carrots quickly until 
you are down to one, it should be enough to pass him early.  The purpose of 
this is to be ahead of him on the straightaway on the opposite side, you won't 
have any carrots, but you'll be ahead.  While you are ahead simply ride at 
normal speed and move around so that you are always in front of Ingo, he won't 
be able to pass you.  By the time you reach the next corner all six of your 
carrots will have replenished and you will still be ahead of him, use five of 
them quickly to get around and you'll be far ahead when you cross the line.

Winning the race does indeed net you the horse however Ingo closes the gate 
and will not let you leave the ranch.  When you have a powerful and fast 
jumping horse though, this isn't exactly a problem.  Ignore the gate and note 
the fence that runs on both sides along the east and west walls.  You may 
think that these are too high however use a couple of carrots and run toward 
either outer wall and Epona will jump the fence and jump out of Lon Lon Ranch 
entirely.  With Epona on the field you can now get from place to place much 
easier than you could before, remember that Epona's Song can always be used to 
summon the horse to you.  With the horse in your possession now, your next 
destination is going to be Kakariko Village, so ride over there now.
______________________________________________________________________________


       __   __)
      (, ) /
        /(
     ) /  \_
+-- (_/      AKARIKO VILLAGE --------------------------------------- 01.10 --+

When you get into Kakariko Village the first thing you are going to want to do 
is head for the Graveyard and go up the stairs beside the Graveyard and into 
the windmill.  Speak to the crazy guy with the music box in here and then 
while standing in front of him, take out your Ocarina.  He will teach you a 
very special song, the SONG OF STORMS which is played with the following 
combination: A, down, up, A, down, up.  The Song of Storms will, as you 
probably have figured, make it start raining and storming.  The practical uses 
for this song are few in number, but you do need it, so now you have it.

The next thing to do is also quite important because it is the first step in 
acquiring the best sword in the game, the Biggoron Sword.  You cannot complete 
the sidequest fully at this point in the game of course, but you can start it.  
Make your way to the southeast corner of town where that woman is beside the 
chicken coop, the same woman who wanted you to help her find all the Cuccos 
seven years ago.  She tells you about a new type of Cucco she has been 
breeding called a Pocket Cucco.  She gives you the POCKET EGG which will hatch 
overnight just like the other egg.  After acquiring it, play the Sun's Song 
once, and then again to make the egg hatch into a POCKET CUCCO.  Once you have 
the Pocket Cucco, head to the very middle of town and go into the house just 
to the northeast.  In here you will find Talon sleeping on a bed.  Stand 
beside him with your new Pocket Cucco equipped on one of the C Buttons and use 
it.  Talon should wake up and then when you talk to him, he should mention 
something about going back to the ranch.  You have now accomplished your goal, 
return and speak with the girl standing beside the chicken coop once again.  
After speaking with her, use the Pocket Cucco and assuming it woke up Talon, 
it will be happy like she asked.  As a reward for what you have done she gives 
you COJIRO, a special Blue Cucco which has stopped crowing entirely.

With that out of the way and little to nothing you can do to continue the 
sidequest at this point, so make your way to the Graveyard.  Make your way to 
the northwest area where you planted the sprout as Young Link, there is not a 
platform here which when stepped on, raises you up to the ledge.  Roll into 
the crate to destroy it and you will get a PIECE OF HEART [018].  Now drop 
down again and notice the tombstone beside the bean plant that has three small 
flowers in front of it, and was not there back when you were Young Link.  Grab 
hold of this tombstone from behind and pull it backward to reveal a hole in 
the ground.  Drop down this hole.  At the bottom you will find the ghost of 
Dampe who, of all things, seems eager to race you, the race begins!

Your goal is to follow Dampe through the winding halls without losing him, 
it's not actually a "race" in the sense that you can never win, you only real 
goal is to keep up enough so that you aren't left behind.  Dampe drops fire 
traps which are easy enough to avoid assuming you see them in advance.  Always 
be sure to note which direction he turns because many will eventually lead you 
to dead ends.  If you manage to follow him all the way through to the end 
without being left behind that he places a large treasure chest in front of 
you which contains the HOOKSHOT.  Now that you have the Hookshot in your 
possession which is one of the best items ever, proceed through the open door.

There are two blocks in this room piled on top of one another.  What you need 
to do to get past this is play the Song of Time on your Ocarina, (right, A, 
down, right, A, down.)  Now you need to return to the Graveyard once again, we 
aren't quite done with Mr. Dampe.  Agree to his race once again, this time we 
are going to do everything we can to finish in under one minute, that is the 
goal.  Follow these directions carefully and you cannot lose, assuming you 
manage to avoid the fire.  First of all you have to keep rolling in order to 
win this, rolling is indeed faster than running for the most part.  Jump down 
at the start and begin chasing after him, make a right, then a left and follow 
him straight between the purple flames.  Go left at the fork and follow him 
down the hall then make another left and left again at the next two forks, 
this will take you to the door.  Through the door turn right a couple of times 
to reach the stone room, move toward the right wall diagonally while still 
rolling.  Make a left at the next fork then a right at the purple flames to 
reach the second door.  In this room don't follow him, instead move diagonally 
to the right and you will cut across and save some time.  Head up to those 
ledges you have to climb, and climb up the left one.  Go left when you reach a 
fork and this takes you to that walkway that goes around to the final door.  
If your time was in fact less than one minute then you are rewarded with the 
PIECE OF HEART [019].  That's it for racing, return to the main town area.

Back in the village, play the Sun's Song so that it becomes night time.  Use 
your newly acquired Hookshot to hook yourself onto one of the roofs in the 
village.  Make your way to the far north where the gate leading up to Death 
Mountain Trail is located.  Go up the stairs so you are between the gate and 
the stairs and walk toward the east wall, then turn around and face south.  
Notice the house just a few steps southeast of you, it's the Potion Shop and 
has a picture of a cauldron on the outside.  When you enter this shop it's 
called the Potion Shop so you know it's the right one.  You need to get on top 
of this house.  From where I told you to stand, near the wall between the 
stairs and the gate, fire your Hookshot at the little bit of red roof you can 
see from here and you should be able to pull yourself up onto the Potion Shop.  
Now run to the other side of the roof and hook across to the purple roof with 
the guy sitting on it.  Speak to him and he will give you PIECE OF HEART 
[020].  Next you will want to make your way to the southwest corner of town.  
Climb the stairs and hook onto the roof of the House of Skulltula.  From there 
turn around and face south, hook onto the house with the chicken coop attached 
to it.  During the night you will find a GOLD SKULLTULA [044] crawling around 
on the wall up here.  Defeat it using your Hookshot, you can also grab the 
token with the Hookshot.  That about covers everything to do here now.

Leave the village and get on your horse, there are a couple of very small 
things to do before we continue with anything else.  First of all remember how 
Talon said he was going back to Lon Lon Ranch?  Head to Lon Lon Ranch now, on 
Epona of course, be sure to bring Epona into the ranch with you.  When you 
speak to Malon while riding the horse two times, she will offer to let you 
race and obstacle course.  The time to beat is 50 Seconds and she says that if 
you beat it you will get a present.  The obstacle course isn't going to be too 
easy, you have to make two laps around in the fifty seconds allotted and there 
are fences that you have to jump.  Even if you seem to do everything perfectly 
you often won't beat the time so here are a couple of tips.  At the beginning 
use a fair number of carrots but don't use any as you go around toward the 
long straight on the other side, it's just small gates and your carrots will 
replenish.  Use as many as you need to get over the medium and high gates on 
the long side and then when you land let one or two replenish and use all of 
them down until you only have one left going over the last medium gate toward 
the finish line.  Let them replenish as you cross the line and start over with 
the exact same strategy again, except this time when you get near the finish 
line just waste all of them to get across as fast as possible.  Assuming you 
get a time of 0:49 or better then Malon will give you a present, she says it 
is too big to give you so she'll have it delivered to your home.  The present 
is in fact a whole cow that now lives in your house in Kokiri Forest.  I guess 
you can get all the Lon Lon Milk you want quite easily now.

As I said one more final little task before proceeding, this one is far easier 
than that last one, just a simple fetch.  Return to Hyrule Castle town and 
make your way through the Market up toward Ganon's Castle.  Follow the path 
around and under the arch.  When you get through the arch turn around and
