there you will see the GOLD SKULLTULA [045] crawling around on the wall, get 
it using your Hookshot.  With that out of the way it's time to head back to 
the Kokiri Forest where it all started and more specifically, the Lost Woods.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______)
      (, /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ HE LOST WOODS ----------------------------------------------- 1.29 --+

Immediately as you enter Kokiri Forest you are attacked by a plant on your 
right, defeat the enormous thing before proceeding any further.  There are all 
sorts of enemies around the town but nothing too dangerous.  Don't worry about 
doing anything here in the village itself, simply head up to the Lost Woods.

Just as you enter the Lost Woods, make a left and head down that path.  There 
is a man sitting beside the stump here in this area, but unfortunately he is 
sleeping.  That however can be remedied quite easily, equip the blue Cucco 
Cojiro on one of your C-Buttons and then use it while standing in front of the 
man.  He offers to give you an item which will go bad if you don't deliver it 
quickly, I think we have time to make a quick detour.  Accept his offer and he 
gives you the ODD MUSHROOM.  You now have three minutes to get to the Potion 
Shop in Kakariko Village.  If you do not have Epona then you can pretty much 
forget being able to do this.  Since you should have the horse though it won't 
be too difficult.  What you need to do is quickly get out of the Lost Woods 
and jump onto your horse.  Ride the horse all the way back to Kakariko 
Village.  At the north end of Kakariko Village there is the Potion Shop, go 
inside and take the back door to get around behind, the other option would be 
to Hookshot onto the roof and drop down to reach that fenced area at the back.  
Climb the stairs and enter the other door back there, it will take you to a 
witch.  Use the Odd Mushroom while standing in front of her and she will give 
you the ODD MEDICINE.  With that in hand you now need to head back to the Lost 
Woods once again.  Go to where the man was sitting previously and there you 
will find a small girl.  Stand in front of her and use the Odd Medicine to 
acquire the POACHER'S SAW.  Your quest for the large sword is proceeding well.

From the entrance of the Lost Woods again, make a right, then a left, then a 
right, then a left.  When you try to make that last left, you'll find there is 
a small elf boy blocking the path similar to back at the very beginning of the 
game.  Getting by him is simple, simply stand beside the boy and pull out your 
Ocarina.  Play Saria's Song for him and he will move.  In the area after this 
make a left again and you will come across that small part of the forest where 
you planted a Magic Bean when you were a kid.  Step on the Magic Bean platform 
and it will take you up to a GOLD SKULLTULA [046].  Be sure to kill it and 
grab the token.  Leave this little area of the forest and make a left, then 
another left, then a right to reach the Sacred Meadow.  Walk forward and Navi 
will stop to talk to you.  There is a new type of enemy which walks the paths 
here, one which is vulnerable from behind.  If you are in front of this enemy 
it will charge and attack you, however if you are behind it, the enemy cannot 
detect you and you can kill it in a single hit by firing you Hookshot right at 
the centre of its back.  Make your way through this area to the end where you 
will find kind of the boss of these enemies, he wields a large club.  This 
enemy slams it down on the left, then the right, then left and that's his 
pattern.  It takes three sword slashes to kill him.  Once you get past this 
enemy there is a scene at the top of the stairs.  You will learn the MINUET OF 
FOREST here.  After learning it equip your Hookshot and aim up at the tree 
branch above, you need to fire it there to get up and reach the entrance.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ________) 
      (, /       
        /___,    
     ) /         
+-- (_/   OREST TEMPLE --------------------------------------------- 01.30 --+

Welcome to the first of many Adult Link dungeons, these can be quite difficult 
but I'm sure you will manage to get through just fine.  The first thing you 
are going to want to do is step forward and defeat the two wolves which appear 
out of the ground.  Once they are defeated, turn right from the entrance and 
you will see some vines on the wall.  Climb these vines up and go left a 
little bit, you can drop down on top of the tree.  Take aim to your right 
where there is a GOLD SKULLTULA [047] crawling around on the wall and defeat 
it using the Hookshot, then grab the token.  Walk out onto the branch and jump 
to the other tree branch, there you will find a small chest with a SMALL KEY 
inside of it.  After acquiring this key, jump down and head through the door.

Watch out for the Skulltula which drops down right in front of you in this 
hallway.  When it drops down just wait for it to spin around and then you can 
kill it quite easily.  Make your way down the hall and enter the next room.  
The elevator is up here but then four Poes appear and make it descend, you 
aren't going to be able to take that elevator until you find all four of the 
Poes.  Run across to the opposite side of the room and climb up, while 
standing beside the door look to your right and use the Hookshot to get the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [048].  After eliminating the Gold Skulltula, head through the 
door you are standing beside.  There is a skull enemy floating around in here 
covered in blue fire.  While this enemy is covered in fire he cannot be 
harmed, target him with the L/Z button and fire your Hookshot to remove the 
fire and then feel free to dispatch him normally with the sword.

Through this next door there are two extremely difficult enemies.  Not only do 
these (the Stalfos) fight very well, their attacks deal two entire hearts of 
damage, you have to have a good defense while fighting these enemies as well 
as a good offense.  Here's what to do.  Target one at a time, while targeting 
one of the other will never attack you.  Now keep a distance from the 
enemy, if you get to close they can hit you even with your shield up.  Wait 
for the enemy to attack, block it and hit the A button to do a jump attack, 
this should work quite well and allow you to defeat them with minimal damage.  
Once the two enemies are dead open the small chest to get a SMALL KEY.  Return 
to the main hall and head to the northwest corner (as if the way you came in 
was the south side.)  In other words, turn right when you come out of the room 
you were just in, you should find a short hallway with a black at the end.  
Stand in front of this block and play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, 
A, down.)  The block will disappear and you can go through the door.

Defeat the plant enemy which is directly in front of you and then make a 
right.  You should see vines covering the wall, equip the Hookshot and aim it 
up at the Skulltulas.  There are three of them on the vines but it's not 
possible for you to aim high enough to hit the third, so kill the lower two 
and climb up on the far right side.  I really mean the far right, as far over 
as you can get.  When you get close to the Skulltula try to pass it when it's 
face is pointing in another direction, you should be able to get into that 
little door area before it attacks.  In the next room whip out the Hookshot 
and fire it at the skull to remove its blue fire, then kill it.  This will 
unlock the doors and reveal a large chest containing the DUNGEON MAP.  Head 
through the door that you didn't come in from, Eliminate the plant on the 
balcony and then you have two options.  You can climb up onto the railing on 
the left and jump from there to the vines on the wall.  Shimmy right and up 
until you are above the arch and that weird emblem, then drop to the balcony 
below you.  The easier way is to simply equip the Hookshot and fire it at the 
weird emblem, that will take you right over there.  Upon landing step on the 
red button to lower the water level and then jump off the balcony.

At the bottom on one side you will find that well which just drained.  Jump 
down into it, get the Recovery Hearts and run to the end of the tunnel to find 
a small chest containing a SMALL KEY.  Leave this well and get ready for a 
difficult move.  Notice that in the middle of the water here there is a raised 
area of land, well on top of this land you will probably notice a small 
treasure chest.  What you need to do is stand at the side and aim your 
Hookshot for this treasure chest.  The problem is though that when you hit it 
from the side, it will pull you up but not far enough to actually stay on 
land.  The trick is to aim your Hookshot for a part of the chest which is 
actually closer to the wall than the edge or the water, this way it will pull 
you all the way up and you'll stay there.  Open the chest to get a RECOVERY 
HEART and then take a few steps in front of it to find GOLD SKULLTULA [049] 
crawling around on the wall.  Use your Hookshot to get the token and then jump 
down and go through the door which takes you back to the main hall again.

On the west side of the main hall there is a locked door, given that you 
should have a total of three Small Keys at the moment I think it's safe to 
assume that you can sacrifice one of them.  Head west through this door and 
use your Hookshot to eliminate the Skulltula in front of you.  In the next 
room you will be attacked immediately by one of the blue flame skulls, since 
you probably don't have time to use your Hookshot note that blocking it with 
your shield will also knock away the blue flame.  Ascend up the ladders and go 
through the arch, Navi will stop for a moment to tell you a few things.  After 
Navi is finished talking to you, turn left and there you will see a block in 
your way.  Grab it with the A button and pull backward until it is aligned 
with the path in the direction you were moving previously before Navi 
interrupted you.  Now push the block up in that direction until you eventually 
hit the wall.  At that point turn around and go down the hall which the block 
was obstructing previously, make your way around to the same block again but 
from a different side.  Push it along until the end where it drops down a 
little, this means you have done it correctly, listen for the confirmation 
sound.  Now you can climb up the block and up to the higher area.

At the first fork look left to find another block, just a different colour 
this time.  This part is slightly annoying, what you have to do is pull it 
out, but you can't pull it out all the way since you hit the wall.  After 
you've got it out as far as you can drop down to the lower area and head to 
the hall which had the original block.  Climb up the ladder here and make a 
right, then a right again.  Push the second block as far as it will go, then 
turn around and go back up again.  Now that the second block is aligned in the 
hall, push it along to the end where it will drop down and you'll hear a 
sound.  Climb up this block and up to the next ledge, make a right at the top 
and follow the path around to a ladder.  This takes you up to a room with two 
flaming skulls and a locked door, since locked doors do not require you to 
defeat the enemies first, simply ignore the skulls and go through it.

Be careful here, the hallway is a little messed up.  When you reach the room 
at the end you need to heed Navi's words carefully, if you stand too long in 
one spot an unseen force will descend from above and take you back to the very 
first room of the dungeon, it isn't pleasant.  Simply jump across and use your 
final key to unlock this door.  There is a staircase in this room for you to 
start climbing down, ignore all the weird stuff that happens as you descend 
and enter the room at the bottom, prepare for a challenging fight.  This fight 
has two phases, the first is a battle against a single Stalfos with a large 
hole in the middle of the room.  Your opponent has the ability to walk on the 
invisible ground but you do not so when doing battle be sure to stay to the 
side.  After this enemy is defeated the hole fills in but two new Stalfos 
appear.  What happens here is that they have the ability to revive themselves 
as long as the other is still alive.  The trick to winning here is to hit the 
first Stalfos with two jump attacks, then hit the second Stalfos with two jump 
attacks.  Now both of them only have one more jump attack before they die, 
quickly kill one and then move onto the other, with only one hit you can kill 
it before the second has a chance to revive.  Doing this makes a large chest 
appear containing the FAIRY BOW, a very important item you will need.

Return to the previous room (the door with the red carpet in front of it, not 
the blue.)  In this area there are three paintings which randomly have the Poe 
in them.  When you aim at these paintings the Poe disappears, in order to be 
able to hit it you have to get to a far away distance, aim with your Fairy Bow 
and fire.  Do this to all three paintings when they have the Poes in them and 
the Poe will come out.  Go down to the bottom of the stairs and battle it, it 
takes three jump attacks with your Master Sword to kill them.  When the Poe 
dies it lights the torch and a chest appears containing a SMALL KEY.  Head 
through the door leading to the room where you battle the Stalfos and continue 
across to the next set of stairs.  Shoot your Fairy Bow at all three paintings 
in this room as well, then kill the Poe when it appears.  Once again a torch 
is lit and a large chest appears.  This large chest contains the COMPASS for 
the dungeon.  Climb the stairs and use your Small Key on the door at the top.

Turn around immediately and go back into the stairwell.  Head back down the 
stairs, through the room at the bottom and up the other stairs.  Jump across 
this room and go through the twisted hallway.  Just outside the twisted 
hallway turn around and look above the door to see an eyeball there.  Shoot it 
with your Fairy Bow to untwist the twisted hallway.  Now this next this is 
totally not worth your time but I'm letting you know.  If you go back to where 
that first large green block was, and climb the ladder you will find an 
eyeball on the wall.  Shoot it to make a small chest appear containing a 
BUNDLE OF ARROWS, that's right, just arrows.  Anyway head through the hallway 
which is no longer twisted and jump down.  Here you will find a fancy chest 
which contains the BOSS KEY.  This is a special key which not surprisingly 
allows you to gain access to the boss.  There is a hole in the middle of this 
room, drop down it and defeat the flaming skulls at the bottom to unlock the 
door.  When you go through it you should be in a familiar looking area.

Ahead of you is a raised stone object with two Recovery Hearts on it.  Jump to 
this thing and walk slowly to the end.  Equip your Hookshot and aim it up at 
the wall, there is a GOLD SKULLTULA [050] crawling around on the wall.  If you 
have been following the guide up to this point then congratulations, you now 
officially have exactly half of the game's total Gold Skulltulas.  Turn around 
carefully and jump back to the ledge.  As you can see from your minimap there 
are three paths leading west, take the bottom one.  In this room there is one 
of those hand enemies that charges at you with some kind of super punch.  When 
this happens defend yourself with the Hylian Shield and then attack the hand 
with your sword.  After it is defeated it splits into little hands.  At that 
point just kill them off with the Hookshot or use the Spin Attack or whatever.  
A small chest appears now containing a SMALL KEY.  Once you have this leave 
the room and make a right, the door leads you to a familiar area.

Head all the way up to the room with the eyeball before the untwisted hallway.  
Fire an arrow at the eyeball and the hallway will twist itself once again.  
Make your way through this area, down the stairs and up the stairs again.  
Take the door here at the top which you should have already unlocked, if not, 
then unlock it now.  In this room once again you will be targeted by an unseen 
enemy, but this time it is in your best interests to kill the enemy.  Jump 
down and take a look under yourself.  When a shadow begins to appear start 
running.  At a certain size it will stop following you and the hand will drop, 
take aim and kill it using your sword.  There is only one and you will not be 
bothered anymore, at least until you leave the room.  Climb the ladder on the 
south side and head south.  Defeat the two green flame skulls when the flames 
subside and then use your one remaining key to open the door at the end.

This room is actually quite interesting, on the west wall there is an eyeball 
switch however it is covered in ice.  What you have to do is jump onto one of 
the moving pillars in the middle and aim your Fairy Bow at it.  Wait until the 
pillar moves you around to the east side, there the torch will be in between 
the eyeball and your arrow so that when you shoot the arrow, the fire will 
heat it up and allow it to melt the ice.  The shot is rather difficult but 
here's how I do it.  Aim at the eyeball and don't even think about the torch, 
all that matters is keeping yourself aimed at the eye.  When you happen to 
pass by the torch, then fire at the eye, the shot doesn't have to come too 
close to the fire to be successful.  This will twist the previous hallway, now 
go back through it to the room before and drop down the hole at the end.

There is some kind of crazy checkered pattern in this room and a ceiling which 
id dropping down over and over again.  What you want to do is wait for it to 
drop and then head up, staying close to the right wall.  When the Skulltula 
drops down kill it with the Hookshot and stand on the square where it came 
down from.  After the next drop make your way up and stand on the button, wait 
here for the ceiling to drop then go up a couple, kill the Skulltula and take 
its square.  From here you cane see the small chest and see that the square 
beside it is safe.  Go up and open the chest to get a BUNDLE OF ARROWS and 
then take the north door into the next room.  Here you will find a picture of 
a Poe on the wall.  Take aim with the Fairy Bow and fire at the picture, some 
blocks will fall down.  You have one minute to push together a picture of the 
Poe, the block in the middle is not used, you only need four.  The solution is 
far too obvious to go through the pain of describing, the picture is clear as 
day.  After putting them together you have to defeat the Poe which unlocks the 
western door and lights the torch.  Now proceed through the west door.

There is a Skulltula waiting for you in this hallway, simply kill it using the 
Hookshot and continue back to the main hall once again.  When you jump down a 
battle against a miniboss will begin, this boss can be a real pain in the ass 
if you don't know what you're doing so listen well.  There are four copies of 
this boss but only one real one.  When will appear and begin to circle around 
you.  A split second after all four of them appear, one of them will do a 
quick turn, and that is the real one.  Auto target that one with the L/Z 
button and fire your Hookshot at it.  That's how to win, it takes a decent 
number of hits, but early identification like this means you cannot lose.  If 
you hit the wrong one then the remaining three start spinning faster, you 
don't want that.  Once the fourth and final Poe is defeated then all four 
torches are lit and the elevator once back up.  Ride it down from here.

It's not quite time for the boss yet, there is still something odd going on.  
Notice that on both sides of the room there are things sticking out of the 
wall, you can grab these things from the sides and push or pull.  Push the 
room clockwise three times and an opening will appear which leads to a button, 
go press it.  After pressing the button rotate it clockwise another two times 
and enter the room with the Skulltula.  Kill the Skulltula and open the small 
chest to get a BUNDLE OF ARROWS.  There is a also a GOLD SKULLTULA [051] 
crawling around on the wall in this room.  Leave the room and rotate clockwise 
once more, a second button will be available to push.  Now rotate the room for 
the last time, twice clockwise and hit the third and final button.  This opens 
up the north hall which takes you to the Big Key door and to the boss.

Upon arriving at the top nothing really happens, it's not until you turn 
around and try to leave that the boss appears, the Evil Spirit from Beyond, 
Phantom Ganon!  He begins the battle by jumping into one of the six paintings.  
Now after this he will come out of a different painting, but it isn't going to 
be that simple, he actually comes back out of two paintings.  One of these two 
enemies will get to the edge of the painting and suddenly turn around, this is 
the fake.  The other will jump out of the painting through a purple portal and 
shoot a huge electricity blast at the ground which goes in all directions, 
being at the edge of the field is still the safest place for this.  What you 
have to do is randomly guess which one is right, when he reaches the edge if a 
portal appears it's the right one.  You should have been aiming your Fairy Bow 
the whole time, when the portal appears fire into the middle of the portal and 
Phantom Ganon will be hit, turning around and not attacking.  If you guessed 
wrong then be ready to move out of the way and avoid the attack which deals 
two hearts worth of damage each hit.  If you guess wrong, the instant you see 
him turn around the is time to spin around 180 degrees and fire an arrow, you 
have to be extremely quick with this one and it's difficult, but you can hit 
the real Phantom Ganon before he attacks if you're lucky.  Aiming is probably 
a luxury you won't be afforded.  After three hits the second phase starts.

Phantom Ganon's horse has been destroyed it seems and now it's time to fight 
him as he simply floats around on his own.  This boss only has one attack and 
it's only half as strong as the previous one, but it's more frequent.  What he 
does is float around and fire this energy blast at you.  The energy blast can 
be deflected with the Master Sword back at him for damage.  Unfortunately the 
boss can deflect it back at you, and then you back at him and then him back at 
you.  If you timing sucks you'll be in trouble but fortunately there seems to 
be a way of getting around it.  First of all here's what I find most 
important, maybe it's just me but it seems then when you are moving around, 
Phantom Ganon doesn't deflect it back nearly as often, he kind of just lets it 
hit him.  This isn't true all the time but the point is, after deflecting it 
back, don't give him a stationary target and he will probably be less likely 
to hit it back.  Try to be moving as well when you strike it in the first 
place.  After Phantom Ganon is hit by the blast run up to him and begin 
slashing with your sword as much as possible.  Repeat this pattern until dead.  
After the battle there is a scene, grab the HEART CONTAINER and take the warp 
portal out of the dungeon.  You will also acquire the FOREST MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ADVENTURE -------------------------------------- 01.31 --+

Return to Kokiri Forest and play the Sun's Song to make it change to night 
time here.  Head for the southeast house with the two pillars coming out of 
the top and up there you will see GOLD SKULLTULA [052].  Get it from up there 
using your Hookshot.  Return to Hyrule Field as there are a few optional 
things we can accomplish at this point.  First of all you are going to want to 
return to the Temple of Time, there you will meet Shiek again and you will 
learn PRELUDE OF LIGHT.  Now if you didn't teach the Scarecrow at Hyrule Lake 
a song when you were young, then go back and do that now.  If you still 
remember the Scarecrow's Song then don't worry about it.  As an Adult, get   
on Epona and head down to Lake Hylia where you will find the Scarecrow.  Play 
the Scarecrow's Song while standing beside him and he'll say that anytime you 
play that song from now on, if Pierre is near then he will help you.  That is 
going to come in handy.  With that out of the way, get back on Epona and 
return to Hyrule Field, it's time for some good old fashioned Poe catching.

First of all, in order to catch the Big Poes you must be riding Epona and you 
must have the Fairy Bow.  Assuming you have met these conditions, get on your 
horse and get ready to make chase.  The very first Big Poe is found simply by 
getting on Epona right at the gate to Hyrule Castle Town and riding down the 
path directly to the south.  A ghost should appear, at this point jam the A 
button to give your horse about four carrots to keep up and then hold B, 
quickly fire one arrow at the Big Poe and then another arrow.  When it dies it 
will drop something, assuming you have Empty Bottles use an Empty Bottle to 
catch the BIG POE.  You will need a new bottle for each one that you catch.

Since you still have two Empty Bottles on you let's keep going.  The next one 
we're going to get is over west of the entrance to Hyrule Castle Town, beyond 
the wall head for the northwest corner of the field and follow the stream 
down, just away from it a bit and you should find the next BIG POE.  After 
this we are going to want to head for the entrance to Gerudo Valley.  
Southeast from there a little bit is a fork in the path kind of which splits 
into two, three if you count the one you are coming from.  At this point if 
you go north you should manage to find the third BIG POE.  Eliminate it and 
collect it with your third Empty Bottle.  Now that all three are filled, 
return to the entrance of Hyrule Castle Town, that small room with all the 
pots inside and speak to the guy there.  He'll give you rewards if you equip 
the Big Poes and use them while standing in front of him.  He says that you'll 
get a special prize once you reach 1000 points, and since each Poe nets you 
100 points, that means you must collect a total of ten.  Leave this place and 
return to Hyrule Field, we've got lots more Big Poes to catch here.

Make your way down to Lon Lon Ranch on your horse and head for the north side.  
Notice that just outside the entrance to the ranch there is a tree, when you 
approach this tree another BIG POE is going to appear, this will be your 
fourth.  The fifth is an extremely difficult one to get.  Make your way toward 
Kakariko Village and when you cross the bridge go east toward the river under 
that cliff ledge thing.  The Big Poe might appear here, but if you stay close 
to the wall it will not.  Turn around and ride back toward the tree in front 
of the Kakariko Village entrance, if you're lucky the BIG POE will appear 
there, if not it will likely appear over the river out of reach.  That's the 
fifth.  One more before we go back and redeem our spirits again.  Next you 
will want to take a look at your map.  Notice a big ugly "y" shape made by the 
paths in between Lon Lon Ranch and Kokiri Forest?  Your destination is the 
fork in the path where all three of them meet, there you will find a rock and 
when you approach the rock, the sixth BIG POE will appear.  Catch it in your 
bottle and then make your way back to Hyrule Castle Town to sell all three.

We're definitely getting closer now.  For the seventh one you are going to 
have to return to the area where you got the previous one, the fork in the 
path between Lon Lon Ranch and Kokiri Forest.  As you make your way south from 
here you will notice a green tree and beside this tree are a bunch of little 
grass shrubs.  Run through these shrubs and the next BIG POE should appear.  
Now this may sound weird, but the next one after this is almost in the exact 
same place.  Ride off for a bit and turn around, then come back.  Ride through 
the forest, through the area a bit east of here and you should be able to find 
yet another BIG POE, that covers eight of them.  For the ninth you are going 
to want to head north of the "y" fork.  Notice that east of Lon Lon Ranch 
there is a stone wall which curves in the shape of a boomerang.  Get on the 
east side of this wall, the lower side and ride your horse directly along the 
edge of it.  At the bend in the middle, the ninth BIG POE will appear and you 
will be able to capture it.  At this point head back to Hyrule Castle town and 
redeem the three Poes you have, there is only one more remaining.  

Lastly there is only one more Big Poe remaining.  To find this one head back 
west toward the Gerudo Valley.  Remember the earlier one you got around the 
fork in the path, southeast of Gerudo Valley?  Well north of that fork there 
is a lone tree and when you ride past this tree, the tenth BIG POE will appear 
and allow you to capture it.  That covers all ten of the Big Poes you need, 
return to Hyrule Castle Town one final time now.  Enter the small room and 
present the final Poe to the ghost man, he will give you the fourth and final 
EMPTY BOTTLE in return for you work.  Coupled with the 500 Rupees you have 
already made from it, the reward is reasonably nice all things considered.

Our next destination is going to be Kakariko Village for a couple of reasons, 
I would likely recommend you go there anyway, but as it turns out that where 
you're going to have to go to proceed with the main game, so this will 
certainly save time.  The reason I suggest you head for Kakariko Village is 
because at this point, you should have collected at least 50 Gold Skulltulas.  
Enter the House of Skulltula here, yes you can still do it while you are an 
adult.  Speak to the guy in the upper right corner and he will give you a 
PIECE OF HEART [021].  Your next destination is the Shooting Gallery, a 
building which was in Hyrule Market before is now in Kakariko Village.  The 
Shooting Gallery is the building in the centre of town which did not even 
exist when you were Young Link.  The Shooting Gallery is going to be exactly 
as you remember it with one seemingly minor yet important difference, the 
order in which the Rupees appear is entirely randomized.  All the positions 
for them are the same, such as the fast moving Red Rupees, the jumping Green 
Rupee, but the order in which they appear changes.  Your goal is to anticipate 
and hit all ten of the Rupees once again, except this time using your Fairy 
Bow.  If you can manage to hit all ten then you will be rewarded with the BIG 
QUIVER, a special item which allows you to carry a total of 40 Arrows instead 
of 30.  If you do it again after this your reward will only be 50 Rupees.  
Keep in mind that if you manage to hit at least eight out of ten, you are 
still given a free game after that to try out your luck once more.

At this point I would like to take the opportunity to admit a slight omission 
which really doesn't matter since I'm covering it now.  Also if you've just 
been using the checklist, it wouldn't make much difference anyway.  There is a 
Gold Skulltula to acquire here in Kakariko Village which was not covered 
earlier in the walkthrough when you were Young Link.  If you are trying to 
collect all 100 then I recommend you do the following: head back to the Temple 
of Time in Hyrule Castle Town and insert the Master Sword to become young 
again then head to Kakariko Village.  When you enter, if it's not night time 
then play the Sun's Song.  See the tree in the centre of town right near the 
entrance?  Run up to it and roll into the tree, GOLD SKULLTULA [053] will drop 
down and then you can grab the token.  Make your way back to the Temple of Time 
and change to Adult Link again.  While we're on the subject we might as well 
get up to speed, there is one other Skulltula I neglected to mention before, 
but worry not.  Once you have that one you will be totally up to speed with 
how many you should have at this point.  It shouldn't take very long at all, 
simply play the Minuet of Forest which will teleport you to the area right in 
front of the Forest Temple.  Head down from here past that enormous enemy and 
go up the ladder straight ahead.  Up top here turn left and you will see the 
GOLD SKULLTULA [054] crawling around on the wall, use your Hookshot to get it.

At this point make your way back to Kokiri Village once again, as an adult.  
The fastest way I imagine would be to play the Prelude of Light and warp back 
to the Temple of Time, and then make your way over to Kakariko from there.  
With all of this out of the way, make your way up to the Death Mountain.  
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EATH MOUNTAIN --------------------------------------- 01.32 --+

Begin making your way up the Death Mountain Trail, it's a little bit more 
difficult now given that there are boulders rushing past you every couple of 
seconds, but you've got those rocks for cover at least.  When you get up to 
Dodongo's Cavern, get onto the Magic Bean plant and ride it up.  Jump off to 
the north almost immediately above the cave and grab the PIECE OF HEART [022] 
which was sitting up there.  You can ride this platform all over the mountain, 
but for now head back to the entrance of Dodongo's Cavern.  There is still one 
more Skulltula in Dodongo's Cavern which you have not yet acquired, let's go 
and get that one right now.  Enter Dodongo's Cavern and jump to the middle 
then across to the right.  Take the southern exit leading east.  This is the 
first area you entered before the one with all the baby Dodongos.  Go up and 
kill all of them since they will distract you at this part.  Somewhere around 
the middle of this hall you will hear the sound of a Skulltula.  You can also 
tell when you are close when Navi flies away from you and turns green right 
beside the Skulltula.  If you stay close to the left wall and move along (left 
from the perspective when you come into the room) you should see it, but you 
can't get up there.  Play the Scarecrow Song while standing beside it and a 
scarecrow will appear up there.  Stand back and shoot your Hookshot to grab 
onto the scarecrow and get up there, now you can snag GOLD SKULLTULA [055].

Leave Dodongo's Cavern and make your way up to the entrance of Goron City 
whether it be by the Magic Bean platform or just walking up there it doesn't 
make a difference.  In front of the entrance there is a circle of stones and 
when you stand in the middle of this circle you should be able to feel the 
controller rumble.  Instead of setting down a bomb here, what you want to do 
is take out your Ocarina and play the Song of Storms (A, down, up, A, down, 
up).  Suddenly a hole will appear underneath you and you'll drop down to an 
area with a small treasure chest, inside this chest is a HUGE RUPEE.  Leave 
this cave and make your way down to the entrance of the Goron City.

When you get inside, note the ropes which suspend the platform in the middle.  
Cross one of these ropes and while standing on that middle platform you'll hear 
a Skulltula sound.  Cross one of the ropes which will give you a view of the 
back of that panel and there is a GOLD SKULLTULA [056] on the back, you can 
get it using your Hookshot.  After getting this, drop down one level and note 
the small Goron rolling around here.  Equip your bombs and drop one so that it 
explodes as he rolls over it, then he'll begin to talk to you.  Speak to him 
again and he'll go on about the Fire Temple, then start crying.  At that point 
you have to ask him about both options which appear, one after the other.  
Once you do that the Goron gives you the GORON TUNIC which is heat-resistant 
and will allow you to pass through really hot areas like the Death Mountain 
Crater without worrying about burning to death.  All of the doors open at this 
point so feel free to explore the city.  Take the southwest path on this same 
floor and it will lead you to a giant Goron, it seems he has recently finished 
his weapon and he is willing to sell you the GIANT'S KNIFE, a new weapon for 
Link.  You may think this is great, however the knife will break quite easily 
after very little use, so what you want to do is just let it sit in your 
inventory for now, we'll utilize its power later when it will be more useful.

Drop down to the very bottom of town and take the west exit to find the Goron 
Shop again, not much has changed here in the past seven years.  Enter 
Darunia's room, the one to the north at the very bottom of the city and grab 
hold of the statue at the top of the room.  Drag it backward and this will 
reveal the secret path leading into the Death Mountain Crater.  If you see the 
timer appear then it means you didn't equip the Goron Tunic, go to your menu 
and do that now.  When you enter this area make a left and stop where the 
bridge has collapsed.  Equip your Hookshot and shoot it at the pole just ahead 
of you, this will pull you across to the complete section of the bridge.  As 
you start walking forward a scene occurs where you learn the BOLERO OF FIRE.  

Now there is a special trick you can do to net some neat stuff.  For this you 
will have to switch to Young Link however, to do this simply use your Ocarina 
and play the Prelude of Light (up, right, up, right, left, up.)  This will 
take you right back to the Temple of Time where you can simply place your 
Master Sword into the slot and become young again.  Right after doing this, 
play the Bolero of Fire (down, A, down, A, right, down, right, down) to get 
right back to the Fire Temple where you just were.  These songs really come in 
handy.  If you don't have any bottled bugs then play the Minuet of Forest (A, 
up, left, right, left, right.)  When you arrive in the Sacred Forest Meadow, 
make your way all the way south to the Lost Woods.  Head south and climb that 
ladder, drop down in the middle where the hole leading to the Fairy Fountain 
is.  Heal yourself up and put a Fairy in three out of your four Empty Bottles, 
you'll need them for the Fire Temple.  Leave the fountain and head all the way 
south back to The Lost Woods.  From here go east, south, south, west and drop 
down the hole.  Slash the grass here and bugs will crawl out, capture some in 
one of your Empty Bottles and then play the Bolero of Fire to get back to the 
Death Mountain Crater again.  Approach the small hole just beside you and drop 
the bugs into it, a GOLD SKULLTULA [057] will pop out of the hole for you to 
defeat.  After doing this plant one of your remaining Magic Beans in the hole 
and then play the Prelude of Light to get back to the Temple of Time.  Remove 
the Master Sword to switch to Adult Link once again, then play the Bolero of 
Fire one final time to get back to the Fire Temple.  

Step onto the Magic Bean plant which is now fully grown and it will lift you 
up, after a couple of seconds you'll pass by the PIECE OF HEART [023] which 
you can get simply by hopping off and picking up.  If you stay on the platform 
it also takes you past Piece of Heart [008] which was somewhat difficult to 
get, so if you missed it before you can get it quite easily now.  Just jump 
into the lava afterward and you'll reappear back on the warp panel in front of 
the Fire Temple.  When you're ready to proceed, head north and drop down the 
area with the ladder.  At the bottom the path leads into the Fire Temple.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ________) 
      (, /       
        /___,    
     ) /         
+-- (_/   IRE TEMPLE ----------------------------------------------- 01.33 --+

Begin by making your way up the stairs at the very beginning.  On your right 
is a locked door so we won't be going that way, head left and go through the 
door there.  Listen to what Darunia has to say and then from the door look 
left and jump across to the pillar in that direction.  Keep jumping across 
until you reach a button which opens the gate beside you.  Speak to the Goron 
in the cage and then open the small treasure chest behind him to acquire a 
SMALL KEY.  Return to the entrance of this room and jump across to the pillar 
on the other side.  When you can go no further take out your Hookshot and fire 
it at the object to get across.  Up here are four pots, two of which contain 
Faeries.  If you followed my advice and put three of them in your Empty 
Bottles, take this opportunity now to catch a fourth one.  Head back once 
again to the door at the entrance of this room and go back out.

Use the Small Key which you just got to unlock the door in the other direction 
which takes you to an enormous room.  Make your way over to the right side of 
this room, feel free to walk on the lava for short periods of time, you won't 
take any damage doing that.  When you get to the door on the right side, take 
out one of your bombs and place it in front of the door.  Head through the 
door and step on the buttons which opens up the cage.  Speak to the Goron 
inside and then open the small treasure chest behind him to get a SMALL KEY.  

Return to the previous room and head across to the opposite side, there is a 
normal door there which leads to a hall and a cage with another Goron inside, 
open the cage and speak to him.  Open the small chest behind him to get yet 
another SMALL KEY.  Notice that outside of this hall, above the door that you 
just went through is one of those blue blocks.  Stand near it and take out 
your Ocarina, play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and the 
block appears right in front of you.  Climb up on top of the block and proceed 
through the door up top here.  Anyone familiar with past games may very well 
recognize the flying floor tiles, if you wish you can simply run around like 
an idiot trying to dodge them, or you can position yourself in a corner and 
just use your shield, that way is much easier.  Speaking of familiarities, 
that monster in the corner may look familiar as something which wants to suck 
up your equipment.  Stay the hell away from it at all times, kill it using 
attacks which work from a distance for example the Fairy Bow, bombs of the 
charge up Spin Attack you have.  When the monster has been defeated then you 
can kill the GOLD SKULLTULA [058].  That's all for here, leave the room.

Return to the enormous room with the bridge once again and head for the door 
on the left side, left being from the perspective of entering it from the 
previous room.  There is a locked door at the end of the bridge, use one of 
your two Small Keys to open it up.  Slide down the ramp leading through the 
fenced section and make a left.  Stand in the corner and aim your Fairy Bow 
directly up, there are two bats perched on the cage that you are going to want 
to kill before they knock you off while you're climbing around.  Start 
climbing up the chain link wall here and up onto the very thin edge.  Cross 
the little area where the bats were hiding to find three Recovery Hearts and 
then carefully make your way across the thin fence to the other side, get ready 
to do the Spin Attack and defeat even more bats here.  Drop down onto the 
pillar and push the block over the edge.  Wait until the block is safely on 
the ground and then jump down onto it.  There will be a blast of fire which 
launches it up into another room, use your Small Key and continue.

Make a left in this room and climb up, but be careful.  As you can see one of 
the torches is moving, it's not a torch but rather some kind of slug monster.  
Fortunately it only moves slowly so you should be able to kill it quite 
easily, just keep your distance, that's important.  There are two of them to 
eliminate here and once that is done, jump across to the other side where you 
see some kind of crystal thing.  Push the large block down to the ground and 
drop down with it, now pull the block until it cannot be moved any further in 
the little depression.  Press the A button to climb up onto the block and jump 
across to the platform in the direction of the door you came through.  From 
here you can begin to climb up the chain links.  Stop at the first platform 
and move over so that you are above that crystal thing, what you need to do is 
take out a bomb, drop it on the crystal and then quickly climb up to the fence 
which is on fire.  Climb it quickly before the crystal returns to its original 
state and the fire returns.  Follow it up to a door at the top.

Make a left when you enter this room and stay as close to the wall as you can.  
You're going to have to be very careful because there are large boulders 
rolling around this area randomly.  If you stay along the wall eventually you 
will come to a little enclosed area with a door, head through the door and 
step on the button to open up the cage for the Goron, then get the SMALL KEY 
from the chest behind the Goron.  Leave the room and listen carefully.  When 
you get outside press the L/Z button so that you are facing forward, away from 
the door you just came through.  Keep this perspective, we'll use it for 
directions.  Go up either path and then make a left, this should take you back 
in the direction you came from.  When you hit a wall go down and this should 
take you into kind of a dead end area.  Strike the wall here with your sword 
until it makes a different noise, then pull out your bombs and blast the wall.  
In here you will find a GOLD SKULLTULA [059].  Return to the entrance of this 
room and go in the opposite direction this time, staying close to the wall.

Eventually you should come across a cage with a button in front of it, step on 
the button to open the cage and then speak to the Goron.  Open the small 
treasure chest behind him to get another SMALL KEY.  Leave here and follow the 
left wall all the way around, you should reach a locked door.  If you 
eventually make it around to the other door, the one that brought you to the 
hallway then you've gone too far.  Turn around and try again, it should be 
easier this time because the door is kind of hidden from view when you come 
from the other direction.  Use one of your keys to get through this door.

The room below you here is that enormous room you went through previously, 
meaning that if you fall you'll have to go back through a bunch of areas 
you've already traversed.  The point is simple, don't fall.  Head across to 
the opposite side and use your last remaining Small Key on the door here.  In 
this next room you will notice when you jump down onto the metal area that a 
wall of fire is going to spike up behind you and begin moving.  Fortunately 
the wall doesn't move very fast at all so you have plenty of time to get 
across to the other side and jump up to the door.  Instead of going through 
the door however, note the metal gate on the other side of this ledge, you can 
climb over it and drop down to a large treasure chest which contains the 
DUNGEON MAP for this dungeon.  Take the only exit you can here and return to 
the previous room.  Make your way across to the door and go through this time.

Turn right and pull out your Ocarina.  Play the Scarecrow Song and a scarecrow 
will appear on the platform above you, use the Hookshot to get up there with 
him.  From here fire the Hookshot to get across to the other side onto the 
platform which rises up.  At the top you'll be in a room with a wall you can 
climb, at the top there you will come across GOLD SKULLTULA [60] on the wall, 
get it with the Hookshot.  Now climb up that wall and into the next room.  
Immediately take a look on your left here and you will find another GOLD 
SKULLTULA [061] just relaxing on the wall there.  Beside you there is a button 
on the ground.  Now note that after stepping the button the flames around the 
chest dissipate.  You can run up the slope but soon it becomes too steep, you 
have to run toward the wall and go around that way but it takes longer.  I 
think if you keep rolling you can make it but I imagine the intent was as 
follows.  Step on the button and then run to the bottom of the slope, whip out 
the Hookshot and fire it at the pillar at the top of the slope, this will pull 
you up and give you time to reach the small chest which happens to contain a 
HUGE RUPEE.  After this go all the way back to the boulder room upper area.

Jump across to the first platform and eliminate the fire slug.  On this 
platform note the enormous crack on the ground, it doesn't get much more 
obvious than this.  Place a bomb on the crack and drop down to the hall below 
which leads you to another Goron cage, step on the button to open it and then 
speak to the Goron.  Open the small chest to get a SMALL KEY and then climb up 
the chain link wall where you dropped down a moment ago.  Jump across to the 
corner ledge above you, in other words in the same direction you were moving 
when you first entered the room, then turn right and jump onto the pillar with 
the button on top of it.  From there just make your way across from one to the 
next until you reach the cage of the Goron, speak with him and then open the 
small chest to acquire another SMALL KEY.  Drop down and make your way back to 
the room with the moving wall of fire.  Cross this room until you reach those 
two pillars which have or had Recovery Hearts on top of them, go left to the 
edge then turn around and jump up to the platform with the locked door on it.

Try not to be too overwhelmed here, there's all sorts of stuff going on.  What 
you want to do is drop down and go to the right.  Notice of course all the 
pillars here, well between some of the pillars a wall of fire will pop up when 
you get close, so basically it's actually a maze, you just can't see the walls 
from a distance.  Stay along the right wall unless there is fire in your way, 
on this wall you will reach a door, go through it and open the large chest to 
acquire the COMPASS for the dungeon.   Next head across to the southwest area 
of this huge room, and here after going through the maze you will find a door 
leading north with a lock on it, use one of your two keys to go through this 
door.  When you come out the other side you'll see a button.  Navigate between 
the flames and step on this button, but it's only temporary so you need to 
move quick.  The wall of fire drops but you cannot run directly toward it, 
you'll be blocked.  Instead go around to reach it.  Don't try to open the door 
here, it's actually fake.  Instead blast it with a bomb and head through.

Time for a miniboss battle, the Flare Dancer can be a challenging foe but like 
most, if you know what you're doing then the battle becomes significantly 
easier.  The Flare Dancer pops out of the middle on fire and begins to shoot 
little bits of flame at you, dodging these should not be too difficult.  When 
the Flare Dancer actually jumps out of the middle that is your chance.  Make 
sure you have the Hookshot equipped and target the Flare Dancing using the L/Z 
button.  Hit the Hookshot button and you should be able to pull the actual, 
hit-able section of the boss out.  Now it is entirely harmless and you are 
free to begin slashing away.  What I like to do is hit the boss as much as 
possible before it gets away, then it's going to run around in a circle and 
come back to you.  When it sees you it will turn around and run again, often 
before you even have a chance to strike so do this.  As it runs around charge 
up the maximum power Spin Attack and move toward the boss, the instant it 
freaks out to turn around, let loose for good damage.  Do this about three or 
four times and the miniboss will quickly fall victim to your blade.

Jump onto the platform in the middle and it will rise up to the next room 
above.  Climb up the metal linked wall here and then look over the edge on 
your right.  Drop a bomb down onto the crystal and wait for it to explode, 
then climb up the other wall and go through the door.  In this room make your 
way over to the button but don't step on it.  Use your Fairy Bow to eliminate 
all the flaming bats in this room first as they will definitely get in your 
way.  Step on the button and run along the thin path that leads around to the 
large treasure chest at the top, you have more than enough time, it's just a 
matter of being careful not to fall that makes it difficult.  When you reach 
this large chest, open it up to get the MEGATON HAMMER.  This special item 
allows you to smash stuff, a very useful item indeed.  Drop back down to the 
entrance of this room and use it to smash the large tile there in front of 
you.  After doing that simply drop down from here into the room below.

Use the Megaton Hammer to smash the statue thing away from the door which it 
is blocking and then proceed through that door.  There are quite a few flaming 
bats in this room, four to be exact, eliminate all four of them without 
destroying the two boxes near the wall.  After doing that, equip your Megaton 
Hammer and pound the block in the centre of that lowered area, the one right 
between the torches.  When this is done, the whole ground should transform 
into a staircase.  Pick up one of the two small boxes and jump down to the 
bottom.  Step on the button and then set down the box on top of it.  Through 
the door there is another panel to smash with the hammer, this one drops you 
down.  Climb up on the box and up to the door beside the switch.  The switch 
is rusted, and as you know, the Megaton Hammer fixes everything so use it here 
to unlock the door with the power of violence.  Head through it now.

Stand on the edge here and equip your Ocarina, you need to play the Song of 
Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) to move the block across from you.  Jump 
across and grab the edge, then pound the rusted button down with the hammer to 
open the cage below you.  Speak with the Goron and then get the SMALL KEY from 
the tiny treasure chest behind him.  Climb up onto the large blue block and 
then head through the door that you entered this room from.  Jump across to 
the pillar thing and pound the panel down using your Megaton Hammer.  Ride it 
all the way down to the bottom and surprise surprise, you're back right at the 
beginning again.  Leave this room and head back toward the very first room of 
the temple.  From the entrance, take a look to the right of the stairs to see 
a statue.  Break this statue using your Megaton Hammer and proceed through.

This is just a good old fashioned, "defeat all the enemies" room.  The super 
charge up Spin Attack actually works quite well here.  In the room past this 
one, hold the R button to bring up your shield again and protect yourself from 
the floor tiles, then eliminate the huge blob from a distance.  There is a 
GOLD SKULLTULA [062] crawling around on the wall in here for you to kill, 
after that just head through the door here to encounter yet another Flare 
Dancer.  If for some reason you forget how to fight this thing, simply refer 
to the strategy above or to the boss section or something, I'm not going to 
put it all here again.  After defeating the enemy a small chest appears 
containing BOMBS, what a nice treat.  Head through the door and pound the 
button down using the Megaton Hammer.  Speak to the Goron and then open the 
chest behind him to acquire the BOSS KEY.  Take the south door back to the 
entrance once again and climb the stairs, then through the left door.  Jump 
across in this room and use the Boss Key on the door on the opposite side.

I sincerely doubt you will have too much trouble with this boss at all, but of 
course it's still important to know how to fight him, because in the end 
knowing how to fight is what makes it so easy.  Knowledge is power.  Forget 
absolutely every weapon and item for this battle with the one exception being 
the Megaton Hammer, it's your only weapon.  There are nine holes in the field 
here and the boss will randomly pop out of one of them.  You can tell which 
one he is going to pop out of by seeing where the flames after gathering.  
When the boss pops out, keep your distance a bit, he will sweep around the 
proximity with some kind of flaming dragon hair.  After that you have a window 
to run up and slam him with the Megaton Hammer.  Doing so will make him swing 
his flaming hair one more time and then drop, allowing you to go in with the 
hammer for a second hit.  After doing this he will pop out of the next hole, 
but you cannot attack.  He will use one of two attacks, either he will fly 
around the field breathing fire at you or he will drop rocks from above.  The 
fire one can be dodged simply by running and not stopping, being careful never 
to stay in his line of vision.  The rock one is even easier, they make large 
shadows before they fall so stay away from the shadows.  After this the 
pattern repeats with one subtle difference.  The dragon may pretend to pop up 
in one place and then appear somewhere else, keep this in mind, but it really 
never makes much difference.  About five or six successful runs for the win.
Grab the HEART CONTAINER which appears where the skull disappears and then 
step into the warp portal for a scene where you acquire the FIRE MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ------------------------------------------------ 01.34 --+

Your destination now is the Ice Cavern, there are a couple of places you have 
to pass through first of course before you are able to get there.  Make your 
way on the Hyrule Field, east of Kakariko Village to reach Zora's River again.  
When you get into Zora's River, make your way up the path until your come to 
the Magic Bean platform.  Step on this platform and allow it to take you up to 
that area, high above the others with the hole in the ground and where there 
was a Cucco wandering around previously, at the top of that long ladder.  The 
Magic Bean platform doesn't actually stop there so you are going to have to 
jump off as you pass by.  Once you land, take a look to the southwest, if you 
don't see anything crawling on the wall then play the Sun's Song so that it 
changes to night, the GOLD SKULLTULA [063] will only appear on the wall during 
the night.  After this jump across again and jump down to the east.

Make your way along toward the end of the river, and when you reach the bridge 
which takes you across, just before those funny land bridges, stand on the 
normal bridge and look to the north.  Look up at the top of the wall during 
the night and there you should see the Skulltula.  You cannot reach it with 
your Hookshot normally, what you have to do is hop up onto the fence below it, 
and use you Hookshot from there.  This way you will be able to reach the GOLD 
SKULLTULA [064].  From here make your way up across the land bridges and stand 
in front of the waterfall, play Zelda's Lullaby to open it up and go through.

Zora's Domain it seems is completely covered in ice, so it's going to make 
moving around here quite a bit different than when you first arrived.  There 
really isn't much to do here at the moment so head up to the king's room and 
take the path around behind which leads to Zora's Fountain.  Instead of seeing 
Jabu Jabu here you will find a slab of ice which runs up and out over the 
water.  Run along this ice and jump down to the ice platforms floating in the 
water.  The ones which lead east take you in the direction of a Piece of 
Heart, however it isn't meant to be easy.  First of all the platforms are 
tilting all over the place, second of all they are also slippery.  Lastly the 
ones going east are spinning around in circles.  What you want to do basically 
is jump to one of the spinning platforms and just stay on it until it goes 
around, and then jump to get the PIECE OF HEART [024].  Note that if you fall 
off, the only platform you can climb back up onto is the very first one that 
you jump down onto from the ice slab at the beginning.  After you've got the 
heart piece, jump west across the rotating platforms and then up to the cave.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____
        (, /
          /
      ___/__
+-- (__ /    CE CAVERN --------------------------------------------- 01.35 --+

Welcome to the Ice Cavern, unlike the temples there is no song to warp here, 
no special medallion to collect, however it is very important that you make it 
through this place, so let's get moving.  The icicles that you come across 
right at the beginning can be destroyed using your sword.  Be careful of the 
ones on the ceiling which will drop and try to hit you.  In the room that you 
come to there is a spinning spikey thing that you have to watch out for and 
then three difficult enemies in the middle of the room.  These enemies have an 
ice breath attack which freezes you and does quite a bit of damage.  What you 
need to do is keep running around and until they use they breath attack and 
then run up to one of them and start attacking, maybe two of them, just don't 
get hit by the breath.  There are about four of them total in this room to get 
rid of.  One of them moves around and is partially invisible, simply kill all 
four and the door will open.  Follow that path to the next area.

Here you will find some kind of spinning ice fan.  Do you see those Rupees in 
the centre of the room?  Well not only will they give you five Rupees when 
collected, but you are also required to collect all of them in order to 
proceed to the next area.  We'll begin by picking up the three in the path of 
the spinning fan, do that now.  From the entrance where you came in, go left 
and break the three icicles to find the fourth Silver Rupee.  Lastly from the 
entrance go right and climb up to the gate, you can jump off from here to 
acquire the fifth and final Silver Rupee which will open the gate.  Before you 
leave the room, head back over to where you broke the icicles and look up on 
the wall, there you will see a GOLD SKULLTULA [065].  Run across to the other 
side and climb up to the gate which is now open, proceed down the hall.

Wait for the ice monster to use the frozen breath and then run up to it and 
slash while avoiding the spike traps which also happen to be moving around in 
this area.  The room after this is quite large, what you want to do is climb 
up the first ledge and whip out your Fairy Bow.  There is a new type of enemy 
in this room, bats covered in blue fire which freezes you.  The difference 
between them and the normal flaming bats is that once they hit you, they 
remain on fire.  Take all three of them out with your Fairy Bow and then cross 
the path to kill the statue and find three Recovery Hearts there.  Jump back 
across and then up to the pillar and from there to the blue torch.  This 
special flame, the BLUE FIRE can be captured in bottles.  I would recommend, 
assuming you don't have any big important healing items in there, that you 
capture some Blue Fire in all your bottles, if not all then at least three.

Cross the walkway and you'll come to a large hunk of red ice with a large 
treasure chest frozen inside it.  Use the Blue Flame to melt the ice and open 
the chest to get the DUNGEON MAP for this semi-dungeon.  Turn around and jump 
to that pillar with the red ice hunk on it.  Use the Blue Fire to melt it and 
then beak the pot, you should find a PURPLE RUPEE inside.  Return to the torch 
and refill those two bottle with Blue Fire, then return to the room with the 
spinning ice fan.  Jump down and look to your left for a large wall of red 
ice, use one of your Blue Flames to melt it and then head down that path.  

When you reach the end, run around to try and make all the ceiling ice fall, 
and make sure that you have the Fairy Bow equipped.  Break all the ice around 
this area and be ready to eliminate the many bats which are hiding around 
here.  Melt the red ice on your right and then open the large chest to acquire 
the dungeon's COMPASS.  Next melt the red ice on the left side and pick up the 
PIECE OF HEART [025].  Equip your Hookshot and then look up above where you 
got the Piece of Heart to find GOLD SKULLTULA [066].  Refill your bottles 
using the Blue Fire in this room, and then backtrack to the room with the 
giant spinning ice fan.  Head to the opposite side and use your Blue Fire on 
the red ice wall there.  It'll lead you to a very large room again.

Make sure you have the Fairy Bow equipped because there are at least three 
bats in this room, if not more.  From the entrance go left and then look left 
to find yet another GOLD SKULLTULA [067] crawling around on this wall here, be 
careful though because the ground doesn't entirely cover the area and it's 
very possible to fall down and wind up at the beginning of the dungeon again.  
From the start of this room go to the back right corner and pick up the first 
Silver Rupee.  From the start of this room once again, approach the ice block 
and grab hold of it with the A button.  Push it west in the direction you are 
moving when you enter the room and hen climb on top of it and get the second 
Silver Rupee from the pillar.  After this push the block north and then climb 
up it, use the Blue Fire to melt the red ice and get the third Silver Rupee.

Drop down and push the block west, meaning it will slide off the cliff, no big 
deal because it will automatically respawn at the entrance to this room.  This 
time push the block south and climb up onto it to get the next Silver Rupee.  
Push it west following this and climb up onto it, you should be able to pick 
up the fifth Silver Rupee easily which opens the gate.  Now push the block 
south off the cliff.  When it respawns at the entrance to the room, push it 
west, then south, then west.  Jump from the block up to the blue torch and 
some bottles with Blue Fire.  Jump back down and push it south, then east, 
then south.  From here you should be able to jump from the block up to the 
hall where the gate opened.  Follow the path to the end and melt the red ice, 
both pots just contain a single Recovery Heart.  Proceed through the door.

There's kind of a miniboss here but not really, more of a normal enemy.  
You've fought similar ones before, use your shield constantly and attack the 
wolf only after he attacks you.  The jumping A button attack works great 
because you don't have to manually lower you shield to use it, just hit A 
after defending against one of his attacks when the wolf's guard is down.  
When the wolf is defeated a large chest appears in the middle of the room, it 
contains IRON BOOTS.  These are obviously going to be useful for water, since 
you can now float right down to the bottom.  After this there is another scene 
where you learn the SERENADE OF WATER.  Now equip the Iron Boots and walk into 
the little water pool behind the large chest, you'll float down to the bottom 
and be able to walk through the door.  This brings you back near the entrance 
of the dungeon, unequip the Iron Boots, make sure you have at least one Blue 
Fire in you inventory and then leave the Ice Cavern for Zora's Domain.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ------------------------------------------------ 01.36 --+

Assuming you're coming back from the direction of the Ice Cavern, the Zora 
king will be right here in front of you encased in red ice.  Use the Blue Fire 
to free him and then speak to the king.  As a way of saying thank you, he will 
give you the ZORA TUNIC which allows you to breathe underwater.  With this in 
hand it's time to leave and return to the Hyrule Field.  Play Epona's Song to 
summon the horse and then make your way to Lon Lon Ranch but don't go inside.  
Instead ride the horse northwest of the ranch and step beside the lone yellow 
tree.  When you get off you should feel your controller rumbling when you 
stand beside the tree.  Place a bomb there at the base and it will blast a 
hole in the ground.  Down here is a small body of water, what you need to do 
is swim into it and equip the Iron Boots.  You will sink down to the bottom 
and acquire a PIECE OF HEART [026].  Leave this area and head for Lake Hylia.

When you arrive there, make your way over to the laboratory thing beside the 
bridge and step on the Magic Bean plant.  When it rises up, jump off 
immediately onto the roof of the house and climb the ladder.  Before climbing 
the ladder be sure to equip the Fairy Bow and aim up, eliminate all birds 
which might potentially knock you off the ladder.  At the top here you will 
find PIECE OF HEART [027].  Jump down and get on the Magic Bean platform once 
again, this time stay on it and it will bring you over to the Fishing Pond.

Fishing as Adult Link is quite similar to fishing as Young Link, the one main 
difference is that the fish are bigger.  Your goal is to catch the fish in the 
very middle of the pond, the enormous one swimming around the broken logs and 
stuff, it's pretty much always in the same place.  This fish takes forever to 
actually bite your hook, I must have cast at least two dozen times or more.  
It eventually did bite, sometime during the daylight hours.  This fish should 
be at least fifteen pounds or more, which will net you the reward GOLDEN SCALE 
and allow you to dive underwater for nine seconds as opposed to six.

Now that you have this, leave the Fishing Pond and head for the laboratory 
again, but this time actually go inside.  There is a creepy old man in here 
beside the small pond.  Equip the Iron Boots and jump into the water, have 
your Hookshot equipped as well.  At the very bottom, roll into the crate down 
here and a GOLD SKULLTULA [068] will pop out, you can kill it using the 
Hookshot.  Note the three Red Rupees down here, feel free to collect them as 
well.  Now swim to the very bottom of this pond using your new Gold Scale, not 
you Iron Boots and speak to the man to acquire PIECE OF HEART [028].  

The Water Temple is located in the middle of Lake Hylia, on the north side of 
the centre island but underwater.  You need to equip the Iron Boots and the 
Hookshot and then jump down to the gate.  Fire the Hookshot up at the blue 
diamond above the gate and it will open, allow you to enter the Water Temple.
______________________________________________________________________________


  __       __)
 (, )  |  /
    | /| /
    |/ |/
+-- /  | ATER TEMPLE ----------------------------------------------- 01.37 --+

Remove your Iron Boots and enter the first room of the Water Temple.  Make a 
right as you jump into the water and swim over to the right side.  Climb out 
of the water into the hall and it will quickly lead to a small square of 
water.  Jump in and equip the Iron Boots to sink down to the bottom.  Here you 
will find a large block in the wall, grab hold of it and pull it out as far as 
you possibly can.  After doing this go back the way you came and unequip the 
boots so that you float back up.  At the top, climb out of the water and equip 
the Iron Boots again.  Walk to the edge before the water, and drop down 
slowly, you should be able to land on the ledge below where you will find two 
pots.  Follow this hallway all the way down to the end and use your Hookshot 
to eliminate the enemies here.  After doing this Hookshot up to the panel 
above you past the spikes, and then from there aim it up toward the ceiling 
where you will see more panels you can hook on to.  Up here there is a crystal 
and a large chest which cannot be opened due to a water spray.  What you need 
to do (well actually there are tons of solutions) is stand beside the water 
spray and chest, then fire an arrow from your Fairy Bow at the crystal.  Open 
the large chest while it is momentarily available to get the COMPASS.

Leave this area and return to the main room once again.  Equip your Iron Boots 
and drop off the edge a second time, this time you will fall all the way down 
to the bottom.  Run along the path beside you when you land and at the end you 
will come across Princess Ruto once again, the Zora girl whom you first met in 
Jabu-Jabu's Belly.  Equip the Kokiri Boots and swim all the way up to the top 
until your surface out of the water again.  Proceed through the door and 
you'll find yourself face to face with a bunch of spike traps.  They rill 
around the room after you trying to chase you down.  What you need to do is 
simply run away from them until they transform into small rocks, this is their 
alternate form.  While in this form you can simply perform a spinning sword 
attack to destroy them all and spawn a large treasure chest.  Inside the chest 
you will find the DUNGEON MAP for this dungeon.  Return to the previous room.

In this room on one of the walls there is a Triforce symbol plaque that you 
can see.  What you want to do is stand beside this symbol and play Zelda's 
Lullaby on your Ocarina.  This will lower the water level.  Now jump all the 
way back down to the bottom again.  Break the pots here to refill your arrow 
stock and note the two torches on the wall which you have to light.  Stand at 
the entrance of this room so that the torch in the middle is aligned in your 
view with one of the torches in the corner and then equip your Fairy Bow.  
You're done this before, when you fire an arrow through a flame the arrow 
becomes engulfed in flames itself, this is how you light the torches in the 
corners.  Once the torches are lit the door opens up and you can go through.

This room contains three new enemies, clam-type enemies which cannot be hurt 
while their shells are closed.  When you get close to one of them they open up 
but then after a split second, they charge at you.  What you have to do is run 
up to them and be ready to swing your sword right away before they charge, it 
only takes a single hit to kill them.  After all three of them are dead then a 
small chest appears in the centre of the room containing a SMALL KEY.  Leave 
this room and return to the main room once again.  Head over to the west side 
where you will find a block that you can climb up on top of, and another 
larger block which you can grab hold of and push down the hallway.  At the end 
of the hall this block drops down into the water, jump into the water after it 
and equip your iron Boots so that you fall to the bottom.  Run to the end of 
the path and equip the Kokiri Boots to float up to the top.

Here in this room you will find a crystal on your right that you can hit, 
which makes the water jet in the middle of the room rise up very high.  Your 
goal is to jump into this water jet and them from the top of it across to the 
other side.  If you happen to fall then note there is a panel on the side you 
entered from that you can latch with the Hookshot.  On the other side of the 
room head through the door.  In this room you need to equip the Iron Boots and 
then drop down into the water.  Notice while standing at the bottom if you 
look up a to the west, there is a Hookshot panel on the wall there beside the 
gate.  You need to position yourself somewhere where you can hit this, but 
don't grab it right yet.  Note that up and to the left of that there is a 
dragon's head statue with a crystal in its mouth, hitting the crystal opens 
the gate.  What you have to do is find somewhere where you can hook the panel 
beside the gate and then stand there, but aim your Hookshot up at the crystal 
first and then go up to the gate.  Quickly approach the shell enemies and 
unequip the Iron Boots to float up above them.  Here you will find a small 
chest containing a SMALL KEY.  Afterward hit the crystal switch and then jump 
back into the water with your Iron Boots equipped, back to the entrance.

On your way back you may find at the end, that the water isn't high enough to 
climb out of, you need to have the Iron Boots equipped and Hookshot out of 
there.  Back in the main room once again run over to the south side and run 
down the south hall to the end where you will see a crack in the ground.  
Equip your bombs and place a bomb on this crack in the ground.  Equip your 
Iron Boots and jump down into the water.  Defeat the shell enemy in front of 
you using the Hookshot and then float up to the top on the other side.  There 
should be a button in front of you, step on this button to make the water rise 
up further and then use your Hookshot to snag that panel off ahead of you.  
Notice there is a closed gate with a crystal behind it here and you cannot 
open the gate.  Since arrows, Hookshot, etc don't work to hit the switch, all 
that remains is the power of magic!  Stand by the gate, twirl your analog 
stick 360 degrees and press the attack button to hit the crystal, if this 
doesn't work then simply hold down the attack button until your sword turns 
orange.  Eliminate the GOLD SKULLTULA [069] here.  Equip your Iron Boots again 
and as always, now you need to make your way back to the main room again.

When you reach the main room, straight ahead of you is a locked door, open it 
using one of your two Small Keys.  You need to Hookshot your way up this area, 
the first panel is located on the north side of one of the platforms above, so 
run up to the north wall and turn around, then look up to see it.  There is a 
Triforce symbol here, stand beside it and play Zelda's Lullaby in order to 
raise the water level up even higher.  Leave through the door beside you here 
to return to the main room.  Turn right and run over to the east side with 
your Iron Boots equipped.  Drop down to the bottom and continue east down the 
hall.  When you reach the room where you first met Ruto, equip the Kokiri 
Boots and float up to the level above you.  Blast a hole in the wall here 
where the large crack is and open the chest for a SMALL KEY.  Head back to the 
main room again and float up to the very top on the north side to reach the 
door where you came out of most recently.  There are bars across it, but an 
unlit torch beside it.  Fire an arrow through this torch to unlock the door.

Back in the room where you raised the water level once again, equip the Iron 
Boots and float all the way down to the bottom,  note that beside the door 
where you came in there is now a path leading down much deeper.  At the bottom 
make your way along the hall to the end where you will find a crystal.  Hit 
the crystal with your Hookshot to make quite a few enemies drop down from 
above.  You need to kill all of them using only your Hookshot but don't worry, 
it's easier than it sounds.  Hitting the spikes once will make them revert to 
their stone form after which you can kill them easily, the shell enemies are 
no different than they were before.  After defeating all of them a hole in the 
ceiling opens up on the other side, swim up there and acquire another SMALL 
KEY.  Return to the previous room and float all the way up to the top, there 
is a door there back to the main room.  Run over to the west side and jump
