across the water to reach the locked door there, proceed through it.

Stand on the jet of water on the side of this room and fire an arrow at the 
crystal on the other side.  It will shoot up and bring you up into the air 
with it.  At the top jump off and proceed through the door.  Stand beside the 
Triforce symbol on the wall here and play Zelda's Lullaby.  Now that the water 
level is at its highest point once again jump down into the main room and look 
at the west wall once again, there door is locked again, but it's not actually 
the same door since the water level is higher.  Unlock this west door and head 
through.  Be careful of the two bats at the entrance of this room, you can 
kill them relatively easily with the Hookshot or the Fairy Bow.  Drop down 
onto the first platform and then drop down once again onto the one below it.  
Here's how this works, on the other side there are platforms which move 
downward along with the waterfall and then go around behind it underwater 
before popping out at the top again.  You cannot stand on them once they are 
covered by the waterfall it will knock you off, so your goal is to hook onto 
one while it's moving down and then when you get onto it, quickly hook onto 
the one above, and then maybe the one above that, all the way until you are 
able to hook onto the panel at the top that connects to the other side of the 
room.  Use your last remaining Small Key on this door and go through it.

Equip your Fairy Bow and fire an arrow at the crystal in the middle, after 
doing this the water will rise up along with the enemies (kill them to get 
them out of your way.)  All right, let's start by facing west and firing the 
Hookshot so you grab the panel on the statue and reach the next platform.  You 
cannot hook over to the platform to the north for two reason, if you hook onto 
the statue panel you'll fall in the water and if you hook on the wall panel 
you'll be stick.  The solution is to hit the crystal in the middle again so 
the water level drops and then hook onto the panel on the wall to the north.  
Climb over the statue and hit the crystal in the middle again.  Hook onto the 
statue to the east and then hit the crystal yet again, for the fourth time.  
Jump onto the statue head and while standing on it, shoot the crystal for a 
fifth and final time, it will rise up with you on it.  Use your Fairy Bow in 
order to eliminate the disgusting creature on the north side and then use your 
Hookshot to hook onto the panel on the ceiling which will carry you over the 
spikes.  If you need magic refill (which you are going to) then simply break 
the two pots here beside the door.  Head through the door when you're ready.

This room is extremely odd, not only is it surrounded by invisible walls, but 
you can also walk on water here.  Make your way across to the north side where 
you will find a barred door and nothing you can do about it.  Turn around and 
move back toward the tree, there you will see a shadow, it's Shadow Link!  
This miniboss is extremely difficult but very beatable.  He might end up 
hitting you a fair amount, but that shouldn't stop you from doing the same, 
the battle requires patience and good dodging/blocking ability.  First of all 
Shadow Link will mimic every attack that you do, so if you target him with L/Z 
and then keep using the overhead swing, you'll clash swords until the end of 
time.  What you really want to do is avoid using L/Z targeting, that way he 
won't be able to line up his deflection swings as easily.  Running up to 
Shadow Link and pounding the B button actually works surprisingly well, you'll 
probably find you get hit a fair number of times doing this but you should hit 
him as well just as often if not more than you get hit yourself.  If you don't 
like the Kamikaze method there are always other ways.  You can try to come up 
from the side with a horizontal slash or a vertical slash on an angle which he 
cannot deflect from.  You can try blocking his attacks and quickly countering 
with one of your own.  I find the easiest time to hit him without actually 
being hit yourself, is if you can land an overhead swing just a split second 
after he does one, this is difficult but you won't be hit.  One thing to note 
is to avoid using the stab, he'll jump on your sword and hit you every time.  
After Shadow Link is defeated the room returns to normal and you can go north.

Open the large treasure chest in this room and Link will acquire the LONGSHOT, 
an upgrade for your Hookshot which allows you to hook onto thing which are 
twice as far away.  I'm sure you had probably noticed by now that the 
Hookshot's range wasn't exactly the greatest thing in the world.  Notice that 
one of the floor panels behind the chest here is different from the rest, it's 
one of those blue blocks you've seen before which can be displaced simply by 
standing beside it and playing the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, 
down).  After doing that then drop down the new hole to the room below.

When you enter this room, begin by equipping the Iron Boots as you are 
definitely going to need them.  Jump into the river and make your way down 
the path.  When you get close to the whirlpool, stay close to the wall and 
unequip the Iron Boots to get past them, then quickly equip them again.  Walk 
up to the next whirlpool and use your Longshot to get the GOLD SKULLTULA [070] 
off of the wall.  After that put down the Longshot and run past the whirlpool 
as close to the wall as you can, you shouldn't have to take off the Iron Boots 
for this one.  For the next one you do, unequip them but stay as close as you 
can to the right wall and swim to the ledge with the two pots.  After climbing 
up there equip the Iron Boots and drop down, walk along the bottom where the 
path isn't sloping and climb up the other ledge in front of you.  Take a look 
at the wall and fire an arrow from the Fairy Bow at the eyeball switch, then 
jump across to grab the ledge where the gate opened and open the small chest 
to get a SMALL KEY.  After doing this then jump down into the room you've 
visited before and make your way back to the main room.  You don't have to use 
the Longshot for anything this time as the water level is high enough.

We getting there, gradually.  What you are going to want to do this time is 
equip the Iron Boots and head over to the east side of the room.  Make your 
way along the path here and float up to the top where the Triforce symbol is 
that you first used to lower the water level to the bottom.  Return to the 
main room and enter the door on the south side of the centre structure, the 
one which was locked before.  Use your Longshot to get up to the Triforce 
symbol on the wall but don't raise the water level.  Instead use the Longshot 
and aim it up toward the ceiling, off in the distance is a GOLD SKULLTULA 
[071] but since the reach of this thing is so good, you can get it from here.  
After doing this shoot over to the door and head through it.  

Make your way around to the south side and note the eyeball switch on the wall 
here.  Equip your Fairy Bow and fire an arrow at the switch to open the gate 
and reveal a Longshot panel.  Use your Longshot to hit this and swing across.  
Follow the path to the end and push the block forward until it drops into 
place.  Make a right and open the small chest to acquire a SMALL KEY.  Turn 
around 180 degrees and run to the end, use your Longshot to hook up to the top 
and get back to the front hall.  Jump down and enter the centre structure door 
at the bottom.  Longshot up to the Triforce panel and plate the song beside it 
in order to raise the water level to medium.  After doing this leave and jump 
across to the mid-level west door.  Ride the jet of water up to the top again 
and play the song beside the Triforce panel so that the water is at the very 
top.  Jump down and take the western door at the top.  This will bring you 
back to the room with the waterfall and the moving platforms.  On one of the 
walls here you will find GOLD SKULLTULA [072], make sure to get that one.  

Return to the main room and equip the Iron Boots, now jump down into the 
water.  On the very bottom head down the north path and unequip your boots.  
Climb out of the water and use your Longshot to get across over top of the 
spikes to the door, use one of your Small Keys to open it up.  In this next 
room use whatever arrows you have left in your Fairy Bow to eliminate the 
enemies standing on the water here and then just jump in the water and swim 
straight across as quickly as possible to be safe.  In this next room, jump 
down and be ready to defeat little aquatic monsters which jump out at you.  
When they are taken care of, bomb the walls here on both the east and west 
sides.  Go down through the broken wall on the left side and push the block 
until it goes no further, go through the east side and pull it out, then go 
back to the west side and push it until it falls in the water and lands on the 
switch.  When the water rises up then swim over and take the west exit.

In this room you're going to want to eliminate the enemies on the far side of 
the room before doing anything, as they will almost surely get in your way.  
Step on the button to raise the water jets up and jump across them, you only 
need to jump across two: the first one and then the left one.  Go through the 
door quickly before the timer runs out and the gate drops down again.  Equip 
your Iron Boots and jump into the water after a rock rolls by, get the GOLD 
SKULLTULA [073] from the wall and drop down under the water below it.  At the 
end of this hall, take off the boots and float up, then open the locked door 
here.  This enormous coloured chest of course contains the BOSS KEY for the 
dungeon.  Re-equip the Iron Boots and make your way back.  Follow the water 
stream where the boulders drop to land in a familiar room, and then from there 
make your way back to the main room.  Swim up the very top here.

On the north side the statue has risen up and has a target on it, so Longshot 
over there.  Equip two Empty Bottles and then break the pots beside the door, 
capture the two faeries in the bottles before heading through.  This room is 
deceptively difficult, but you can get through it no problem.  The ground is 
sloped so once you start moving up you can't stop or you'll slide back down.  
Stand on the far left side and let the spike trap pass you.  It will go over 
to the far right side, then change direction and come back.  Just when you see 
it coming back in your direction out of the corner of your eye, that's when to 
start moving up, you'll make it easily to the door leading to the boss.

When you enter this room nothing really happens, only after dropping into the 
water and climbing one of the ladders to one of the four central pillars does 
the boss, Morpha, actually reveal itself.  The boss is just some weird little 
red amoeba but it has some serious power.  Basically the boss only has one 
attack, it creates weird tentacle like water things that come up and try to 
hit you.  If one of them lands on you, you take about one heart worth of 
damage, but if one of them grabs hold of you, it can deal upwards of three 
hearts, this attack is very powerful!  Fortunately like I said this is the 
only attack and it's extremely easy to dodge, all you have to do is stay in 
motion.  Don't attempt to fight this boss on the central pillars, jump to the 
outside and move around, it's much, much easier.  After one of the tentacles 
drops down, Morpha will begin to move back and forth through the tentacle.  
Take aim near the end of the tentacle with your Longshot and fire at Morpha to 
suck it out of the water and leave it open to attack.  The reason I say to 
fire at the end of the tentacle is because it's easier to aim and hit Morpha 
when it is changing direction.  After getting it out of the water you can 
attack with your sword.  I recommend using the Spin Attack, the one where you 
press B after spinning the analog stick.  Not only is it powerful but you have 
a good chance of hitting Morpha more than once if you keep doing it, perhaps 
even three times.  After you have hit the boss enough, more water tentacles 
pop up that the boss doesn't appear in, so just dodge them and keep an eye out 
for the real ones that Morpha actually uses for your chance to attack.  After 
the battle is over, get the HEART CONTAINER from between two pillars down at 
the bottom and then step on the warp portal to receive the WATER MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ YRULE ------------------------------------------------ 01.38 --+

After you have completed the temple and find yourself in Lake Hylia again, 
play the Sun's Song after the scene is over so that it becomes night time.  
Now after doing this, stand on the northeast side of this little island you 
are on and aim the Longshot up at the top of the tree.  Use it to hook onto 
the top and pull yourself up so that you land on the long branch.  There is a 
Gold Skulltula here beside you but you cannot get it from here, cross the 
branch to the end and turn around.  Use your Longshot to aim at it and claim 
the GOLD SKULLTULA [074].  Now drop down again and wait until the morning.

Take a look at the little stone plate beside you.  It says that when the water 
in the Lake Rises, aim for the morning sun.  Do as it says, just after you 
hear the cock-a-doodle-doo of morning, take aim with your Fairy Bow and fire 
up at the sun.  When you do, a special item will descend down from above.  Swim 
over and pick up the FIRE ARROW.  This is a special type of arrow you can 
shoot whenever you want, with a tip of flames.  Equipping the Fire Arrow is 
the same as equipping anything else, it will just automatically equip the 
Fairy Bow on the same slot.  Once you have that then it's time to make a 
return trip to being Young Link once again.  Equip the Ocarina and play the 
Prelude of Light (up, right, up, right, left, up) to warp back to the Temple 
of Time.  When you arrive place your Master Sword in the stone to head back.

Now that you are young again there are a couple of things for you to do.  
First of all, leave the Temple of Time and head up through the Market toward 
Hyrule Castle.  Sneak past all the guards here and make your way all the way 
around to the garden entrance where Talon was sleeping near the beginning of 
the game.  South of this place there is a large tree which stands by itself, 
stand beside it and play the Song of Storms (A, down, up, A, down, up) to 
reveal a secret hole.  Drop down this hole and bomb all three weak walls here.  
Behind one of them you will find a GOLD SKULLTULA [075].  You can get it using 
your Boomerang since Young Link is unable to use the Longshot.  

Time to put that Song of Storms to another good use.  What you want to do now 
is head over to Zora's River, east of Kakariko Village.  Remember where you 
stood on the log and had the frogs looking at you from in the water.  Stand on 
this log and play the Song of Storms (A, down, up, A, down, up) to have them 
give you a PIECE OF HEART [029] as a reward.  After doing this stay where you 
are an play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and they will 
pop up again to give you a PURPLE RUPEE.  After this every single frog should 
have grown in size, whip out the Ocarina and they will let you play a game.

This minigame, which can only be played after you have made all the frogs grow 
in size, consists of you playing notes that correspond to each specific frog.  
A butterfly will appear over the frogs' heads and you have to press the button 
for that frog extremely quickly or the game ends.  It's actually quite 
difficult even when you know the mapping (from left to right, the frog buttons 
are A, left, down, up, right).  Fortunately the solution is always the same, 
meaning all you really have to do is read off of this screen and press the 
buttons quickly, you don't even have to look at the frogs.  The solution is as 
follows: (A, left, right, down, left, right, down, A, down, A, down, right, 
left, A).  Doing this will net you a reward, the PIECE OF HEART [030].

With that out of the way now we're going to go and get the item we actually 
came back in time to acquire in the first place.  What you need to do now is 
head back to Kakariko Village and enter the windmill shack.  Play the Song of 
Storms in there (A, down, up, A, down, up) and it will cause the windmill to 
speed up, draining the water out of the well.  Once that happens you can leave 
the room and jump down to reach the sub-dungeon, Bottom of the Well.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      OTTOM OF THE WELL ----------------------------------- 01.39 --+

There is a small crawlspace in front of you, get on your hands and knees and 
crawl through here.  Drop down over the edge and kill the Skulltula which 
stands in front of you.  The wall past the Skulltula doesn't even have a 
physical property, you can just walk right through it.  In this next room, 
make a right at the fork and begin down the path with water in it.  Be careful 
as a floating green skull might come up from behind.  At the other end you 
will find a Triforce symbol on the ground, stand on it and pull out your 
Ocarina.  Play Zelda's Lullaby while standing on this thing to lower the water 
level.  Return to the entrance of this room and drop down the hole, open the 
small chest to acquire some BOMBS and then crawl into the crawlspace.  Defeat
the Skulltula and climb up the vines on the wall, then go through the door.

There are four hand things coming up in the corners or this room, when you get 
close to the hands they grab at you and hold you in place.  When this happens 
you can wiggle the analog and tab the A button to get out of the grip.  It 
also has another side effect, it makes the boss appear.  The boss is a big 
weird blob man.  To defeat him here's all you have to do.  Target him with the 
L/Z button and get close.  When he lowers his head down press the A button to 
do a Jump Attack for a good damage.  At that point he'll go underground again 
and you have to let yourself be grabbed by one of the hands in order to lure 
him out.  Repeat this process a few times and the boss will be defeated.

After the miniboss is defeated, open the large chest to acquire the LENS OF 
TRUTH.  You'll need this for the upcoming dungeons.  The rest of this 
particular dungeon is entirely optional, so feel free to leave at this point, 
because you have the item you came for.  Head back to the room where you first 
went through the fake wall.  Face in the same direction as if you just came 
through it and equip the Lens of Truth, ahead of you will be a large open 
wall, but that's not the one you want.  Check both left and right of this just 
a couple steps down the halls in both directions to find other hidden doors 
leading to two chests which both contain SMALL KEY.  The one down the right 
hall is actually found only if you go around the corner a bit.  

Once you have both keys return to the entrance of this room once again and go 
left.  Follow the hall around the corner and through the hidden door on the 
right to find a large chest containing the COMPASS.  Return to the hall again 
and make your way down to the end.  Turn left and you'll drop down a hole.  
When you reach the middle here, collect as many Silver Rupees as you possibly 
can and then go down the hall past the two orange flame torches and through 
the rocks to find a large chest with the DUNGEON MAP in it.  Climb the long 
ladder and get the remaining Silver Rupees, the door at the top will open.  

Once again return to the first fake wall in the main room and go left.  Half 
way down this hallway make another left and move toward the gate, then jump 
down in front of it.  Open the small chest here to get a RECOVERY HEART and 
then climb the vines behind the chest.  In this next room there is a mummy you 
have to defeat.  After doing that whip out the Deku Sticks (remember those) 
and light the torches beside the coffins.  You'll get a lot of bats flying at 
you but one of them will contain another SMALL KEY.  Once you have that return 
to the main room again.  Enter the middle area of the main room, there are 
four entrances to this thing, they are all hidden, but easy to find.

When you get in here, check the one jail cell in the corner for a small chest 
containing DEKU NUT.  Open the locked door on the northwest side, defeat the 
flower and the possessed urns and then use your Boomerang to get GOLD 
SKULLTULA [076].  Next enter the northeast room and defeat all the bats around 
here.  Equip the Lens of Truth to see the path going around the edge.  Walk 
along this path and use your Boomerang to get GOLD SKULLTULA [077].Back in the 
previous room once again, head back out into the hallway.  At the northeast 
corner there is a crawlspace.  Head through here and use your final key to 
open the door.  The second you enter this room equip the Lens of Truth so that 
you don't fall down the hole.  Open the small chest here to get a BLUE RUPEE 
and then go through the door.  In this room quickly defeat the disgusting blob 
monster and then use your Boomerang to get the GOLD SKULLTULA [078] from the 
wall, there is also another BLUE RUPEE in the treasure chest.  

Finally there is one more thing to do.  In the main room again, make a left 
down the hall to the north end and make a left again where the hole is.  You 
can pass the hole on the left and then bomb that weird lump wit ha crack in it 
to reveal a small chest with a DEKU NUT inside.  That's it for this dungeon, 
it's done but it's not quite time to get back to your old age yet.

The next small task will take you to Hyrule Market, which is where you would 
have had to go anyway so no matter.  When you get into the Market, play the 
Sun's Song so that it becomes night time.  To the right of where you enter the 
main Market (right means from the human perspective, not Link's) there is a 
door to a treasure chest minigame only open at night.  Enter this building.  
Speak to the man and give him the 10 Rupees he wants in order to play the game 
then use the DOOR KEY you get to enter the next room.  There are two chests 
here and you have to guess the correct one.  This is easy, simply equip the 
Lens of Truth and open the chest with the key now, and four more times after 
this.  Your reward for cheating is the PIECE OF HEART [031], your reward for 
actually trying your luck at a 3.125% chance of winning honestly, is the same.

With that out of the way it's time to go to back.  Head back to the Temple of 
Time and pull the Master Sword out of the stone.  You may think that now it's 
time to head to the next adult temple, and perhaps you're right, but for those 
who are interested in the best sword in the game, read the next section.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      IGGORON'S SWORD ------------------------------------- 01.40 --+

It's finally time to finish the sidequest you should have already begun.  If 
not you can check out the sidequests section for the whole thing up to this 
point.  Right now the trading item you should have in your inventory is the 
Poacher's Saw.  Make your way now to the Gerudo Valley.  Approach the bridge 
which is totally broken and equip the Longshot.  Use the Longshot to hook onto 
the target on the other side and get across.  When you do this, go and speak 
to the fat guy here.  Equip the Poacher's Saw onto one of your C buttons and 
use it while standing in front of him.  He'll offer you the BROKEN GORON'S 
SWORD in return for the Poacher's Saw.  Next head to Kakariko Village.

When you arrive here there is a scene.  Here is where you will learn the 
NOCTURNE OF SHADOW, the song which will warp you to the Shadow Temple, our 
next destination but only after we complete the Biggoron Sword sidequest.

Leave Kakariko Village through the north exit and begin the trek up the Death 
Mountain Trail.  When you reach the Magic Bean plant, step on it and jump off 
as it goes up past the bomb flower.  During the night you should hear 
something crawling around here, equip the Megaton Hammer and break the rock 
beside the bomb flower to find GOLD SKULLTULA [079].  After doing this jump 
back down and ride the Magic Bean plant again up to its highest point and jump 
off.  Make your way up the climbing wall and on your right at the top is the 
Biggoron.  Use the Broken Goron's Sword in front of him and he'll recognize 
it.  He says that before he can repair anything, he's going to need some 
Eyedrops from King Zora.  In return he gives you the PRESCRIPTION you need.

Make your way to Zora's River, west of Kakariko Village.  If you don't take 
Epona, then before you enter the river play Epona's Song so that the horse is 
close by for when you leave.  Navigate through the river and into Zora's 
Domain, stand on the platform before the king and use the Prescription.  He 
will give you the EYEBALL FROG which is needed in order to fill the 
prescription, get it to the Lake Hylia Laboratory as soon as possible.  
Quickly run down and get out of Zora's Domain toward the river (approximate 
time remaining 2:45) then jump into the river and run down all the way to 
Hyrule Field (approximate time remaining 2:00), now get on Epona and ride down 
to the south end and into Lake Hylia (approximate time remaining 1:00).  Head 
for the lab and go inside, use the frog beside the old man here (approximate 
time remaining: plenty).  He'll give you WORLD'S FINEST EYE DROPS.

These don't have a very long shelf life either so you need to get back to the 
Biggoron as quickly as possible, here's what to do.  Leave the house and get 
on Epona, then return to the Hyrule Map (approximate time remaining: 3:45).  
Now ride Epona all the way north to Kakariko Village and go inside 
(approximate time remaining: 2:40).  Make your way up to the Death Mountain 
Trail (approximate time remaining: 2:15).  From here ride the Magic Bean Plant 
up to the top and get to the bottom of the climbable wall (approximate time 
remaining: 1:30).  Use your Longshot and eliminate all the enemies on the 
wall, this is very important and you will save a ton of time killing them 
instead of being knocked off.  Climb the wall after they are dead and use the 
Eyedrops in front of the Biggoron to get the CLAIM CHECK for the sword.

He says to wait a few days, and that's just what you're going to do.  Play the 
Sun's Song about five times or so and speak to him again to get the BIGGORON'S 
SWORD.  This is the most powerful sword in the game, and it's yours!  It will 
make boss battles and more significantly easier.  The power of the sword is 
equivalent to that of the Giant's Knife except that unlike the knife, it 
cannot break.  Unfortunately you can't use it along with your shield, but with 
something like this, who cares!  You now have the ultimate weapon.

Now your main goal is to reach the Shadow Temple, to get there simply play the 
Nocturne of Shadow (left, right, right, A, left, right, down) on your Ocarina 
and it will take you to the graveyard.  Descend down the stairs and you will 
come across quite a few torches here which need to be lit.  Jump onto the 
centre platform and equip the Din's Fire spell, use it here and every last one 
of the torches will be lit.  Now the door to the Shadow Temple is open.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ HADOW TEMPLE ----------------------------------------------- 01.41 --+

Head down the hall and around the corner, you'll need your Longshot equipped 
to get across this large gap here.  Pass through the fake wall and turn left, 
equip the Lens of Truth at this point and go through the hidden door on your 
left when you come to it.  In the next room go through the fake wall directly 
ahead of you and then equip the Lens of Truth and look to your right, there is 
another hidden door there.  Open the large chest in this room to acquire the 
DUNGEON MAP for this dungeon.  Leave the room and then turn right, with the 
Lens of Truth equipped you should be able to see another hidden door on the 
wall here.  Make your way down the hall and you'll reach another very similar 
room.  Turn right, then left and directly ahead of you should be another fake 
wall this time with a door behind it.  Proceed now through that door.

Another miniboss exactly the same as the one you fought as Young Link down at 
the bottom of the well.  If you have the Biggoron's Sword then two jump 
attacks alone will be enough to take this enemy down.  When you kill him, a 
large treasure chest appears in this room which contains the HOVER BOOTS, 
special shoes which allow you to hover above the ground but give absolutely no 
traction.  Actually the game lies, there is traction, it's just significantly 
reduced.  Leave this room and return to the one with the skull pillars.

Back here near the entrance once again, equip the Lens of Truth and take a 
look at the pillars surrounding the statue in the middle.  One of them will 
not disappear when you look at it, all of the others will.  Push the object 
coming out the side of the statue to point in the direction of the real skull 
and the door will open.  You can get there only if you have the Hover Boots 
equipped, simply roll across the pit in the ground and head through the door.

At the bottom of the ramp be careful of that laser-shooting enemy here, make a 
right and proceed through the fake wall to reach a door.  When you enter this 
room make sure the Biggoron's Sword is equipped, and totally waste the two 
mummies in order to make a large treasure chest appear.  Inside this chest is 
the dungeon's COMPASS.  Turn around and return to the laser statue, continue 
across without changing direction and go through the door on the opposite 
side.  In there, there is a death statue in the centre equipped with two 
spinning sickles.  Avoid them while you make your way around the room 
collecting all  of the Silver Rupees you can.  Two of them are in little 
enclosed areas while another can only be obtained by Longshotting up on top of 
the crate.  Do not go into the little enclosed area with the creepy face 
panel, there is a hole in the ground.  Once you have all of the Silver Rupees, 
go through the now-open gate and check inside the little treasure chest to get 
a SMALL KEY from it.  Return to the room with the laser statue in it.  

There is one more path you haven't take it, but instead of simply walking 
through the wall, you must destroy it using a bomb.  This is of course the 
east path by the way.  Use your Small Key to unlock the door and head through 
it.  Be careful while running down the hall here, a Skulltula is going to drop 
down from above on at least four occasions, even while you're trying to pass 
underneath the falling guillotines.  When Navi interrupts you to say to be 
careful of the shadows, then don't stay in one place too long or you'll be 
grabbed and brought all the way back to the beginning again.

Jump across through all of the falling guillotine blades until you reach the 
south wall.  At the end of the path you'll have to fight a Stalfos for the 
first time in quite awhile, you may find that you have an easier time 
switching back to the Master Sword so you can use the shield, or perhaps not.  
Either way defeat it and then equip the Lens of Truth and look to your left.  
You'll see that there are platforms connecting this path to a door at the end.  

You know what sucks about this room?  Spinning death blade sickle statue which 
cannot be seen!  That's what sucks about this room.  What you want to do is 
equip the Longshot and make your way around while avoiding the blades, and 
kill the bats that occupy this room.  Also a single slash from the Biggoron's 
Sword should be enough to take out that disgusting blob thing.  When all 
enemies are eliminated the gate will open and you can go inside to find a BLUE 
RUPEE in the chest and a GOLD SKULLTULA [080] crawling on the wall.

Turn around and head all the way back to the big large room, defeat the 
Stalfos skeleton enemy again and then jump to the platform across from him 
which is moving up and down.  Jump across again and begin collecting the 
Silver Rupees here in this area, the fifth one can be found by blowing up the 
laser turret in the centre here.  The path leads you to a large room with 
dropping spike platforms.  Equip the Lens of Truth and look to your right, you 
will see a block in the wall.  Pull it out from the wall and place it on the 
track.  Begin pushing it along below the spike platforms.  When you get past 
the first one, push it only a little bit so it blocks the second one, bit is 
still partially under the first one.  This way neither one of them can drop 
down.  Make a right into the jail cell and open the small chest to get ARROWS.  
Turn around and enter the opposite cell, here you will find GOLD SKULLTULA 
[081].  Grab hold of the large block from the other side and pull it all the 
way to the end.  Jump on top of it and up to the ledge on the left.  Hop over 
the spike thing to the opposite side and open the small chest containing BLUE 
RUPEE.  After that turn around and step on the button, it makes a small chest 
appear on the opposite side.  Go back across to it and open the chest to get a 
SMALL KEY.  From here turn around and head back to the main room again.

Before you reach the platform which is moving up and down, make a left and 
equip the Lens of Truth.  There are moving platforms to jump across to reach a 
locked door on the other side.  This here is a very interesting room, you'll 
have to use a number of tools to get through it.  The first tool you should 
use is the Lens of Truth which will allow you to see the invisible spikes on 
the floor here.  Be sure to eliminate the zombie things and a small treasure 
chest will appear.  The next tool you will need is the Longshot, many of the 
Silver Rupees can only be acquired by hooking onto the panels on the wall.  
Lastly there is a high up invisible panel in one corner, once you hook onto it 
and drop you'll need to use the Hover Boots in order to get the fifth and 
final Silver Rupee which unlocks the door.  Open the small treasure chest to 
acquire a BLUE RUPEE and then proceed through the western door.

You'll need the Fairy Bow equipped here so you can take out the flaming bats 
near the entrance of this room.  Same goes for the two normal bats at the back 
of the room.  Behind the flaming skull you will find a GOLD SKULLTULA [082], 
be sure to get that.  notice there are some Bomb Flowers in this room, lift up 
one of the bombs and throw it on top of the flaming skull in order to blow it 
up.  A SMALL KEY will fall out of the broken wreckage for you to pick up.  
Return to the room with the invisible spikes.  There is an invisible Longshot 
panel on the ceiling above the door, shoot for it and then head through it.

Be sure you've got the Iron Boots equipped, there are enormous fans in these 
halls which are trying to blow you back, but with the boots you can make it 
past just fine.  For one of them you will need to use the Longshot to get over 
the gap.  Make your way to the end and go through the door.  Eliminate the two 
zombies in this room and then equip the Lens of Truth to see a hidden small 
chest containing ARROWS.  You might also want to equip the Ocarina and play 
the Sun's Song, doing so will make a fairy appear.  Return to the previous 
room and take a look on your right, you'll see (if you have the Lens of Truth 
equipped) that there is a hidden door in the wall.  This can get quite 
difficult, you need to quickly jump to the right (or simply use the Hover 
Boots) in order to get over there.  Once you make it proceed through the door.

Open the small chest here and collect the BLUE RUPEE from inside after you 
kill both of the mummies.  After doing this equip your bombs and blow up that 
weird lump near the door.  When you do so, equip the Lens of Truth and you'll 
see a small chest containing a SMALL KEY.  Use the key to unlock the door and 
enter the next room.  On your left is a large block, pull this block and place 
it in the little depression, then climb on top of it.  There is a ladder here 
to climb up as well.  When you reach the top take out your Ocarina and play 
the Scarecrow's Song.  One will appear on the platform across from you, use 
the Longshot to grab hold of him and swing over to another GOLD SKULLTULA 
[083].  Get back across to the other side and climb the ladder once again.

This time jump left and on top of the boat, there you will find a Triforce 
symbol.  Stand on top of it and play the Ocarina, Zelda's Song in particular.  
The boat will begin to move and as it does, two Stalfos enemies will drop 
down, you can dispatch them quite easily using the Biggoron's Sword.  When the 
boat stops, jump off the left side before it sinks and then go through the 
door on the right when you land.  In here instead of having a bunch of fake 
walls, there are a bunch of actual walls you can't see.  Defeat the two 
Floormaster enemies which appear in here and the small ones that it splits 
into, then proceed through the door to the west.  Climb the stairs to the top 
and grab a bomb from a Bomb Flower.  Throw three of them into the three 
spinning skulls and they will explode into quite a few Rupees.  After 
collecting them check the wall for another GOLD SKULLTULA [084].  Return to 
the previous room and this time take the south door.  You'll need to equip the 
Lens of Truth once again because there is an invisible Floormaster enemy in 
this room.  Once defeated a small chest will appear containing SMALL KEY.

Return to the previous room and take the only door you haven't taken before, 
that's the door leading north.  Right when you enter this room, go to the menu 
and equip the Din's Fire ability on one of your C Buttons.  Use it and it will 
destroy the spiked walls which are coming toward you.  Open up the small chest 
to acquire a BLUE RUPEE, then open up the large coloured chest to get the BOSS 
KEY.  Now leave here, take the east door and return to the large boat room.

You'll need the Fire Arrows equipped here, notice that on the other side there 
are a number of Bomb Flowers.  Take aim and fire at the Bomb Flowers to ignite 
them and blow them up.  The large statue will fall down and then you can cross 
it safely to the other side, use a Small Key to unlock the door there.  This 
room is quite easy, simply equip the Hover Boots and the Lens of Truth.  Run 
across the first gap and turn left a little bit, roll to reach the second 
small platform and then from there just roll to the boss door.  Use your Boss 
Key to open it up and head through.  Step over the hole and then drop down.

The amazing thing about this boss is that its one of the hardest, and one of 
the easiest bosses at the same time, depending entirely on how familiar you 
are with the fight.  I've had times when I went through two full complements 
of hearts thinking I couldn't possibly win, and other times defeating the boss 
without being hit.  If ever there was a boss where a super offensive approach 
is best, this is certainly it.  The longer it takes you to fight this boss, 
the less chance you have.  We'll begin with his attack, there are two hands 
which bounce around and shake the ground so it is difficult for you to stand 
still without being bounced around.  They can become a fist and punch you, or 
grab you, or they can hover overhead and stomp on you.  They can knock you out 
of the arena onto the slime which damages you.  Each one of this attacks deals 
at least two hearts worth of damage, possibly more.  Dodging the attacks 
basically means standing back, and always keeping both hands in your sight.

When it comes to actually attacking the boss it's a whole different story.  
Equip the Fairy Bow with the normal arrows, not the Fire Arrows because it 
will be very important to conserve magic power.  Choose whichever is better 
for you, L/Z targeting or aiming manually, your goal is to take aim and fire 
at the hands.  When you hit one hand it will turn blue and drop an item, 
either an arrow or a magic bottle, both of which you will be needing for this 
battle.  Your goal is to hit one hand, then the other hand quickly before the 
first one can shake itself back to normal.  Once both hands are hit then 
position yourself in the middle of the map, between them and facing them, and 
equip the Lens of Truth.  Only using this will you actually be able to see the 
boss.  Take aim with your Fairy Bow and fire at his eye.  When you do this the 
boss drops and you are free to go in with the Biggoron's Sword swinging like 
crazy.  The thing about this boss is that using the Biggoron's Sword, you only 
have to do this twice, the boss will die on the second set of sword swings, so 
this is why I say offensive is so good.  The attacks are extremely hard to 
dodge and powerful, but if you're fast, you can kill him very quickly.
Collect the HEART CONTAINER from the ground and step into the warp portal.  
This will take you to the sages and net you the SHADOW MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ERUDO'S FORTRESS ------------------------------------ 01.42 --+

With this temple complete it's time to head for the Gerudo Valley as an adult.  
When you get there and reach the broken bridge, use the Longshot to get across 
it or jump your horse, Epona over the large gap.  Play the Sun's Song so that 
it becomes night time and then look behind the big stone arch opposite the 
carpenter to find GOLD SKULLTULA [085].  After doing that break all the nearby 
rocks using the Megaton Hammer and you'll find a PURPLE RUPEE.  Check behind 
the tent at night and there you will find GOLD SKULLTULA [086].  Speak to the 
carpenter and then make your way to the west and enter the Gerudo's Fortress.

When you arrive here, climb the stairs on your right and you'll be seen by a 
number of the Gerudo.  They will grab you and throw you into some kind of 
prison cell.  Use the Longshot to snag the piece of wood above you head and 
get out of the cell.  Jump left so that you land on the roof of the building 
and then go inside.  Since the Longshot, like the Hookshot, has the ability to 
stun enemies, use that ability now to stun the Gerudo standing guard here and 
the other one which walks by.  Run up the hall to the left and use the 
Longshot to get hold of the beams going across the top and then from there, 
head up the ramp and get outside again.  Break the wooden crate here and 
inside you'll find a PURPLE RUPEE.  Stand at the edge beside the railing here 
and look left to see a large treasure chest.  Equip the Longshot and fire it at 
the treasure chest, it will pull you over the gate and right to it.

Inside the large chest is a PIECE OF HEART [032].  Drop down over the edge 
south of the chest, and then down once more.  Turn around and go through the 
door leading north.  Follow the hallway down and speak to the carpenter in the 
jail cell.  After doing this a Gerudo drops down behind you.  They are quite 
difficult but using the Biggoron's Sword you should be able to defeat them 
without any problem.  You'll find that the Spin Attack is especially 
effective.  She drops a SMALL KEY when defeated, use it to open the jail cell 
beside you and free the prisoner.  Leave the cell after talking to him and go 
left then outside.  Head north a little bit and you'll see a door leading 
north just a few steps up.  Enter this room and speak to the carpenter behind 
the jail cell.  Defeat the Gerudo who drops down to get another SMALL KEY and 
then open the carpenter's cell and speak with him to set him free.

Leave here again and then get caught on purpose to be thrown in jail.  Get 
yourself out of there and then equip the Hover Boots.  Hover to the left ledge 
and go through the door again.  Stun the two guards here again and make your 
way out the top, Longshot to the treasure chest just as you did before.  This 
time go as far south as you can on the platform before you fall from there, 
drop down one level right in front of you, you should land on a single square 
with nowhere to go.  Play the Sun's Song on your Ocarina to make it night time 
and when you do, a GOLD SKULLTULA [087] will appear on the wall right beside 
you.  This is on the east wall by the way.  From here, with the hover boots 
equipped take a couple steps west then a couple of steps south in mid-air (or 
just a couple of steps southwest) and you'll reach a platform with a door 
leading to the north.  Enter this door and follow the path down to a big room.  
Notice that directly across from you there is a ledge over the room, use this 
ledge to get across to the other side, as long as you stay high enough you 
won't be seen.  When you get outside turn south and go through the door.

Down the ramp there third jail cell is found, defeat the Gerudo woman to get a 
SMALL KEY and then use it to save the third carpenter.  Leave this area and 
get caught again to return to jail.  Get out of the jail cell and drop down to 
the ground.  As you walk along past those crates, stand in that little 
enclosed area on the left and play the Song of Storms (A, down, up, A, down, 
up).  A hole will appear in the ground leading to a Fairy Fountain.  After 
filling your bottles, leave the fountain and continue along the ground to the 
south.  Before you reach the Gerudo standing still there will be a door 
leading east, go through it.  When you come out the other side you'll be on a 
higher level.  Climb the vines to the south and go through the door directly 
beside you at the top.  Use the Hover Boots to go across the top of this room, 
using the ledge in the middle and out the other side.  Now drop down to the 
north, one small level and there is a door leading north here.  When you get 
inside the hall there is a crate in front of you.  Wait for the Gerudo to walk 
past and stun her, then follow the hall to the right where you'll find the 
fourth and final carpenter.  Get the SMALL KEY from the Gerudo and save him.

After this there is a scene and you get the GERUDO'S MEMBERSHIP CARD.  Now you 
can freely move about the fortress without being thrown in jail.  Leave this 
place and get on your horse, wherever it is.  Bring it back to the fortress 
and ride over to the south side and head east to the shooting range.

The game can only be played during the day, the Gerudo to speak to will not 
appear at night.  Make sure you are on your horse when you speak to her and 
the game will begin.  The cost to play one round is twenty Rupees.

Here's how it works, there are a number of targets and pots along the path.  
You don't control the horse, it moves automatically and you simply fire.  You 
are given twenty arrows each time you try.  Prizes are awarded for 1000 and 
1500 points.  To maximize your score, follow this strategy: on your first run, 
aim your bow and fire arrows at every single pot you pass, each one of them is 
worth 100 points.  After passing the last pot turn yourself forward and aim up 
at the target ahead of you.  Hitting the centre is worth 100 points, the outer 
circle is 60 points and the edge is 30 points.  Try to aim approximately two 
shots for the centre and the horse runs around it.  On the way back aim for 
the targets behind the pots this time.  Aim for about two shots per target and 
when you pass them all, fire all your remaining arrows at the south target.  
Depending on how well you are able to hit the centre, you will eventually 
reach 1500 points.  Here are a couple of random tips.  When shooting the pots, 
position your bow on the same level and don't move it, let the movement of the 
horse guide you.  Simply tap the B button not quite as fast as you possibly 
can, but still at a fairly quick and steady pace and you should be able to get 
all the pots easily.  The second tip is to always be aware that wherever you 
point the tip of the arrow when aiming, the actual shot will go diagonally up 
and to the right of that point, the further the target, the further up-right.

Your prize for successfully accumulating 1000 points is a PIECE OF HEART 
[033].  Your prize for managing to get 1500 points, which is actually quite 
difficult, is the BIGGEST QUIVER which increases your maximum arrow carrying 
capacity to 50 arrows.  That's everything you can win from this game.

After you have finished playing the game, don't leave this area.  Play the 
Sun's Song to make it night time.  Ride the horse to the north end and you 
will see that there is a GOLD SKULLTULA [088] on the northern target.  Ride 
your horse back to the previous area, the actually fortress section itself.  On 
the south side just before the east path leading to the Shooting Range, there 
is a Gerudo woman standing beside a gate.  Speak to her and she'll let you 
into the Gerudo Training Ground for a mere 10 Rupees, it's totally optional.  
If you choose not to do it, scroll down to the next section, Haunted Wasteland.

Enter the dungeon and climb the stairs.  Equip the Fairy Bow and do a 180 
degree turn, above the door where you entered there is an eyeball switch, fire 
an arrow at it to spawn a small chest containing BLUE RUPEE.  At the top of 
the stairs make a left and proceed through the door.  There are two Stalfos 
enemies in this room and you need to defeat them before the time limit of one 
minute runs out, obviously make sure you're using the Biggoron's Sword for 
this.  After killing them both a chest appears containing SMALL KEY, pick it 
up and go through the door beside the chest to enter the next hall.

In here you have one minute and thirty seconds to collect all the Silver 
Rupees.  Ignore the first right turn and take the second right, go right at 
the top of the ramp and get the Silver Rupee before the end, be careful 
because boulders are rolling down here.  Turn around and go back up the hill, 
go left not at the path where you came from but the one after it.  At the top 
of the ramp look left and you'll see two Silver Rupees.  Stand beside the 
first one and equip the Longshot.  Fire the Longshot at the panel on the 
ceiling you can see past the Rupees, it should take you through the first one 
and drop you on the second one, now you're up to three.  When you fall you'll 
be back near the entrance door again.  Face away from the door as if you had 
just come into the room and take the path on the left.  Go left again and grab 
the fourth Silver Rupee just before the edge.  Boulders are rolling down here 
as well.  The fifth Silver Rupee is located at the top of this hill.  
Afterward you'll notice that you cannot get to the door because of the flame 
barriers.  Equip the Longshot and shoot for the ceiling panel to get over 
them.  After that, there's nothing else here so enter into the next room.

Defeat the four wolf enemies in this room and then open the small chest 
appears to get a bunch of ARROWS.  Equip the Lens of Truth and look at the 
door on the right, there is a hidden passage above it for you to Longshot 
into.  When you get up there step on the button and proceed into the next 
room.  Jump down onto the platform in the centre and equip the Fairy Bow.  
Shoot the eyeball of the statue and make your way around from left to right in 
a circle, shooting the eyeballs as you go along.  When all four of them are 
lit up a chest appears containing a SMALL KEY.  Longshot to the panel on the 
wall and then you'll drop down in front of the door.  Head through it.

There are a number of enemies in this room, all you really have to do is 
defeat them all and a chest will appear containing ARROWS.  After doing this 
equip the megaton Hammer and smash all the statues at the sides of the room.  
Shoot the eyeball switch to unlock the door and step on the button to lower 
the fire in the middle and allow you to open the chest for a SMALL KEY.  After 
getting the key, go through the door directly on the wall, not in a hallway.

Here you will have to collect a whole bunch of Silver Rupees again.  Walk to 
the edge and turn around, use the Longshot on the panel right above you to get 
the Silver Rupee above your head.  It might make it easier if you use the 
Hover Boots although they are not required.  Jump down to the first pillar and 
across, collecting the three Silver Rupees on the way to the button at the 
end, step on it and the fire will drop.  Head back to the flaming platform and 
get the fifth Silver Rupee.  The door you want to go through is the door on the 
left from the perspective of when you are just entering the room.

Stand away from the blocks in the floor and equip the Ocarina.  Play the Song 
of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and they will disappear.  Equip the 
Iron Boots and drop down the hole to the bottom.  Your goal is to collect all 
the Silver Rupees and it will make it a lot easier if you defeat the enemies 
first.  After doing that you'll have to use a combination of the Longshot, and 
unequipping the Iron Boots in order to swing around and swim around to collect 
them all.  When this is over, swim back up to the top and open the small chest 
to acquire the fourth SMALL KEY.  Leave this room and make a left, jump across 
the pillars over the fire and head through the door on this side.

Here you will have to fight a couple upgraded lizard enemies, with the 
Biggoron's Sword they shouldn't be too hard to defeat.  Stay away from the 
centre to avoid the laser, but stay away from the edge also as the fire walls 
will damage you.  When the two lizards are defeated, bomb the laser pillar and 
a chest appears with a SMALL KEY in it.  Return to the previous room.

Jump across to the platform with the button on it and take out the Ocarina.  
Play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and some blocks will 
appear here.  use the Hover Boots to reach them and climb up.  Grab the sixth 
SMALL KEY and go through the door.  Make a right here and it leads to a chest 
containing ARROWS.  Directly across from the door where you entered is a chest 
with a BOMBCHU in it.  Leave this room and jump back down onto the pillars.  
Enter the room again where you fought the lizard things and keep going, 
through the next door you will find yourself back in the first room.

Take the centre door this time to reach the ultimate locked room of treasure.  
use two Small Keys to go through the locked door on your left and then the one 
after it as well, now you're down to four.  Open the chest to get a PURPLE 
RUPEE here.  Continue through the next locked door and the one after it, open 
the small chest containing a RED RUPEE.  Continue through the next door and 
open the chest to get ARROWS.  Unlock the other door here and you'll find 
yourself at a dead end.  That's okay, return to the entrance of this room and 
equip the Lens of Truth.  Proceed through the door on the left which you 
unlocked and take a look at the ceiling with the Lens of Truth equipped, there 
is a hole up there.  Climb the fence wall and here you will find a chest 
containing a SMALL KEY.  Take this Small Key back to the door you couldn't 
unlock and unlock it.  Inside the giant chest is the ICE ARROW, like the Fire 
Arrow it is a special elemental type of arrow you can shoot using the Fairy 
Bow.  That's about all there is of any worth here, time to leave this place.

Return to the Gerudo's Fortress and make your way to the northwestern side.  
Climb the ladder and speak to the woman there, she will open up the gate for 
you.  Afterward drop down and get off your horse, enter the Haunted Wasteland.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ AUNTED WASTELAND ------------------------------------- 01.43 --+

Enter the wasteland with your Longshot equipped.  Stand on the crate and fire 
it up at one of the three crates above.  Your goal is to navigate through the 
desert, following the marker flags.  After just a couple you will see a sign 
pointing you in another direction.  Follow it and equip the Hover Boots, they 
will allow you to run onto the magic carpet and speak to the guy.  He sells 
Bombchus for 200 Rupees, not worth it, but whatever.  Head back to the flags 
and keep going.  When you reach a small stone structure, drop down.  Defeat 
the GOLD SKULLTULA [089] there and then equip the Fire Arrows.  Use them to 
light both torches in this room and spawn a small chest with a PURPLE RUPEE 
inside.  Leave this thing and climb up on top of it.  Equip the Lens of Truth 
to find a Big Poe who will lead you in the right direction.  You need to 
follow him until he leads you to the next area with the temple.

Begin by heading north and taking a look between two trees for a wall with a 
crack in it.  Bomb this wall to reveal a Great Fairy Fountain, somewhere you 
haven't been in a long time.  This is where you will learn the third spell, 
Naryu's Love.  Prove your royal connections by standing on the Triforce emblem 
and playing Zelda's Lullaby.  Notice on your map that southwest of the 
entrance is a round object, when you go there you'll find it’s just some dry 
thing surrounded by trees.  Stand on the nearby flat rock and play the Song of 
Storms (A, down, up, A, down, up) on your Ocarina to turn it into a Fairy 
Fountain.  Take a look on the back of the palm tree across from you at night, 
there is a GOLD SKULLTULA [090] on the back of it.  With this out of the way 
head west and enter the temple.  You'll quickly realize there is nothing you 
can actually accomplish in here.  Leave the temple for a scene.  Sheik will 
teach you the REQUIEM OF SPIRIT.  After learning this, you must become a child 
once again.  Play the Prelude of Light to warp back to the Temple of Time and 
place your Master Sword into the stone to become Young Link again.

The first thing you are going to want to do is make absolutely sure that you 
have a bottle with bugs in it.  If not then leave Hyrule Castle Town as a 
child and go east, there should be a hole in the ground that you revealed 
earlier.  Drop down and chop one of the plants, bugs should pop out from 
underneath, use an Empty Bottle and nab them.  Now play the Requiem of Spirit 
(A, down, A, right, down, A) to warp to the Spirit Temple.  In front of the 
door to the temple there is a hole there.  Drop your bugs into the hole to 
find GOLD SKULLTULA [091].  Now plant the Magic Bean.  Since it really won't 
take very long and you don't want to forget later, play the Prelude of Light 
(up, right, up, right, left, up) to warp back to the Temple of Time and become 
Adult Link again.  Play the Requiem of Spirit to warp back to the temple and 
step on the Magic Bean plant.  Make sure it's night time, about half way 
through its flight you'll come across GOLD SKULLTULA [092] on top of a large 
rock mound.  Near the end of the flight, on top of the arch in front of the 
entrance is PIECE OF HEART [034].  After this warp back to the Temple of Time, 
change to Young Link and warp back to the Spirit Temple.  Finally, enter it.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ PIRIT TEMPLE [YOUNG LINK] ---------------------------------- 01.44 --+

Start this temple by climbing up the stairs in front of you.  The statues at 
the top are like the other ones you've encountered before, when you touch 
them, they come alive, so just don't touch them.  Make a left at the top and 
speak to the Gerudo.  When she asks you what you are doing, tell her that you 
aren't doing anything.  After that tell her you hate Ganondorf as well and 
listen to what she says.  When everything is said and done climb through the 
crawlspace.  In order to open the gate in this next room you are going to kill 
all the enemies.  This includes the statue in the middle of the room.  Place a 
bomb beside it and touch the statue.  The bomb should explode and destroy the 
statue.  At the upper part of this room you have two doors and a crawlspace.  
Take the door on the left.  When you enter this room you are going to have to 
fight a Stalfos enemy.  He's extremely difficult as Young Link so I would 
recommend you simply try to knock him off the cliff.  After this you can take 
out the flaming skull if you want to.  Stand at the edge and equip the 
Boomerang.  Aim for the left side of the room and throw, it should come back 
around and hit the crystal on the other side.  This will cause the bridge to 
fall down.  Open the chest for a BLUE RUPPE and go through the door.

This room is interesting, you'll have to take on a new enemy called Anubis.  
This enemy moves exactly as you do so you're going to have to find a way to 
defeat him knowing that.  There is of course a special trick to it.  The enemy 
is weak to fire.  Notice that on the right side there is a crystal that when 
hit, creates fire in front of the door.  What you have to do is run to the 
middle of the room and jump to the left so the other enemy is standing in 
front of the door.  Shoot the crystal from here with your Slingshot and it 
will engulf Anubis in flames.  When the door opens, go through it.

As Navi tells you, you need to be careful in this next room.  Wait for the 
hand to drop down and then kill it to net yourself quite a few Rupees.  This 
is one of those Silver Rupee rooms, you need to collect five of them.  Four of 
them can be found beside, and up on the climbable wall, the fifth is hidden 
behind the torch in the corner.  When you have all five the bridge drops down.  
Go to the other side of the room and use your Boomerang to get GOLD SKULLTULA 
[093].  Eliminate the bats as well.  Since you can't use the Fire Arrows as 
Young Link, you'll have to ignite both of the torches here using the Din’s 
Fire spell.  Turn around and open the chest to get a SMALL KEY and then 
proceed through the door on the same side as the torches you ignited.

This room should look familiar.  Head through the small crawlspace and there 
you will find a locked door, use the Small Key to open it up.  Equip the 
Slingshot and aim up the Skulltulas on the wall.  At the very top, turn 
around and look down to see a GOLD SKULLTULA [094] on the wall, get it with 
the Boomerang.  Run around this room until two of those lizard enemies drop 
down, defeat both of them.  Look up at the side of the room to see a crystal, 
hit it and a chest appears containing a BOMBCHU, there should also be another 
chest here with a BLUE RUPEE in it.  Equip the Bombchu and head over to the 
side of the room, facing the rock in the wall where the light is shining 
through the crack.  Throw a Bombchu to blow this up and head through the door.

Make an immediate left in this room and grab hold of the statue.  Jump down to 
the centre of the room and note the square shaped platform in the middle.  
Stand on this platform and use Din's Fire to light both the torches beside 
you.  A large chest appears here, possibly right where you are standing, open 
it up and collect the DUNGEON MAP for this dungeon.  The wall beside the 
statue that you pushed is a climbable wall, so head back over there and begin 
making your way up the wall.  Ascend the stairs up to the very top and head 
through the door on your left.  There are a number of laser statues in this 
room, if you still have the Bombchus equipped I recommend you use those to 
defeat the statues, they seem to work quite well.  Climb around the room and 
collect all five of the Silver Rupees.  When you do this a torch sets on fire, 
you're going to need that.  Equip the Deku Stick and light it one fire, there 
are three torches in this room to light using the Deku Stick as well.  When 
you light them all, open the chest to get a SMALL KEY and then head onward.
Climb the stairs in this room and approach the door.  Turn around and look 
above the door, equip the Boomerang and get the GOLD SKULLTULA [095].  When 
you're ready for a fight, proceed through the door using your Small Key.

The Iron Knuckle is an extremely difficult enemy, while he is slow he is very 
very powerful.  His attacks can deal up to four hearts worth of damage.  He 
has two types of attacks, a double horizontal slash and a powerful over head 
slash.  The basic tactic for both of them is the same, avoid it and use the 
jump attack.  After the jump attack Iron Knuckle has a habit of quickly using 
the overhead slash, since you don't have time to move backward, try dodging it 
by moving either left or right.  It takes a hell of a lot of hits to defeat 
this guy, even using the jump attack it's at least seven or eight.

After he is defeated, head through the door behind the throne.  It leads you 
outside where you will have to listen to that stupid owl.  After doing so be 
very careful to avoid falling off the ledge and open the large treasure chest 
to acquire the SILVER GAUNTLETS.  These are special gauntlets you will need in 
order to move very large objects.  That does it for what you have to do in 
this temple as a child.  After the scene occurs, play the Prelude of Light.  
Pull the Master Sword out to become Adult Link once again.  Now it's time to 
warp to the Spirit Temple (A, down, A, right, down, A) and go inside.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ PIRIT TEMPLE [ADULT LINK] ---------------------------------- 01.45 --+

Enter the Spirit Temple and use the Silver Gauntlets (they are equipped 
automatically) to push the block on the right at the top of the stairs, out of 
the way.  Keep pushing until it drops into position and then walk over top of 
it.  Make your way to the stairs and equip the Longshot, aim it up above the 
laser statue and shoot it at the crystal above it.  This will unlock the doors 
here, proceed through the door on the left.  Defeat the wolf monster and then 
stand on the Triforce emblem and play Zelda's Lullaby to spawn a treasure 
chest.  Use the Longshot to hook onto the treasure chest and open it up to 
acquire the COMPASS.  Turn around and return to the previous room.

This time take the door on the right.  In this room you need to collect all of 
the Silver Rupees if you can, it's not going to be very hard.  The most 
difficult one is the first one, all you need to do is equip the Hover Boots to 
get it.  Drop down to the lower floor and go back and forth into the little 
enclosed areas to get the next four Silver Rupees.  Play the Song of Time 
beside the blue block in the middle on the left (right, A, down, right, A, 
down).  Behind it you will find the next GOLD SKULLTULA [096].  After 
acquiring it, run back down to the boulder area and go through the door.

There is only a small treasure chest in this room containing a SMALL KEY.  Be 
careful of that blob monster, it drops down from above.  After getting the key 
backtrack a couple of rooms to the room with the laser statue.  Use the Small 
Key on the door in the middle.  Defeat the blob enemy in this room and watch 
out for the pots that fly at you.  Climb up the wall up ahead and at the top, 
equip the Lens of Truth.  One of those invisible hands is scurrying around in 
this room and you'll need to be able to see it to defeat it easier.  

After the enemy is defeated, grab hold of the thing in the middle and rotate 
it so that the light shines on the third sun, ignore the treasure chests that 
come out of the other ones, they are trapped chests.  Now head through the 
west door.  Drop down over the edge and run to the other side and climb up.  
Ascend the stairs to the top like you did before, except this time at the top 
stand beside the torch and play the Scarecrow's Song.  A scarecrow will appear 
on the platform across from you, Longshot to him if you want but to be honest 
it's not even necessary.  There is a GOLD SKULLTULA [097] right beside him and 
you can take it out using the Longshot from this ledge.  

Drop down to the bottom and run back over to the door where you entered the 
room.  Longshot to the ceiling panel above the door and then climb that set of 
stairs.  Equip the Hover Boots and run along the air to land in the hand of 
the statue, there is a Triforce symbol here, stand on it and play Zelda's 
Lullaby to spawn a treasure chest.  Use the Longshot to grab hold of the chest 
in the other hand and launch to it.  Open the chest to acquire a SMALL KEY.  
Climb back up the staircase that you were just on once again, and notice that 
across from where you used the Hover Boots, there is now another chest.  
Longshot to it and open it for a BLUE RUPEE, then equip the Megaton Hammer and 
pound the rusted button down to open the door below, drop down and go through.

There is an enormous block ahead of you, push it until it falls into the hole 
and then turn right.  Another block is here, push this one until it falls into 
a hole as well.  At the end here you will find a rusted button that can only 
be pushed using the Megaton Hammer.  All it does is activate an elevator 
allowing for quick access from the first room to the main large statue room.  
Speaking of that room, it's time to head back there right now.  Go up the 
staircase on the southeastern side for a third time, and this time unlock the 
door at the top using the one Small Key that you still have.

Head through this hall into the next room where the gate drops behind you.  
Equip the Bombchus on one button and the Fire Arrows on another.  Take a 
couple of steps to the left and face the laser statue.  Use a Bombchu while 
facing it despite the fact that there is a pit in between them.  The Bombchu 
will go down the pit for quite awhile and eventually come back up, destroying 
the statue.  Now take aim with your Fire Arrows and kill all three of the 
Anubis enemies in this room easily.  Now take the southeast door.

Getting through this door is actually quite difficult, but when you know what 
you're doing it shouldn't be too bad.  There is a button in the middle of this 
room and when you step on it, the door on the right opens, but the instant you 
step off it the door closes.  You can't move the statues onto it because 
they are living enemy statues.  What you need to do is equip the Fairy Bow and 
stand beside the door on the right.  Take aim and fire at the statue on the 
other side of the button, so that the button is between you and the statue.  
The statue will jump toward you, stepping on the button along the way, and 
open the door allowing you to go through.  In this next hallway equip the Lens 
of Truth and you'll see two treasure chests, one contains a RECOVERY HEART and 
the other contains a RECOVERY HEART as well.  Proceed through the next door.

Another battle against an Iron Knuckle occurs in this room, but with the 
Biggoron's Sword it takes only a mere four jump attacks to kill him, and the 
range is much longer, so overall this enemy is much much easier.  Leave the 
room and go outside, here you will find a large treasure chest containing the 
MIRROR SHIELD.  Remember the room with the puzzle where you had to make the 
statue come alive and step on the button for you?  Head back to that room now.  
There is a beam of light coming into the room, stand in the beam with the 
Mirror Shield equipped and reflect the light up to the sun on the wall.  This 
will unlock the door beside you and allow you to go through and get SMALL KEY.

Use your bombs or Bombchus to blow up the laser statues in this room and look 
up.  Your challenge is to climb up this wall without falling down, the 
climbable sections are moving.  Note however, that climbable walls can be 
latched onto with the Longshot, aim it up to the very top one and forget the 
whole challenge entirely.  In the next room stand on the Triforce symbol and 
play Zelda's Lullaby to open the door.  Defeat all of the fire slugs in this 
room, then you need to find a way to get that chest.  Take out your bombs and 
place one beside the door to the left of the chest.  When it explodes equip 
the Fairy Bow and the Longshot, fire an arrow at the eyeball behind the door 
and then Longshot to the panel on the ceiling, you should land on a platform.  
Jump from this platform to the other platform and step on the button, then 
drop down and open the chest to get the BOSS KEY.  Leave this room and make a 
right, head through the door at the top of the stairs into the next room.

Approach the gate on the left and strike the crystal with your sword through 
the gate.  Head through the door across from you and in the next room, you'll 
be attacked by a lizard monster.  Once you defeat it go the other side of 
the room and defeat the floating skulls.  Turn the mirror so that it's facing 
90 degrees in the other direction toward the weird lump in the wall.  Blow up 
the rock lump with a bomb and head through.  Defeat the next lizard monster 
and turn this mirror as well to face the large one in the other room, now head 
back there.  Stand in the shining light and aim your Mirror Shield up toward 
the west wall where the sun emblem is to lower the platform in the middle.  
You'll find yourself back in the main room again with light shining down on 
your head.  Use the Mirror Shield to reflect it toward the face of the large 
statue and when it breaks off, Longshot to the gate across from you.

Through the boss door it's not quite time to fight the true boss yet, first 
you must take on another Iron Knuckle.  Not just any Iron Knuckle, a larger, 
faster stronger, mostly the same version of the Iron Knuckle.  Basically you 
won't notice too much difference, just that it takes more hits to kill.

The true bosses are the Sorceress Sisters Twinrova.  To begin the fight 
simply climb up onto the centre platform and walk to the middle.  They fly 
around on their broomsticks, one of fire element, one of ice element.  Each of 
them only have one attack, a powerful ice beam or a powerful fire beam.  
Fortunately both attacks can be deflected using your Mirror Shield, in fact 
you will be counting on that.  In order to defeat the bosses what you have to 
do is deflect the beam of one boss and aim it at another boss.  The beam is 
constant so you'll be able to swing it around for a couple seconds in hope of 
hitting the other boss.  Use L/Z targeting to make sure you are looking 
straight at the boss firing the attack, it's harder to deflect on an angle.  
Each of them must take two or three hits before the second phase begins.

Both bosses meld together and form on single boss now.  This one isn't too 
much more difficult than the last one.  How it works is the boss fires either 
fire or ice projectiles at you, and once again you have to block them with 
your shield.  The difference is that this time they aren't reflected, but 
rather absorbed.  You need to absorb three of the same elements in a row and 
then that will fire off a blast at the boss and knock her down.  For example 
if she fires ice, then ice, then ice, block all three of them and you'll fire 
an ice blast.  If she fires ice, fire, ice, ice, block the first ice, but 
dodge the fire attack and block the next two ice attacks.  You can also absorb 
three fire attacks in a row, the point is that they must be the same.  After 
knocking her out of the sky, jump to the platform and begin swinging your 
sword and using jump attacks.  With the Biggoron's Sword you should only have 
to knock her out two times before she dies.  Here's a tip for the best way to 
win the fight, hold down the L/Z targeting button and the R button.  When she 
launches an attack, if it's the one you want then block it, if not don't let 
go of the buttons, just hold left or right and tap A to jump to the side.
Defeating the boss nets you a HEART CONTAINER after the scene, then as usual 
you will be brought to see the sages and be given the SPIRIT MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      EFORE THE END --------------------------------------- 01.46 --+

Time to do a little clean up and finish off those heart pieces and Gold 
Skulltulas.  At this point there should only be one more Piece of Heart that 
you do not have, and there's nothing special about it at all, I simply 
neglected to mention it when the time came up originally.  All you have to do 
is make your way back to the Zora's Fountain, the one with the entrance to the 
Ice Cavern.  Stand on the ice and equip the Iron Boots, jump down into the 
deep water.  In the exact centre at the very bottom you will find PIECE OF 
HEART [036], the final piece of heart which will bring your total heart 
containers up to twenty.  Now for the final three Gold Skulltulas.

While still in the fountain area, head for the southeast side and play the 
Sun's Song so it becomes night time.  In front of the cave there is a large 
rock, lift it up using the Silver Gauntlets and drop down.  Equip the Lens of 
Truth so you can see the Skulltulas here.  Climb the ladder at the end and 
look at the wall on your left to find GOLD SKULLTULA [098].  After getting it, 
head back into Zora's Domain from here.  From the king's room that the path 
leading to the top of the waterfall and look to your left, there you should 
see another GOLD SKULLTULA [099].  Only one more to go now.

First before you do anything, play the Prelude of Light and warp to the Temple 
of Time.  Here you will trigger a scene, when it is all over Link will be 
granted the LIGHT ARROW which allows you to fire arrows of light from your 
Fairy Bow.  Leave the temple and head for Kakariko Village.  Begin climbing up 
the Death Mountain Trail.  As you approach the top, in the path were all the 
rocks are before the climbable wall, break the rocks using your Megaton 
Hammer.  During the night, the final GOLD SKULLTULA [100] will appear on the 
wall behind one of these rocks.  That completes the sidequest for you, it's 
time to head back to Kakariko Village.  Enter the House of Skulltula and speak 
to the guy in the middle, your reward for all this work is a pathetic HUGE 
RUPEE.  The only redeeming fact is that you can leave the house and come back 
to get an infinite number of them, but the real reward is the accomplishment.

Make your way back to Hyrule Castle Town and head up to Ganon's Castle.  Stand 
in front of the entrance at the edge of the cliff and a scene occurs.  
Afterward cross the rainbow bridge and then enter into Ganon's Castle.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ANON'S CASTLE --------------------------------------- 01.47 --+

Head down the stairs and through the door.  You'll notice that you cannot go 
inside the middle thing, but there are many other doors around.  Each door
