corresponds to a temple you have already completed.  No you do not have to 
complete them again, but you must face trials in each before you can proceed.

Head right and go through the door with the green emblem overhead.  Defeat the 
wolf enemy in this room and open the small chest to acquire a BLUE RUPEE.  
Equip Din's Fire and the Fire Arrows.  Shoot a Fire Arrow at the torch above 
the door and then use Din's Fire to light the other four torches around you.  
Head through the door that opens up.  In this room you need to equip the Hover 
Boots in order to get through.  Equip the boots and then walk to the left, be 
sure to do so only when the fan is blowing.  You'll get the first Silver Rupee 
and it will blow you to a platform.  After landing, roll over to the pillar 
with the button on top of it and press the button.  Turn around and Longshot 
to the target, then climb up and grab the second Silver Rupee.  From here run 
across the air to the platform in the middle with the laser statue and from 
there to the platform on the opposite side to get the third.  Roll back to it 
and turn right toward the exit door to get the fourth, climb up and get the 
fifth off the small platform when the fan is not going, then proceed through 
the door.  There is a weird gel-like object in this room, equip the Light 
Arrows and fire one at the core in the middle.  After this you will appear 
back in the main room again.  Go right but ignore the blue door, continue up 
the stairs to the top where you will find the door with purple above it.

At the beginning of this room you will see a chest on your left containing a 
BLUE RUPEE, you can Longshot to get over there if you wish.  From here equip 
the Hover Boots and shoot a Fire Arrows at the torch on the opposite side to 
spawn a number of platforms.  Run along them to get to the other platform wit 
the blob monster on it.  Ignore this monster because you have to move quick, 
keep going along to the next platform and from there roll off of it angled 
slightly to the right so you drop to an ice platform with a button on it.  
Stand on the button to spawn a treasure chest then turn around and Longshot to 
it.  The chest contains GOLDEN GAUNTLETS, an upgrade to the Silver Gauntlets 
which allow you to lift very heavy objects with your bare hands.

Now what you want to do is equip the Lens of Truth and look to your left, 
there you will find an invisible path which you can walk across to the rusted 
button.  Use the Megaton Hammer to smash it down and then follow the path 
back.  Find the other invisible path which takes you to the door and head 
through it.  Once again equip the Light Arrows and fire one at the core thing.

Now that you have the Golden Gauntlets it's time to leave Ganon's Castle for a 
moment.  Leave the castle and make a left at the bottom of the rainbow bridge, 
follow this path to the end and lift up that enormous rock to reveal another 
Great Fairy Fountain.  She will outline all of your hearts in white which 
doubles you health total, it will now take twice as many hits to kill you than 
before, one of the most helpful upgrades in the game.  Leave the fairy 
fountain and then cross the rainbow bridge and enter Ganon's Castle again.

Back in the main room choose to enter the door you passed before, the one with 
the blue circle above it.  You'll need at least one Empty Bottle for this.  
Break the icicles in front of you and collect some Blue Fire in one of your 
Empty Bottles.  Next, open the chest on the right to get a RECOVERY HEART but 
not the one on the left as it will just freeze you.  Use your Blue Fire on the 
red ice slab to melt it get some more Blue Fire and then proceed through the 
door.  You've got two minutes to do the following, grab hold of the block 
further away from you and push it to the right, and then down so it falls into 
the hole.  The pot on the side here has a fairy hidden under it if you're 
interested.  After that push the other block right, up and then left.  Climb 
on top of it and melt the red ice up here, use the Megaton Hammer to pound 
down the rusted button and then go through the door at the top.  Fire a Light 
Arrow at the core of this weird thing here to return to the main room.

Enter the door with the red circle above it and equip the Goron Tunic so that 
the timer disappears.  Also equip the Hover Boots as well.  Your goal here is 
to go around collecting all the Silver Rupees.  On your right is the first 
one, be careful because the platform will drop.  On your left you'll find the 
second one on a platform with some spinning flames.  The third one is on the 
left as well, use the Golden Gauntlets to lift the giant block.  After 
throwing this block it lands on the other side, go over to it and there you 
will find Silver Rupees four and five.  Proceed through the door at the top 
side, get there using the Longshot, and fire a Light Arrow at the core.

Head through the door with the orange circle above it this time.  Equip the 
Bombchus and destroy the laser statue to start and then begin moving about the 
room collecting all of the Silver Rupees.  Feel free to move the statues so 
that the spike traps move out of your way.  To get the final one in the 
middle, Longshot to the panel on the ceiling.  In the next room defeat the 
fiery slugs and then hit the crystal through the gate with your sword.  Open 
the chest which contains Bombchus and equip them.  Now look up at the top of 
the gate to see a hole, throw a Bombchu through there and it will hit the 
crystal on the other side, unlocking the gate.  In the final room, equip a 
Fire Arrow and aim it up at the ceiling, shoot the web and then stand in the 
beam and reflect the light with your Mirror Shield to reflect light at the 
correct sun emblem.  If you hit one of the wrong ones, be ready to move and 
avoid the hand that comes down to grab you.  Proceed through the door and fire 
a Light Arrow at the core here, that's five out of six now completed.

The last door is hidden behind a huge rock in the large room you can only move 
with the Golden Gauntlets, it's the yellow room.  Don't move at all when you 
get here, equip the normal Fairy Bow as well as the Lens of Truth, there is a 
Skulltula as well as three bats to eliminate in this room.  Once you've done 
so, open the chest in the middle of the room to get a SMALL KEY.  Use the 
Small Key to get into the next room where you will find a Triforce symbol.  
Stand on it and play Zelda's Lullaby on your Ocarina, a chest drops with 
another SMALL KEY and the door opens.  Here it's just a matter of collecting 
the four Silver Rupees around you while avoiding the boulders, then 
Longshotting to panel in the ceiling and getting the fifth before the one 
minute timer runs out.  In the next room watch out for the hand which drops 
from the ceiling and equip the lens of Truth to see the exit door.  Head 
through it and use the Light Arrows to shoot the core here as well.

Now that all six are taken care of, it's finally time to enter the centre 
structure.  Basically the point of this final area is to challenge your 
fighting ability, and if you've got the Biggoron's Sword it's not going to be 
much of a challenge at all.  At the top of the stairs, ignoring the flaming 
bats, the first battle is against two lizards.  Defeat them and then continue 
up to take on two Stalfos.  Defeat them and open the coloured chest to acquire 
the BOSS KEY.  Next up in the area above there are two simultaneous Iron 
Knuckles to eliminate.  You can take them on at once or one at a time if you 
wish.  Go through the door at the top of the stairs using the Boss Key and 
enter the room here.  Leave the pots alone and proceed through the door on the 
right.  Follow the long staircase up and prepare for the final battle.

The battle has begun, it's quite similar to one you've already fought before 
with Phantom Ganon.  What you need to do is jump from the middle platform to 
one of the corners and turn around.  Equip the Biggoron’s Sword and whip it 
out.  Ganondorf will fire an energy blast at you, deflect it with your sword 
and he'll deflect it back.  Keep doing this over and over again until 
eventually it hits him.  At this point you have a very short window of time to 
bring up a Light Arrow and fire it at him.  When he goes down either roll 
across to the middle platform with the Hover Boots equipped, or Longshot to 
Ganondorf himself and slash with the Biggoron's Sword.  Now that you have the 
idea, it's just a matter of repeating this pattern.  What I like to do is hit 
him with the arrow then quickly roll to the centre platform, let loose a Jump 
Attack and then swing like mad.  Doing this it will only take a mere three 
knock downs to actually take him out, but the battle isn't over yet.

The castle is being destroyed, you have to escape from it.  Begin by following 
the princess down the outer walkway and through the gate which she will open 
for you.  Keep following her in the next room and go through the gate.  This 
continues for a but until she is trapped by a flame barrier.  At this point 
you need to quickly defeat the two Stalfos enemies to rescue her and keep 
going.  Be careful of the flaming rocks the rest of the way down.  If you have 
the defensive upgrade to your health then it shouldn't be too much to worry 
about though.  There's another scene and then the true final battle begins.

The first thing of course that you will notice when this battle begins is that 
you do not have your Master Sword for the fight, there are two weapons of 
choice here, obviously the Biggoron's Sword takes top place but given that you 
won't necessarily have it, the Megaton Hammer comes second.  If you were 
hoping for a big and challenging boss battle you likely won't find it here, 
but still you're in for an impressive fight against an enormous enemy, which 
still works out quite well.  The tactic to use here is relatively simple, with 
whatever weapon you are using, roll under Ganon's legs and slash like crazy at 
his tail.  Roll out of the way to avoid his sword swings and repeat this 
pattern over and over again.  Ganon takes quite a few hits to go down.  The 
alternative to this is targeting his face with the L/Z button and firing a 
Light Arrow to stun him, then simply running around behind and doing the same 
thing.  The first way is faster and consumes less magic, although the second 
way will allow you to have time to use a Jump Attack.  At about the half way 
point Ganon will fall and Zelda will allow you to grab the Master Sword again.  
The second half of the battle is exactly the same.  Ganon might move a little 
faster but it's nothing much different.  Keep attacking like crazy with the 
Biggoron's Sword, but near the end of the battle you're going to have to 
switch to the Master Sword.  The battle can only be won with a swing of the 
Master Sword, no other weapon will suffice.  Strike the final blow and...

That's it, you have saved Hyrule.  All that remains now is to watch the ending 
sequence of the game.  Congratulations of finishing Ocarina of Time.
______________________________________________________________________________


______________________________________________________________________________
~~\/~~~~~~~\/~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\/~~~~~~~\/~~
  || 02.00 ||                Sidequests & Minigames              || 02.00 ||
__||_______||____________________________________________________||_______||__
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~
All sidequests are covered in the main walkthrough, if you followed the whole 
walkthrough and are now checking this section for more to do you will likely 
be disappointed to find nothing new, however if you played through the game 
without help, you likely missed some interesting little minigames and such 
here and there, so this section will take you through them all one by one.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ HOOTING GALLERY -------------------------------------------- 02.01 --+

Locate the Shooting Gallery, it is the house in the Hyrule Market with the 
large target symbol and arrow through it, this place should not be too 
difficult to find.  What you have to do in the Shooting Gallery is pay 20 
Rupees in order to begin the game.  You are given fifteen shots and you have 
to shoot down ten Rupees.  If you shoot down all ten you will get a special 
prize.  Each Rupee appears in the exact same position every single time:

1 - The first one appears directly in the middle of the screen, a green one.  
    It is not moving so simply fire at it head on when you see it.
2 - The second one is a blue one that moves in from the left side of the 
    screen toward the middle and stays just a bit to the left.
3 - The third one comes in from the right side of the screen toward the 
    middle and stays just a bit right of the middle, a blue one.
4 - The fourth is a green one which pops out of the middle, up into the air
    and then drops down, hit it at the highest point of the jump.
5 - The next one is a blue Rupees similar to the second one however it comes
    from the left near the middle at the same time as number six
6 - Comes in at the same time as number five but from the right side, you
    have to hit both of them before they both exit the screen.
7 - This is a red Rupee which moves at a moderate speed from the right side of 
    the screen to the left side of the screen, it will not stop.
8 - This is a red Rupee which moves at a moderate speed from the right side of 
    the screen to the left side of the screen, it will not stop.
9 - This is another red one which this time moves from the left side of the
    screen to the right side of the screen, quite quickly so aim fast.
10 - This is another red one which this time moves from the left side of the
     screen to the right side of the screen, quite quickly so aim fast.

If you manage to hit eight out of the ten targets then you will be rewarded 
with a free game, if you manage to hit ten out of the ten targets then you 
will be rewarded with a BULLET BAG for your Deku Seeds, this allows you to 
carry 40 Deku Seeds for use with your Fairy Slingshot instead of 30, and is 
quite a helpful item.  Be sure to win this item as it will likely help out.

Quite awhile later when you have become adult Link, the Shooting Gallery is 
located in Kakariko Village.  Instead of using the Fairy Slingshot now, you 
will be using the Fairy Bow.  The Shooting Gallery is the building in the 
centre of town which did not even exist when you were Young Link.  The 
Shooting Gallery is going to be exactly as you remember it with one seemingly 
minor yet important difference, the order in which the Rupees appear is 
entirely randomized.  All the positions for them are the same, such as the 
fast moving Red Rupees, the jumping Green Rupee, but the order in which they 
appear changes.  Your goal is to anticipate and hit all ten of the Rupees once 
again, except this time using your Fairy Bow.  If you can manage to hit all 
ten then you will be rewarded with the BIG QUIVER, a special item which allows 
you to carry a total of 40 Arrows instead of 30.  If you do it again after 
this your reward will only be 50 Rupees.  Keep in mind that if you manage to 
hit at least eight out of ten, you are still given a free game after that.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ UPER CUCCO CATCHING ---------------------------------------- 02.02 --+

You'll find this game at the Lon Lon Ranch.  Upon arriving in Lon Lon Ranch 
what you want to do is go through the door with the sign over top of it.  
There are all sorts of chickens running around in this room and it is going to 
be the focus of a minigame.  Speak with Talon and for ten Rupees you can 
challenge yourself to try to find all three Super Cuccos in thirty seconds.  
Basically it's just a matter of lifting chickens and throwing them out of the 
way until you pick up the correct ones.  Try to keep your eye on one of the 
Super Cuccos at the beginning and then just lifting chickens randomly to find 
the other two.  After you have located all three of the Super Cuccos, Talon 
will reward your with some LON LON MILK.  It's not actually the milk that is 
worth it but rather the EMPTY BOTTLE that you have after the milk is gone.
______________________________________________________________________________


     )   ___     
    (__/_____)   
      /          
     /
+-- (______) UCCO CATCHING ----------------------------------------- 02.03 --+

This "minigame" can be found in Kakariko Village during the day only.  Head 
back to the graveyard entrance but don't actually go into the graveyard, 
instead speak with the woman standing just opposite the entrance with her 
hands on her face beside the chicken coop.  Speak to her and she will ask you 
to bring back her Cuccos to the pen.  The first one is easy, it should be 
walking around right beside you near the entrance to the graveyard, grab it 
with the A button and throw it into the fenced area beside the girl.  Go back 
to the entrance of the village and you will find a second Cucco wandering 
around minding its own business.  Pick it up over your head with the A button 
and then run all the way east up the stairs, and turn around to find another 
set of stairs going up to that incomplete brick house.  There is a special 
neat trick about Cuccos, when you jump off a cliff the violent flapping of 
their wings will allow you to glide.  Jump off the edge of this incomplete 
house with the Cucco still in hand and glide directly west to reach the little 
raised ledge with the Cucco, behind that fence that you could not access 
otherwise.  Throw your Cucco back down into the middle of town and take this 
one over to the chicken coop.  Go back to the main middle area of town with 
the tree and notice the crate against the house when you face northeast.  
Break this crate by rolling into it and grab the Cucco, bring it back to the 
chicken coop.  From the middle of town take the large north set of stairs up 
toward the path that leads to Death Mountain, you should come across a Cucco 
there which is just minding its own business.  Pick up this Cucco and bring it 
back to the girl.  Grab hold of the fourth one, which is the one you threw off 
the edge when you landed and got the Cucco behind the fence.  Don't throw it 
back, but head toward the graveyard entrance, climb the stairs leading north.

You should still have the Cucco in hand like I said, you'll need it here in 
order to glide.  Notice that if you run straight north and jump off the edge, 
you can't quite make it over the large fence, don't worry.  Notice that beside 
the large fence is a smaller fence that actually extends out further toward 
you, this fence you CAN barely glide over so do that now, you'll land beside a 
door.  Face the main area where you just came from and throw the Cucco back, 
you can get it easily afterward.  Jump down behind you and you'll land beside 
a hole, drop down into the hole and open the small treasure chest to acquire a 
RED RUPEE.  Climb out of this hole and then go north to find a Cucco beside 
the ladder.  Lift it up and throw it over the fence like you did the other 
one, it will be there waiting for you after.  Now climb that fence up to where 
the windmill is and grab the final Cucco.  Use this one to glide back across 
and throw it into the chicken coop.  Now all you have to do is find those two 
which you threw over the fence for later and that takes care of all seven.  
Speak to the girl and she will reward you nicely with another EMPTY BOTTLE.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      OMBCHU BOWLING ALLEY -------------------------------- 02.04 --+

After you have completed the Dodongo's Cavern the Bombchu Bowling Alley in 
Hyrule Market is open for business.  Each game costs 30 Rupees and you'll 
likely be playing a lot so be sure to bring a lot of money with you.  Here's 
how it works, there are a number of prizes which are randomized.  The prizes 
are Bombs, Purple Rupee, Bombchus, PIECE OF HEART [010] and BIGGEST BOMB BAG, 
the final two are obviously the ones you'll really be working toward.  Each 
game gives you a total of ten Bombchus and three targets, the first and final 
targets are always identical while the middle one is randomized.  You can lift 
one Bombchu into the air with the B button, once lifted the bomb is activated 
and it's on a timer so do not lift until you are fully ready to throw.  A 
Bombchu will go in a straight line ahead of where you throw it, but it will 
not explode when it hits a wall, rather it will climb that wall.  You have to 
use your geometric knowledge and angles and such to win this game:

1 - For this one, the easiest one all you have to do is stand in the middle in 
    front of the blue arrow and throw a Bombchu straight ahead.  It should go 
    into the hole in the middle and explode, taking down the wall.

2 - As I mentioned there are three possible targets for this one and they are
    randomized.  The easiest is when the target appears in the middle.  
    Before you only had to deal with the spike trap obstacle but now you
    have to get it past that and the Cucco as well.  Wait for the Cucco
    to move or when it's about to move and throw your Bombchu straight ahead.
    The next possibility is for it to appear on the far left.  For this one 
    stand a couple black notches left of the middle and look straight ahead.  
    Tap the analog stick extremely lightly to the left and throw to get it in.  
    The third and final possibility is that it will appear on the far right.  
    This is similar to the one on the far left however I find you have to 
    stand closer to the right wall for this one than stood close to the left 
    wall for the other one.  Rather than standing a few notches off the 
    centre, stand a few notches left or the right wall and tap the analog 
    stick weakly to the right.  It should go down and then into the hole.

3 - This final one is the most difficult, a large Cucco appears in the middle 
    and while it is very possible to get it down the middle past both of them, 
    it's extremely difficult and not worth doing.  Instead stand exactly where 
    you would stand if it was on the far left, about two or three black 
    notches left of the middle arrow.  Now tap the analog stick on a 45 
    degree angle to the upper right, or northeast then let loose.  If you're 
    lucky it will curve around the wall and into the hole eventually.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   IVING GAME ------------------------------------------ 02.05 --+

You'll find this one in Zora's Domain, take the path along until you reach a 
large set of stairs.  At the top there is a big, gross, thing.  Ignore it for 
now and make a left turn, follow the path through the tunnel leading west and 
speak to the Zora at the end.  You will have the option of playing the Diving 
Game for 20 Rupees.  The Diving Game is one of the easiest minigames ever, 
your goal is to jump down into the water and collect all five of the Blue 
Rupees.  Dive into the water from the waterfall above and then hold down the 
L/Z trigger button.  This should give you a nice view from above which will 
allow you to see your shadow.  Position yourself close to one of the five Blue 
Rupees and hold the A button to dive down.  At the bottom, hold the analog 
stick in whatever the direction the Rupee is in, in the event you are not 
directly over top of it.  You should be able to collect them all for sure.  

After successfully collecting all five Blue Rupees, head back up to where you 
started the game and speak to the Zora there once again.  The Zora will reward 
you with a SILVER SCALE which allows you to dive for six seconds rather than a 
mere three seconds, this means of course that you can go deeper as well.  
After this you can still play the game as much as you like and make money off 
of it.  More money in fact, now the Zora will throw Red Rupees in the water 
along with the blue ones.  Some green ones two, but you'll still make a lot.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ________) 
      (, /       
        /___,    
     ) /         
+-- (_/   ISHING POND ---------------------------------------------- 02.06 --+

The Fishing Pond is located in Lake Hylia, on the far eastern side.  Speak to 
the guy at the counter and tell him you wish to go fishing, it only costs 20 
Rupees.  Here's how to play.  Walk up to the water and press the L/Z button to 
look at a fish, then you can cast your line with the B button and press the A 
button to break the line.  Wiggle the lure in with the analog stick and reel 
it in using the A and R buttons.  Press A and down to set the hook.

The pond is filled to the brim with fish so if you want to catch anything big 
you're going to have to prepare yourself in advance.  Look around the pond and 
even swim around the pond trying to find the biggest fish you can.  The one 
you are looking for is almost always situated around the middle, in a tiny 
little area around one of those logs, he's bigger than everything else so he 
should stand out.  Launch your line from a distance and try to catch this 
particular fish, really he's the only one you're going to be interested in.  
Once you eventually catch this fish go back and talk to the guy at the 
counter, as a reward for the ten pound fish you get PIECE OF HEART [014].

Fishing as Adult Link is quite similar to fishing as Young Link, the one main 
difference is that the fish are bigger.  Your goal is to catch the fish in the 
very middle of the pond, the enormous one swimming around the broken logs and 
stuff, it's pretty much always in the same place.  This fish takes forever to 
actually bite your hook, I must have cast at least two dozen times or more.  
It eventually did bite, sometime during the daylight hours.  This fish should 
be at least fifteen pounds or more, which will net you the reward GOLDEN SCALE 
and allow you to dive underwater for nine seconds as opposed to six.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ APPY MASK SALESMAN ----------------------------------- 02.07 --+

The Happy Mask shop is located in Hyrule Market, this sidequest can be done 
while playing as Young Link, however you must have acquired all three of the 
Spiritual Stones.    Enter the Happy Mask Shop and speak to the owner, choose 
to borrow the KEATON MASK.  It is now your job to find a customer who will buy 
this mask from you, and it's not going to be too difficult.  Just leave Hyrule 
Castle Town and make your way back to Kakariko Village.  Set the mask to one 
of your C Buttons and then equip it, after doing that speak with the soldier 
guarding the north exit and he will pay you 15 Rupees for the mask.

Head back to the Happy Mask Shop in the Market once again and this time borrow 
the SKULL MASK.  Your destination now is going to be Kokiri Forest.  Head from 
there up into The Lost Woods.  Make a right at the beginning, then a left, 
then a right, then a left and then a left.  This is the area with the dead 
tree, and two Deku Scrubs.  At the back left corner, not quite in the corner 
but in that general area, there is a hole in the ground hidden underneath the 
grass.  Run around until you drop down it.  At the bottom take a few steps 
forward so that you are overlooking all the Deku Scrubs under the ground and 
equip the Skull Mask.  They will quickly pop out of the ground and start 
running around.  Speak with the Deku Scrub on your right in order to get the 
next DEKU STICK UPGRADE.  Now head back to the entrance of The Lost Woods and 
make a left turn.  Here you will find the Skull Kid up on the pillar.  Jump 
onto the small stump here and press the A button while wearing the mask, Skull 
Kid will give you ten Rupees for it.  Oh well, no money this time.  

Return to Hyrule Market and the Happy Mask Shop again, pay back the shop owner 
the 20 Rupees for a loss of 10 Rupees.  No matter, now you can borrow the 
SPOOKY MASK from him, be sure to do that.  Your destination with this mask is 
going to be the same as the first one, Kakariko Village once again.  Make sure 
you enter the village during the way.  Head for the Graveyard and you will 
find some kind walking around here, equip the Spooky Mask and speak to him, 
he'll buy the mask for full price.  Return to the shop and pay it back.

Head to the shops and spend all of your money, on anything, who cares what, it 
doesn't matter.  The point is to just use up all of your money buying stuff.  
Now there is one final mask now to sell and it's the most difficult, but not 
by much.  Take the BUNNY HOOD from the shop and leave town.  Head southwest 
from Hyrule Town to Lon Lon Ranch, there is a man running laps around the 
ranch.  Once you find this man speak to him while wearing the Bunny Hood and 
he will pay you for it.  Basically what he does is just give you money until 
your wallet is full, that's why I said to waste all the money you had since he 
just fills it to the limit again.  Return to Hyrule Market and give the owner 
of the shop the 50 Rupees, the other 450 are yours to keep.  There is also a 
special bonus, you are now free to borrow the MASK OF TRUTH whenever you want 
to.  The Mask of Truth allows you to listen to all those Gossip Stones placed 
conveniently around the world, before they really didn't do anything.  You can 
also now borrow the GORON MASK, GERUDO MASK and ZORA MASK but they don't 
really serve any purpose besides changing conversations between random people.  
Since you can only have one mask in your inventory at a time, hang onto the 
Mask of Truth as it is the only one which really does anything.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ____  ___)
      (, /   /
        /---/
     ) /   /  
+-- (_/   (__ ORSE RACING ------------------------------------------ 02.08 --+

You have to be adult Link for this one, head to Lon Lon (or Ingo's) ranch and 
he will offer to let you ride one of his horses.  Play Epona's Song and Epona 
the horse will ride right up to you, Epona is the best horse of course.

While you are riding the horse, approach Ingo while riding Epona and press the 
L/Z trigger to target him.  Speak to Ingo and he will ask if you want to bet 
50 Rupees on a race, you certainly do.  When the race begins you will have to 
do one lap around the fenced area against Ingo.  The most important thing in 
the entire race is simply not to get caught on one of the corners, it will 
slow you down immensely.  Stay closer to the wall but not so close that you 
are touching it and everything will be good.  Never use more than five carrots 
at a time and other than that, just race until you eventually win.

When you do win however, Ingo challenges you to a second race and bets the 
horse Epona on this one.  It's going to be a fair bit more difficult but I 
think you can manage it.  Here's the trick I use to win this.  Right at the 
beginning of the race don't really bother with any carrots right away, use one 
just after you start to keep up as you go around the first corner, then 
another one maybe just to keep up.  After the first or second corner, move 
away from the wall a but and use all the rest of your carrots quickly until 
you are down to one, it should be enough to pass him early.  The purpose of 
this is to be ahead of him on the straightaway on the opposite side, you won't 
have any carrots, but you'll be ahead.  While you are ahead simply ride at 
normal speed and move around so that you are always in front of Ingo, he won't 
be able to pass you.  By the time you reach the next corner all six of your 
carrots will have replenished and you will still be ahead of him, use five of 
them quickly to get around and you'll be far ahead when you cross the line.

Winning the race does indeed net you the horse however Ingo closes the gate 
and will not let you leave the ranch.  When you have a powerful and fast 
jumping horse though, this isn't exactly a problem.  Ignore the gate and note 
the fence that runs on both sides along the east and west walls.  You may 
think that these are too high however use a couple of carrots and run toward 
either outer wall and Epona will jump the fence and jump out of Lon Lon Ranch.

There is one more aspect to this, an obstacle course.  Before you can do it 
you must wake up Talon which is the first part of the Biggoron sidequest 
explained below.  After he has been woken up and heads back to the ranch, 
follow him there and speak to Malon two times, she will offer to let you race 
and obstacle course.  The time to beat is 50 Seconds and she says that if you 
beat it you will get a present.  The obstacle course isn't going to be too 
easy, you have to make two laps around in the fifty seconds allotted and there 
are fences that you have to jump.  Even if you seem to do everything perfectly 
you often won't beat the time so here are a couple of tips.  At the beginning 
use a fair number of carrots but don't use any as you go around toward the 
long straight on the other side, it's just small gates and your carrots will 
replenish.  Use as many as you need to get over the medium and high gates on 
the long side and then when you land let one or two replenish and use all of 
them down until you only have one left going over the last medium gate toward 
the finish line.  Let them replenish as you cross the line and start over with 
the exact same strategy again, except this time when you get near the finish 
line just waste all of them to get across as fast as possible.  Assuming you 
get a time of 0:49 or better then Malon will give you a present, she says it 
is too big to give you so she'll have it delivered to your home.  The present 
is in fact a whole cow that now lives in your house in Kokiri Forest.  I guess 
you can get all the Lon Lon Milk you want quite easily now.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   AMPE RACING ----------------------------------------- 02.09 --+

Head to the Kakariko Village Graveyard as Adult Link and move the tombstone 
next to the magic bean sprout.  You will reveal a hole that you can drop down 
and find Dampe's ghost there.  Agree to race him and you have two goals, 
finish the race to get the HOOKSHOT and finish the race in under one minute to 
get the PIECE OF HEART [019].  Follow these directions carefully and you 
cannot lose, assuming you manage to avoid the fire.  First of all you have to 
keep rolling in order to win this, rolling is indeed faster than running for 
the most part.  Jump down at the start and begin chasing after him, make a 
right, then a left and follow him straight between the purple flames.  Go left 
at the fork and follow him down the hall then make another left and left again 
at the next two forks, this will take you to the door.  Through the door turn 
right a couple of times to reach the stone room, move toward the right wall 
diagonally while still rolling.  Make a left at the next fork then a right at 
the purple flames to reach the second door.  In this room don't follow him, 
instead move diagonally to the right and you will cut across and save some 
time.  Head up to those ledges you have to climb, and climb up the left one.  
Go left when you reach a fork and this takes you to that walkway that goes 
around to the final door.  With rolling a time of sub one minute is possible.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      IG POE CATCHING ------------------------------------- 02.10 --+

First of all, in order to catch the Big Poes you must be riding Epona and you 
must have the Fairy Bow.  Assuming you have met these conditions, get on your 
horse and get ready to make chase.  The very first Big Poe is found simply by 
getting on Epona right at the gate to Hyrule Castle Town and riding down the 
path directly to the south.  A ghost should appear, at this point jam the A 
button to give your horse about four carrots to keep up and then hold B, 
quickly fire one arrow at the Big Poe and then another arrow.  When it dies it 
will drop something, assuming you have Empty Bottles use an Empty Bottle to 
catch the BIG POE.  You will need a new bottle for each one that you catch.

Since you still have two Empty Bottles on you let's keep going.  The next one 
we're going to get is over west of the entrance to Hyrule Castle Town, beyond 
the wall head for the northwest corner of the field and follow the stream 
down, just away from it a bit and you should find the next BIG POE.  After 
this we are going to want to head for the entrance to Gerudo Valley.  
Southeast from there a little bit is a fork in the path kind of which splits 
into two, three if you count the one you are coming from.  At this point if 
you go north you should manage to find the third BIG POE.  Eliminate it and 
collect it with your third Empty Bottle.  Now that all three are filled, 
return to the entrance of Hyrule Castle Town, that small room with all the 
pots inside and speak to the guy there.  He'll give you rewards if you equip 
the Big Poes and use them while standing in front of him.  He says that you'll 
get a special prize once you reach 1000 points, and since each Poe nets you 
100 points, that means you must collect a total of ten.  Leave this place and 
return to Hyrule Field, we've got lots more Big Poes to catch here.

Make your way down to Lon Lon Ranch on your horse and head for the north side.  
Notice that just outside the entrance to the ranch there is a tree, when you 
approach this tree another BIG POE is going to appear, this will be your 
fourth.  The fifth is an extremely difficult one to get.  Make your way toward 
Kakariko Village and when you cross the bridge go east toward the river under 
that cliff ledge thing.  The Big Poe might appear here, but if you stay close 
to the wall it will not.  Turn around and ride back toward the tree in front 
of the Kakariko Village entrance, if you're lucky the BIG POE will appear 
there, if not it will likely appear over the river out of reach.  That's the 
fifth.  One more before we go back and redeem our spirits again.  Next you 
will want to take a look at your map.  Notice a big ugly "y" shape made by the 
paths in between Lon Lon Ranch and Kokiri Forest?  Your destination is the 
fork in the path where all three of them meet, there you will find a rock and 
when you approach the rock, the sixth BIG POE will appear.  Catch it in your 
bottle and then make your way back to Hyrule Castle Town to sell all three.

We're definitely getting closer now.  For the seventh one you are going to 
have to return to the area where you got the previous one, the fork in the 
path between Lon Lon Ranch and Kokiri Forest.  As you make your way south from 
here you will notice a green tree and beside this tree are a bunch of little 
grass shrubs.  Run through these shrubs and the next BIG POE should appear.  
Now this may sound weird, but the next one after this is almost in the exact 
same place.  Ride off for a bit and turn around, then come back.  Ride through 
the forest, through the area a bit east of here and you should be able to find 
yet another BIG POE, that covers eight of them.  For the ninth you are going 
to want to head north of the "y" fork.  Notice that east of Lon Lon Ranch 
there is a stone wall which curves in the shape of a boomerang.  Get on the 
east side of this wall, the lower side and ride your horse directly along the 
edge of it.  At the bend in the middle, the ninth BIG POE will appear and you 
will be able to capture it.  At this point head back to Hyrule Castle town and 
redeem the three Poes you have, there is only one more remaining.  

Lastly there is only one more Big Poe remaining.  To find this one head back 
west toward the Gerudo Valley.  Remember the earlier one you got around the 
fork in the path, southeast of Gerudo Valley?  Well north of that fork there 
is a lone tree and when you ride past this tree, the tenth BIG POE will appear 
and allow you to capture it.  That covers all ten of the Big Poes you need, 
return to Hyrule Castle Town one final time now.  Enter the small room and 
present the final Poe to the ghost man, he will give you the fourth and final 
EMPTY BOTTLE in return for you work.  Coupled with the 500 Rupees you have 
already made from it, the reward is reasonably nice all things considered.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ________) 
      (, /       
        /___,    
     ) /         
+-- (_/   ROGGY MINIGAME ------------------------------------------- 02.11 --+

This minigame, found in Zora's River by standing on one of those logs in the 
water, can only be played after you have made all the frogs grow in size, 
consists of you playing notes that correspond to each specific frog.  A 
butterfly will appear over the frogs' heads and you have to press the button 
for that frog extremely quickly or the game ends.  It's actually quite 
difficult even when you know the mapping (from left to right, the frog buttons 
are A, left, down, up, right).  Fortunately the solution is always the same, 
meaning all you really have to do is read off of this screen and press the 
buttons quickly, you don't even have to look at the frogs.  The solution is as 
follows: (A, left, right, down, left, right, down, A, down, A, down, right, 
left, A).  Doing this will net you a reward, the PIECE OF HEART [030].
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      IGGORON'S SWORD ------------------------------------- 02.12 --+

This is one of the longest and most time consuming sidequests in the game 
however the eventual reward, the best sword, is obviously going to be worth 
it.  The sidequest begins in Kakariko Village when you are Adult Link.  Make 
your way to the southeast corner of town where that woman is beside the 
chicken coop, the same woman who wanted you to help her find all the Cuccos 
seven years ago.  She tells you about a new type of Cucco she has been 
breeding called a Pocket Cucco.  She gives you the POCKET EGG which will hatch 
overnight just like the other egg.  After acquiring it, play the Sun's Song 
once, and then again to make the egg hatch into a POCKET CUCCO.  Once you have 
the Pocket Cucco, head to the very middle of town and go into the house just 
to the northeast.  In here you will find Talon sleeping on a bed.  Stand 
beside him with your new Pocket Cucco equipped on one of the C Buttons and use 
it.  Talon should wake up and then when you talk to him, he should mention 
something about going back to the ranch.  You have now accomplished your goal, 
return and speak with the girl standing beside the chicken coop once again.  
After speaking with her, use the Pocket Cucco and assuming it woke up Talon, 
it will be happy like she asked.  As a reward for what you have done she gives 
you COJIRO, a special Blue Cucco which has stopped crowing entirely.

Your next destination after this is going to be the Lost Woods in Kokiri.  
Just as you enter the Lost Woods, make a left and head down that path.  There 
is a man sitting beside the stump here in this area, but unfortunately he is 
sleeping.  That however can be remedied quite easily, equip the blue Cucco 
Cojiro on one of your C-Buttons and then use it while standing in front of the 
man.  He offers to give you an item which will go bad if you don't deliver it 
quickly, I think we have time to make a quick detour.  Accept his offer and he 
gives you the ODD MUSHROOM.  You now have three minutes to get to the Potion 
Shop in Kakariko Village.  If you do not have Epona then you can pretty much 
forget being able to do this.  Since you should have the horse though it won't 
be too difficult.  What you need to do is quickly get out of the Lost Woods 
and jump onto your horse.  Ride the horse all the way back to Kakariko 
Village.  At the north end of Kakariko Village there is the Potion Shop, go 
inside and take the back door to get around behind, the other option would be 
to Hookshot onto the roof and drop down to reach that fenced area at the back.  
Climb the stairs and enter the other door back there, it will take you to a 
witch.  Use the Odd Mushroom while standing in front of her and she will give 
you the ODD MEDICINE.  With that in hand you now need to head back to the Lost 
Woods once again.  Go to where the man was sitting previously and there you 
will find a small girl.  Stand in front of her and use the Odd Medicine to 
acquire the POACHER'S SAW.  Your quest for the large sword is proceeding well.

Right now the trading item you should have in your inventory is the Poacher's 
Saw.  Make your way now to the Gerudo Valley.  Approach the bridge which is 
totally broken and equip the Longshot.  Use the Longshot to hook onto the 
target on the other side and get across.  When you do this, go and speak to 
the fat guy here.  Equip the Poacher's Saw onto one of your C buttons and use 
it while standing in front of him.  He'll offer you the BROKEN GORON'S SWORD 
in return for the Poacher's Saw.  Next head to Kakariko Village.

Leave Kakariko Village through the north exit and begin the trek up the Death 
Mountain Trail.  When you reach the Magic Bean plant, step on it and jump off 
as it goes up past the bomb flower.  During the night you should hear 
something crawling around here, equip the Megaton Hammer and break the rock 
beside the bomb flower to find GOLD SKULLTULA [079].  After doing this jump 
back down and ride the Magic Bean plant again up to its highest point and jump 
off.  Make your way up the climbing wall and on your right at the top is the 
Biggoron.  Use the Broken Goron's Sword in front of him and he'll recognize 
it.  He says that before he can repair anything, he's going to need some 
Eyedrops from King Zora.  In return he gives you the PRESCRIPTION you need.

Make your way to Zora's River, west of Kakariko Village.  If you don't take 
Epona, then before you enter the river play Epona's Song so that the horse is 
close by for when you leave.  Navigate through the river and into Zora's 
Domain, stand on the platform before the king and use the Prescription.  He 
will give you the EYEBALL FROG which is needed in order to fill the 
prescription, get it to the Lake Hylia Laboratory as soon as possible.  
Quickly run down and get out of Zora's Domain toward the river (approximate 
time remaining 2:45) then jump into the river and run down all the way to 
Hyrule Field (approximate time remaining 2:00), now get on Epona and ride down 
to the south end and into Lake Hylia (approximate time remaining 1:00).  Head 
for the lab and go inside, use the frog beside the old man here (approximate 
time remaining: plenty).  He'll give you WORLD'S FINEST EYE DROPS.

These don't have a very long shelf life either so you need to get back to the 
Biggoron as quickly as possible, here's what to do.  Leave the house and get 
on Epona, then return to the Hyrule Map (approximate time remaining: 3:45).  
Now ride Epona all the way north to Kakariko Village and go inside 
(approximate time remaining: 2:40).  Make your way up to the Death Mountain 
Trail (approximate time remaining: 2:15).  From here ride the Magic Bean Plant 
up to the top and get to the bottom of the climbable wall (approximate time 
remaining: 1:30).  Use your Longshot and eliminate all the enemies on the 
wall, this is very important and you will save a ton of time killing them 
instead of being knocked off.  Climb the wall after they are dead and use the 
Eyedrops in front of the Biggoron to get the CLAIM CHECK for the sword.

He says to wait a few days, and that's just what you're going to do.  Play the 
Sun's Song about five times or so and speak to him again to get the BIGGORON'S 
SWORD.  This is the most powerful sword in the game, and it's yours!  It will 
make boss battles and more significantly easier.  The power of the sword is 
equivalent to that of the Giant's Knife except that unlike the knife, it 
cannot break.  Unfortunately you can't use it along with your shield, but with 
something like this, who cares!  You now have the ultimate weapon.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ HOOTING RANGE ---------------------------------------------- 02.13 --+

The shooting range is located in the Gerudo Fortress, you must have Epona and 
head to the eastern area where all of the targets are located to begin.
The game can only be played during the day, the Gerudo to speak to will not 
appear at night.  Make sure you are on your horse when you speak to her and 
the game will begin.  The cost to play one round is twenty Rupees.

Here's how it works, there are a number of targets and pots along the path.  
You don't control the horse, it moves automatically and you simply fire.  You 
are given twenty arrows each time you try.  Prizes are awarded for 1000 and 
1500 points.  To maximize your score, follow this strategy: on your first run, 
aim your bow and fire arrows at every single pot you pass, each one of them is 
worth 100 points.  After passing the last pot turn yourself forward and aim up 
at the target ahead of you.  Hitting the centre is worth 100 points, the outer 
circle is 60 points and the edge is 30 points.  Try to aim approximately two 
shots for the centre and the horse runs around it.  On the way back aim for 
the targets behind the pots this time.  Aim for about two shots per target and 
when you pass them all, fire all your remaining arrows at the south target.  
Depending on how well you are able to hit the centre, you will eventually 
reach 1500 points.  Here are a couple of random tips.  When shooting the pots, 
position your bow on the same level and don't move it, let the movement of the 
horse guide you.  Simply tap the B button not quite as fast as you possibly 
can, but still at a fairly quick and steady pace and you should be able to get 
all the pots easily.  The second tip is to always be aware that wherever you 
point the tip of the arrow when aiming, the actual shot will go diagonally up 
and to the right of that point, the further the target, the further up-right.

Your prize for successfully accumulating 1000 points is a PIECE OF HEART 
[033].  Your prize for managing to get 1500 points, which is actually quite 
difficult, is the BIGGEST QUIVER which increases your maximum arrow carrying 
capacity to 50 arrows.  That's everything you can win from this game.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ERUDO TRAINING GROUND ------------------------------- 02.14 --+

The Gerudo Training Ground is located in the Gerudo Fortress, after you can 
walk around there freely, speak to the person on the southeastern side.  
She'll let you enter the training ground for just a mere ten Rupees.

Enter the dungeon and climb the stairs.  Equip the Fairy Bow and do a 180 
degree turn, above the door where you entered there is an eyeball switch, fire 
an arrow at it to spawn a small chest containing BLUE RUPEE.  At the top of 
the stairs make a left and proceed through the door.  There are two Stalfos 
enemies in this room and you need to defeat them before the time limit of one 
minute runs out, obviously make sure you're using the Biggoron's Sword for 
this.  After killing them both a chest appears containing SMALL KEY, pick it 
up and go through the door beside the chest to enter the next hall.

In here you have one minute and thirty seconds to collect all the Silver 
Rupees.  Ignore the first right turn and take the second right, go right at 
the top of the ramp and get the Silver Rupee before the end, be careful 
because boulders are rolling down here.  Turn around and go back up the hill, 
go left not at the path where you came from but the one after it.  At the top 
of the ramp look left and you'll see two Silver Rupees.  Stand beside the 
first one and equip the Longshot.  Fire the Longshot at the panel on the 
ceiling you can see past the Rupees, it should take you through the first one 
and drop you on the second one, now you're up to three.  When you fall you'll 
be back near the entrance door again.  Face away from the door as if you had 
just come into the room and take the path on the left.  Go left again and grab 
the fourth Silver Rupee just before the edge.  Boulders are rolling down here 
as well.  The fifth Silver Rupee is located at the top of this hill.  
Afterward you'll notice that you cannot get to the door because of the flame 
barriers.  Equip the Longshot and shoot for the ceiling panel to get over 
them.  After that, there's nothing else here so enter into the next room.

Defeat the four wolf enemies in this room and then open the small chest 
appears to get a bunch of ARROWS.  Equip the Lens of Truth and look at the 
door on the right, there is a hidden passage above it for you to Longshot 
into.  When you get up there step on the button and proceed into the next 
room.  Jump down onto the platform in the centre and equip the Fairy Bow.  
Shoot the eyeball of the statue and make your way around from left to right in 
a circle, shooting the eyeballs as you go along.  When all four of them are 
lit up a chest appears containing a SMALL KEY.  Longshot to the panel on the 
wall and then you'll drop down in front of the door.  Head through it.

There are a number of enemies in this room, all you really have to do is 
defeat them all and a chest will appear containing ARROWS.  After doing this 
equip the megaton Hammer and smash all the statues at the sides of the room.  
Shoot the eyeball switch to unlock the door and step on the button to lower 
the fire in the middle and allow you to open the chest for a SMALL KEY.  After 
getting the key, go through the door directly on the wall, not in a hallway.

Here you will have to collect a whole bunch of Silver Rupees again.  Walk to 
the edge and turn around, use the Longshot on the panel right above you to get 
the Silver Rupee above your head.  It might make it easier if you use the 
Hover Boots although they are not required.  Jump down to the first pillar and 
across, collecting the three Silver Rupees on the way to the button at the 
end, step on it and the fire will drop.  Head back to the flaming platform and 
get the fifth Silver Rupee.  The door you want to go through is the door on the 
left from the perspective of when you are just entering the room.

Stand away from the blocks in the floor and equip the Ocarina.  Play the Song 
of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and they will disappear.  Equip the 
Iron Boots and drop down the hole to the bottom.  Your goal is to collect all 
the Silver Rupees and it will make it a lot easier if you defeat the enemies 
first.  After doing that you'll have to use a combination of the Longshot, and 
unequipping the Iron Boots in order to swing around and swim around to collect 
them all.  When this is over, swim back up to the top and open the small chest 
to acquire the fourth SMALL KEY.  Leave this room and make a left, jump across 
the pillars over the fire and head through the door on this side.

Here you will have to fight a couple upgraded lizard enemies, with the 
Biggoron's Sword they shouldn't be too hard to defeat.  Stay away from the 
centre to avoid the laser, but stay away from the edge also as the fire walls 
will damage you.  When the two lizards are defeated, bomb the laser pillar and 
a chest appears with a SMALL KEY in it.  Return to the previous room.

Jump across to the platform with the button on it and take out the Ocarina.  
Play the Song of Time (right, A, down, right, A, down) and some blocks will 
appear here.  use the Hover Boots to reach them and climb up.  Grab the sixth 
SMALL KEY and go through the door.  Make a right here and it leads to a chest 
containing ARROWS.  Directly across from the door where you entered is a chest 
with a BOMBCHU in it.  Leave this room and jump back down onto the pillars.  
Enter the room again where you fought the lizard things and keep going, 
through the next door you will find yourself back in the first room.

Take the centre door this time to reach the ultimate locked room of treasure.  
use two Small Keys to go through the locked door on your left and then the one 
after it as well, now you're down to four.  Open the chest to get a PURPLE 
RUPEE here.  Continue through the next locked door and the one after it, open 
the small chest containing a RED RUPEE.  Continue through the next door and 
open the chest to get ARROWS.  Unlock the other door here and you'll find 
yourself at a dead end.  That's okay, return to the entrance of this room and 
equip the Lens of Truth.  Proceed through the door on the left which you 
unlocked and take a look at the ceiling with the Lens of Truth equipped, there 
is a hole up there.  Climb the fence wall and here you will find a chest 
containing a SMALL KEY.  Take this Small Key back to the door you couldn't 
unlock and unlock it.  Inside the giant chest is the ICE ARROW, like the Fire 
Arrow it is a special elemental type of arrow you can shoot using the Fairy 
Bow.  That's about all there is of any worth here, time to leave this place.
______________________________________________________________________________
