~~\/~~~~~~~\/~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\/~~~~~~~\/~~
  || 03.00 ||                      Boss Guide                    || 03.00 ||
__||_______||____________________________________________________||_______||__
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    OHMA [PARASITIC ARMORED ARACHNID] ------------------- 03.01 --+

Prepare yourself for the first boss battle, the battle against Queen Gohma, 
the enemy that the Deku Scrub was talking about.  When the door slams shut, 
equip your Fairy Slingshot and aim it up at the ceiling, once you make eye 
contact the battle against Parasitic Armored Arachnid, Gohma will begin.  At 
the very beginning of the battle, Gohma will pretty much be wandering around 
doing her own this, this is your chance to attack.  Hold the L/Z button to 
target her eye and tap the Fairy Slingshot button to fire a Deku Seed right 
into it, stunning the boss.  While the boss is stunned, run up to her and 
begin to slash her with your sword as many times as you possibly can.  After 
this the boss will begin climbing up the wall and shimmy onto the roof.  After 
a few seconds she'll drop three eggs down directly below where she is on the 
ceiling.  You now have a small amount of time to go and strike those eggs, one 
single hit will kill them however if you are not fast enough they will hatch 
into those little one eyed enemies you fought before.  Wait another few 
seconds and Gohma will drop down again, and the pattern begins anew.  Target 
Gohma's eye and let her get close, while she is up on her back legs and the 
eye is red that is the best time to fire.  It should take no more than three 
or four sessions of stunning the boss and attacking with your sword.
______________________________________________________________________________


       __   __)
      (, ) /
        /(
     ) /  \_
+-- (_/      ING DODONGO [INFERNAL DINOSAUR] ----------------------- 03.02 --+

I cannot begin to explain the simplicity of this boss, perhaps I will explain 
it by basically just telling you how to defeat him.  First of all you should 
acquaint yourself with the little 45 degree slope between the path you are on 
and the lava in the middle, while standing on this slope you are immune to all 
his attacks.  None of them can hit you there, you could go eat dinner and come 
back the boss would sill be running around like an idiot.  As for actually 
fighting the boss here's how it works.  He stops and begins to suck in air for 
his fire breath attack which launches a blast of fire down the path and around 
the corner.  What you have to do is lift a Bomb above your head while the boss 
is breathing in, and then throw it into his mouth.  When the Bomb explodes the 
boss drops down and that is your chance to hit him exactly once.  Use the Jump 
Attack when he falls.  After the boss gets up he will roll down and around the 
corner, dodge this attack easily by standing on that little sloping area like 
I told you.  At the end the boss uncurls itself and the process begins all 
over again.  It should take only four hits to kill the boss with normal 
attacks, and as little as three with the Jump Attack.  He will never be able 
to use his fire breath as long as you always throw a Bomb into his mouth.  In 
fact the only real danger of damage in this fight is from you, holding a Bomb 
for too long.  When the boss is defeated he will roll himself into the lava in 
the middle of the map and then just die, solidifying it in the process.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    IANT OCTOROK [MINIBOSS] ----------------------------- 03.03 --+

Despite the use of the word "mini" this boss really isn't so mini at all, in 
fact it might just be me but I actually found him to be quite difficult.  The 
weapon of choice for this battle is of course going to be the Boomerang so 
make sure it is equipped.  What happens here is the Giant Octorok circles 
around the middle and chases after you.  If he hits you, you're going to lose 
half a heart piece and if the spikes along the middle thing hit you, you're 
going to lose a quarter heart piece.  When you hit the miniboss with your 
Boomerang, he is stunned for a moment but his only weakspot it on the back so 
you have to run around the circle and try to get his back before he starts up 
again.  For this reason you have to stun him from a fairly decent distance in 
order to have time to get around and stun him again while he is facing the 
other direction.  I find this doesn't work too well so here are two other ways 
of hitting him that I used.  When he comes at you throw the Boomerang to stun 
and then walk backward, but not around the circle.  If you're far enough back 
he may choose to change directions and then you have just enough time to run 
up and hit him with the Boomerang before he takes off.  Also this may not 
sound too appealing, but I find it works better than anything else.  If you 
happen to get hit by the boss, that is your very best chance to strike back.  
He will knock you down and then always turn around.  Get up quickly and throw 
the Boomerang while he is facing the other direction.  The green spot will 
open up and you can slash it with your sword.  The one tip I can give you to 
help out the most is to spend almost the entire battle locked onto the boss, 
don't let him out of your site, throwing the Boomerang without a lock on isn't 
nearly accurate enough and you don't always have time to lock on when he's 
coming at you so stay locked on all the time.  He takes about three hits.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      ARINADE [BIO-ELECTRIC ANEMONE] ---------------------- 03.04 --+

There's all sorts of crazy stuff going on here.  You are battling Barinade, 
the large thing in the middle of the room going crazy with electricity.  The 
first thing to note is that if you're low on health, all of the pots around 
the room each contain a Recovery Heart which should be enough to get you back 
to full from being almost dead.  Secondly make sure you keep running during 
all this because the boss is going to be firing blasts of electricity in your 
direction the whole time.  It is very important to note the following: you can 
move while throwing the Boomerang.  This is important because you're going to 
have to throw the Boomerang, but you're also going to have to be dodging as 
well.  Use the L/Z button to lock onto the three things that attach the boss 
to the ceiling and hit all three of them individually with the Boomerang.  At 
this point he creates a bunch of enemies and attaches to them via electricity.  
They spin around and around, but fortunately they don't do too much damage 
when they hit you.  Aim for the main body part and throw your Boomerang to 
stun it, if it turns blue then you know it's stunned.  Slash it as much as 
possible with your sword until you can't hurt it anymore.  Now what it does is 
take all the enemies close to it and begin spinning around rapidly.  While it 
is spinning rapidly it is very difficult to hit the boss with the Boomerang 
(though it can be done.)  It's easier if you wait.  Eventually the boss will 
disconnect its electricity link to the enemies and then you can hit the boss 
core itself with the Boomerang easier.  When you do hit the boss you still 
can't strike with your sword, instead it gives you the opportunity to target 
the enemies individually and throw you Boomerang at them, it will kill them in 
one hit.  After all those little enemies are defeated then you can stun the 
boss and keep attacking with your sword.  The enemies never respawn so you are 
free to keep stunning and attacking until the boss eventually dies.
______________________________________________________________________________


        _____
       (, /   )
        _/__ /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ HANTOM GANON [EVIL SPIRIT FROM BEYOND] --------------------- 03.05 --+

Upon arriving at the top nothing really happens, it's not until you turn 
around and try to leave that the boss appears, the Evil Spirit from Beyond, 
Phantom Ganon!  He begins the battle by jumping into one of the six paintings.  
Now after this he will come out of a different painting, but it isn't going to 
be that simple, he actually comes back out of two paintings.  One of these two 
enemies will get to the edge of the painting and suddenly turn around, this is 
the fake.  The other will jump out of the painting through a purple portal and 
shoot a huge electricity blast at the ground which goes in all directions, 
being at the edge of the field is still the safest place for this.  What you 
have to do is randomly guess which one is right, when he reaches the edge if a 
portal appears it's the right one.  You should have been aiming your Fairy Bow 
the whole time, when the portal appears fire into the middle of the portal and 
Phantom Ganon will be hit, turning around and not attacking.  If you guessed 
wrong then be ready to move out of the way and avoid the attack which deals 
two hearts worth of damage each hit.  If you guess wrong, the instant you see 
him turn around the is time to spin around 180 degrees and fire an arrow, you 
have to be extremely quick with this one and it's difficult, but you can hit 
the real Phantom Ganon before he attacks if you're lucky.  Aiming is probably 
a luxury you won't be afforded.  After three hits the second phase starts.

Phantom Ganon's horse has been destroyed it seems and now it's time to fight 
him as he simply floats around on his own.  This boss only has one attack and 
it’s only half as strong as the previous one, but it's more frequent.  What he 
does is float around and fire this energy blast at you.  The energy blast can 
be deflected with the Master Sword back at him for damage.  Unfortunately the 
boss can deflect it back at you, and then you back at him and then him back at 
you.  If you timing sucks you'll be in trouble but fortunately there seems to 
be a way of getting around it.  First of all here's what I find most 
important, maybe it's just me but it seems then when you are moving around, 
Phantom Ganon doesn't deflect it back nearly as often, he kind of just lets it 
hit him.  This isn't true all the time but the point is, after deflecting it 
back, don't give him a stationary target and he will probably be less likely 
to hit it back.  Try to be moving as well when you strike it in the first 
place.  After Phantom Ganon is hit by the blast run up to him and begin 
slashing with your sword as much as possible.  Repeat this pattern until dead.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ________) 
      (, /       
        /___,    
     ) /         
+-- (_/   LARE DANCER [MINIBOSS] ----------------------------------- 03.06 --+

Time for a miniboss battle, the Flare Dancer can be a challenging foe but like 
most, if you know what you're doing then the battle becomes significantly 
easier.  The Flare Dancer pops out of the middle on fire and begins to shoot 
little bits of flame at you, dodging these should not be too difficult.  When 
the Flare Dancer actually jumps out of the middle that is your chance.  Make 
sure you have the Hookshot equipped and target the Flare Dancing using the L/Z 
button.  Hit the Hookshot button and you should be able to pull the actual, 
hit-able section of the boss out.  Now it is entirely harmless and you are 
free to begin slashing away.  What I like to do is hit the boss as much as 
possible before it gets away, then it's going to run around in a circle and 
come back to you.  When it sees you it will turn around and run again, often 
before you even have a chance to strike so do this.  As it runs around charge 
up the maximum power Spin Attack and move toward the boss, the instant it 
freaks out to turn around, let loose for good damage.  Do this about three or 
four times and the miniboss will quickly fall victim to your blade.
______________________________________________________________________________


  __    __) 
 (, )  /    
    | /     
    |/      
+-- | OLVAGIA [SUBTERRANEAN LAVA DRAGON] --------------------------- 03.07 --+

I sincerely doubt you will have too much trouble with this boss at all, but of 
course it's still important to know how to fight him, because in the end 
knowing how to fight is what makes it so easy.  Knowledge is power.  Forget 
absolutely every weapon and item for this battle with the one exception being 
the Megaton Hammer, it's your only weapon.  There are nine holes in the field 
here and the boss will randomly pop out of one of them.  You can tell which 
one he is going to pop out of by seeing where the flames after gathering.  
When the boss pops out, keep your distance a bit, he will sweep around the 
proximity with some kind of flaming dragon hair.  After that you have a window 
to run up and slam him with the Megaton Hammer.  Doing so will make him swing 
his flaming hair one more time and then drop, allowing you to go in with the 
hammer for a second hit.  After doing this he will pop out of the next hole, 
but you cannot attack.  He will use one of two attacks, either he will fly 
around the field breathing fire at you or he will drop rocks from above.  The 
fire one can be dodged simply by running and not stopping, being careful never 
to stay in his line of vision.  The rock one is even easier, they make large 
shadows before they fall so stay away from the shadows.  After this the 
pattern repeats with one subtle difference.  The dragon may pretend to pop up 
in one place and then appear somewhere else, keep this in mind, but it really 
never makes much difference.  About five or six successful runs for the win.
______________________________________________________________________________


          __  
      (__/  ) 
        /     
     ) /      
+-- (_/ HADOW LINK [MINIBOSS] -------------------------------------- 03.08 --+

This miniboss is extremely difficult but very beatable.  He might end up 
hitting you a fair amount, but that shouldn't stop you from doing the same, 
the battle requires patience and good dodging/blocking ability.  First of all 
Shadow Link will mimic every attack that you do, so if you target him with L/Z 
and then keep using the overhead swing, you'll clash swords until the end of 
time.  What you really want to do is avoid using L/Z targeting, that way he 
won't be able to line up his deflection swings as easily.  Running up to 
Shadow Link and pounding the B button actually works surprisingly well, you'll 
probably find you get hit a fair number of times doing this but you should hit 
him as well just as often if not more than you get hit yourself.  If you don't 
like the Kamikaze method there are always other ways.  You can try to come up 
from the side with a horizontal slash or a vertical slash on an angle which he 
cannot deflect from.  You can try blocking his attacks and quickly countering 
with one of your own.  I find the easiest time to hit him without actually 
being hit yourself, is if you can land an overhead swing just a split second 
after he does one, this is difficult but you won't be hit.  One thing to note 
is to avoid using the stab, he'll jump on your sword and hit you every time.
______________________________________________________________________________


      __     __)
     (, /|  /|
       / | / |
+-- (_/  |/  |_ ORPHA [GIANT AQUATIC AMOEBA] ----------------------- 03.09 --+

When you enter this room nothing really happens, only after dropping into the 
water and climbing one of the ladders to one of the four central pillars does 
the boss, Morpha, actually reveal itself.  The boss is just some weird little 
red amoeba but it has some serious power.  Basically the boss only has one 
attack, it creates weird tentacle like water things that come up and try to 
hit you.  If one of them lands on you, you take about one heart worth of 
damage, but if one of them grabs hold of you, it can deal upwards of three 
hearts, this attack is very powerful!  Fortunately like I said this is the 
only attack and it's extremely easy to dodge, all you have to do is stay in 
motion.  Don't attempt to fight this boss on the central pillars, jump to the 
outside and move around, it's much, much easier.  After one of the tentacles 
drops down, Morpha will begin to move back and forth through the tentacle.  
Take aim near the end of the tentacle with your Longshot and fire at Morpha to 
suck it out of the water and leave it open to attack.  The reason I say to 
fire at the end of the tentacle is because it's easier to aim and hit Morpha 
when it is changing direction.  After getting it out of the water you can 
attack with your sword.  I recommend using the Spin Attack, the one where you 
press B after spinning the analog stick.  Not only is it powerful but you have 
a good chance of hitting Morpha more than once if you keep doing it, perhaps 
even three times.  After you have hit the boss enough, more water tentacles 
pop up that the boss doesn't appear in, so just dodge them and keep an eye out 
for the real ones that Morpha actually uses for your chance to attack.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /    /
      _/___ /_
+-- (_/___ /   EADHAND [MINIBOSS] ---------------------------------- 03.10 --+

There are four hand things coming up in the corners or this room, when you get 
close to the hands they grab at you and hold you in place.  When this happens 
you can wiggle the analog and tab the A button to get out of the grip.  It 
also has another side effect, it makes the boss appear.  The boss is a big 
weird blob man.  To defeat him here's all you have to do.  Target him with the 
L/Z button and get close.  When he lowers his head down press the A button to 
do a Jump Attack for a good damage.  At that point he'll go underground again 
and you have to let yourself be grabbed by one of the hands in order to lure 
him out.  Repeat this process a few times and the boss will be defeated.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______
      (, /    )
        /---(
     ) / ____)
+-- (_/ (      ONGO BONGO [PHANTOM SHADOW BEAST] ------------------- 03.11 --+

The amazing thing about this boss is that its one of the hardest, and one of 
the easiest bosses at the same time, depending entirely on how familiar you 
are with the fight.  I've had times when I went through two full complements 
of hearts thinking I couldn't possibly win, and other times defeating the boss 
without being hit.  If ever there was a boss where a super offensive approach 
is best, this is certainly it.  The longer it takes you to fight this boss, 
the less chance you have.  We'll begin with his attack, there are two hands 
which bounce around and shake the ground so it is difficult for you to stand 
still without being bounced around.  They can become a fist and punch you, or 
grab you, or they can hover overhead and stomp on you.  They can knock you out 
of the arena onto the slime which damages you.  Each one of this attacks deals 
at least two hearts worth of damage, possibly more.  Dodging the attacks 
basically means standing back, and always keeping both hands in your sight.

When it comes to actually attacking the boss it's a whole different story.  
Equip the Fairy Bow with the normal arrows, not the Fire Arrows because it 
will be very important to conserve magic power.  Choose whichever is better 
for you, L/Z targeting or aiming manually, your goal is to take aim and fire 
at the hands.  When you hit one hand it will turn blue and drop an item, 
either an arrow or a magic bottle, both of which you will be needing for this 
battle.  Your goal is to hit one hand, then the other hand quickly before the 
first one can shake itself back to normal.  Once both hands are hit then 
position yourself in the middle of the map, between them and facing them, and 
equip the Lens of Truth.  Only using this will you actually be able to see the 
boss.  Take aim with your Fairy Bow and fire at his eye.  When you do this the 
boss drops and you are free to go in with the Biggoron's Sword swinging like 
crazy.  The thing about this boss is that using the Biggoron's Sword, you only 
have to do this twice, the boss will die on the second set of sword swings, so 
this is why I say offensive is so good.  The attacks are extremely hard to 
dodge and powerful, but if you're fast, you can kill him very quickly.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____
        (, /
          /
      ___/__
+-- (__ /    RON KNUCKLE [MINIBOSS] -------------------------------- 03.12 --+

The Iron Knuckle is an extremely difficult enemy, while he is slow he is very 
very powerful.  His attacks can deal up to four hearts worth of damage.  He 
has two types of attacks, a double horizontal slash and a powerful over head 
slash.  The basic tactic for both of them is the same, avoid it and use the 
jump attack.  After the jump attack Iron Knuckle has a habit of quickly using 
the overhead slash, since you don't have time to move backward, try dodging it 
by moving either left or right.  It takes a hell of a lot of hits to defeat 
this guy, even using the jump attack it's at least seven or eight.
______________________________________________________________________________


       ______)
      (, /
        /
     ) /
+-- (_/ WINROVA [SORCERESS SISTERS] -------------------------------- 03.13 --+

The true bosses are the Sorceress Sisters Twinrova.  To begin the fight 
simply climb up onto the centre platform and walk to the middle.  They fly 
around on their broomsticks, one of fire element, one of ice element.  Each of 
them only have one attack, a powerful ice beam or a powerful fire beam.  
Fortunately both attacks can be deflected using your Mirror Shield, in fact 
you will be counting on that.  In order to defeat the bosses what you have to 
do is deflect the beam of one boss and aim it at another boss.  The beam is 
constant so you'll be able to swing it around for a couple seconds in hope of 
hitting the other boss.  Use L/Z targeting to make sure you are looking 
straight at the boss firing the attack, it's harder to deflect on an angle.  
Each of them must take two or three hits before the second phase begins.

Both bosses meld together and form on single boss now.  This one isn't too 
much more difficult than the last one.  How it works is the boss fires either 
fire or ice projectiles at you, and once again you have to block them with 
your shield.  The difference is that this time they aren't reflected, but 
rather absorbed.  You need to absorb three of the same elements in a row and 
then that will fire off a blast at the boss and knock her down.  For example 
if she fires ice, then ice, then ice, block all three of them and you'll fire 
an ice blast.  If she fires ice, fire, ice, ice, block the first ice, but 
dodge the fire attack and block the next two ice attacks.  You can also absorb 
three fire attacks in a row, the point is that they must be the same.  After 
knocking her out of the sky, jump to the platform and begin swinging your 
sword and using jump attacks.  With the Biggoron's Sword you should only have 
to knock her out two times before she dies.  Here's a tip for the best way to 
win the fight, hold down the L/Z targeting button and the R button.  When she 
launches an attack, if it's the one you want then block it, if not don't let 
go of the buttons, just hold left or right and tap A to jump to the side.
Defeating the boss nets you a HEART CONTAINER after the scene, then as usual 
you will be brought to see the sages and be given the SPIRIT MEDALLION.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ANONDORF -------------------------------------------- 03.14 --+

The battle has begun, it's quite similar to one you've already fought before 
with Phantom Ganon.  What you need to do is jump from the middle platform to 
one of the corners and turn around.  Equip the Biggoron’s Sword and whip it 
out.  Ganondorf will fire an energy blast at you, deflect it with your sword 
and he'll deflect it back.  Keep doing this over and over again until 
eventually it hits him.  At this point you have a very short window of time to 
bring up a Light Arrow and fire it at him.  When he goes down either roll 
across to the middle platform with the Hover Boots equipped, or Longshot to 
Ganondorf himself and slash with the Biggoron's Sword.  Now that you have the 
idea, it's just a matter of repeating this pattern.  What I like to do is hit 
him with the arrow then quickly roll to the centre platform, let loose a Jump 
Attack and then swing like mad.  Doing this it will only take a mere three 
knock downs to actually take him out, but the battle isn't over yet.
______________________________________________________________________________


         _____)
       /
      /
     /     / )
+-- (____ /    ANON ------------------------------------------------ 03.15 --+

The first thing of course that you will notice when this battle begins is that 
you do not have your Master Sword for the fight, there are two weapons of 
choice here, obviously the Biggoron's Sword takes top place but given that you 
won't necessarily have it, the Megaton Hammer comes second.  If you were 
hoping for a big and challenging boss battle you likely won't find it here, 
but still you're in for an impressive fight against an enormous enemy, which 
still works out quite well.  The tactic to use here is relatively simple, with 
whatever weapon you are using, roll under Ganon's legs and slash like crazy at 
his tail.  Roll out of the way to avoid his sword swings and repeat this 
pattern over and over again.  Ganon takes quite a few hits to go down.  The 
alternative to this is targeting his face with the L/Z button and firing a 
Light Arrow to stun him, then simply running around behind and doing the same 
thing.  The first way is faster and consumes less magic, although the second 
way will allow you to have time to use a Jump Attack.  At about the half way 
point Ganon will fall and Zelda will allow you to grab the Master Sword again.  
The second half of the battle is exactly the same.  Ganon might move a little 
faster but it's nothing much different.  Keep attacking like crazy with the 
Biggoron's Sword, but near the end of the battle you're going to have to 
switch to the Master Sword.  The battle can only be won with a swing of the 
Master Sword, no other weapon will suffice.  Strike the final blow and...
______________________________________________________________________________

(------------------------)-(-------------------------------------------+-----+
 ) Piece of Heart [001]  |o| Head to the Hyrule Market at night and    |  _  |
 )                       |o| locate the fat lady's dog, bring it to    | |_| |
 )                       |o| her in the Back Alley to acquire this.    |     |
(------------------------)-(-------------------------------------------+-----+
 ) Piece of Heart [002]  |o| Head for the southwest corner of Lon Lon  |  _  |
 )                       |o| Ranch and in the tower, move boxes and    | |_| |
 )                       |o| climb into the crawlspace to find this.   |     |
(------------------------)-(-------------------------------------------+-----+
 ) Piece of Heart [003]  |o| In The Lost Woods stand on the tree       |  _  |
 )                       |o| stump and play your Ocarina along         | |_| |
 )                       |o| with the two little things that appear.   |     |
(------------------------)-(-------------------------------------------+-----+
